BASE_CATEGORIES = [s[0] for s in SUBJECTS]
GROUP_OF = {s[0]: s[3] for s in SUBJECTS}
# subjects whose title words are reliable on their own (technical books name their topic);
# for everything else the book's content has the final say once it is added
TRUSTED_TITLE_SUBJECTS = {s[0] for s in SUBJECTS if s[3] in (SEC, DATA, CODE, COMP, SCI)}
CATEGORY_FILE = LIBRARY_DIR / "categories.json"   # your own choices and new subjects, kept even if a book is re-added


def _category_choices() -> dict:
    try:
        data = json.loads(CATEGORY_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}


def all_categories() -> list[str]:
    """The built-in subjects plus any you made yourself (kept just before Other)."""
    custom = [c for c in _category_choices().get("_custom", []) if c not in BASE_CATEGORIES]
    return BASE_CATEGORIES[:-1] + custom + ["Other"]


def category_groups() -> list[dict]:
    """Subjects in their groups, for the menus: [{"group": ..., "subjects": [...]}]."""
    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for name in all_categories():
        groups.setdefault(GROUP_OF.get(name, "Your subjects"), []).append(name)
    order = list(dict.fromkeys([s[3] for s in SUBJECTS[:-1]] + ["Your subjects", "Other"]))
    return [{"group": g, "subjects": groups[g]} for g in order if g in groups]


def category_rank(name: str) -> int:
    cats = all_categories()
    return cats.index(name) if name in cats else len(cats)


def guess_category(title: str, file: str = "") -> str:
    """Guess from the title and file name alone (quick, used before a book is added)."""
    text = re.sub(r"(?<=[a-z])(?=[A-Z])", " ", f"{title} {file}")   # JavaCompleteReference -> Java Complete Reference
    text = text.lower().replace("_", " ").replace("-", " ")
    for cat, _desc, pattern, _group in SUBJECTS:
        if re.search(pattern, text):
            return cat
    return "Other"


_SUBJECT_VECTORS: np.ndarray | None = None


def guess_category_from_content(matrix: np.ndarray) -> str | None:
    """Compare the book's overall meaning (the average of its passage vectors) with each
    subject's description and return the closest subject."""
    global _SUBJECT_VECTORS
    if matrix is None or not len(matrix):
        return None
    try:
        if _SUBJECT_VECTORS is None:
            descs = [f"{name}: {desc}" for name, desc, _p, _g in SUBJECTS[:-1]]
            _SUBJECT_VECTORS = llm.embed(descs)
        centre = matrix.mean(axis=0)
        centre = centre / (np.linalg.norm(centre) or 1.0)
        sims = _SUBJECT_VECTORS @ centre
        return SUBJECTS[int(np.argmax(sims))][0]
    except Exception:
        return None


def set_category(file: str, category: str) -> None:
    category = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", (category or "")).strip()[:40]
    if not category:
        raise ValueError("The subject name is empty")
    choices = _category_choices()
    if category not in BASE_CATEGORIES:
        custom = choices.get("_custom", [])
        if category not in custom:
            choices["_custom"] = custom + [category]      # a new subject of your own
    choices[file] = category
    write_json_atomic(CATEGORY_FILE, choices)
    INDEX.loaded = False          # rebuild the book-to-subject map on the next search


def category_of(file: str, title: str, auto: str | None = None) -> str:
    """Your choice first, then the guess made from the book's content, then the title guess."""
    choice = _category_choices().get(file)
    if choice:
        return choice
    if auto:
        return auto
    return guess_category(title, file)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- adding books
QUEUE: list[str] = []      # file names waiting to be added, in order


def _set(**kw):
    PROGRESS.update(kw)


def _say(msg: str):
    PROGRESS["messages"] = (PROGRESS["messages"] + [msg])[-50:]


def _needs_adding(manifest: dict) -> list[Path]:
    todo = []
    for pdf in sorted(source_files(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
        st = pdf.stat()
        old = manifest.get(book_id_for(pdf))
        if old and old["size"] == st.st_size and abs(old["mtime"] - st.st_mtime) < 2:
            continue
        todo.append(pdf)
    return todo


def _forget_deleted(manifest: dict) -> None:
    current = {book_id_for(p) for p in source_files()}
    for bid in [b for b in manifest if b not in current]:
        _say(f"Removed from index: {manifest[bid]['title']}")
        for ext in (".jsonl", ".npy"):
            (LIBRARY_DIR / f"{bid}{ext}").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        manifest.pop(bid)
    write_json_atomic(MANIFEST, manifest)


def request_add(files: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Queue books to add: the given file names, or every new/changed book if None.
    Returns how many were queued. One worker adds them one after another."""
    manifest = load_manifest()
    wanted = {p.name for p in _needs_adding(manifest)} if files is None else set(files)
    names = {p.name for p in source_files()}
    added = 0
    for name in sorted(wanted):
        if name in names and name not in QUEUE and name != PROGRESS.get("book"):
            QUEUE.append(name)
            added += 1
    if added and _lock.acquire(blocking=False):
        threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True).start()
    return added


def scan_and_add() -> None:
    """'Add all new': queue every new or changed book (kept for the Add all button)."""
    if request_add(None) == 0 and not PROGRESS.get("running"):
        _say("Nothing new to add. Put books in the books folder first.")


def _worker() -> None:
    try:
        _set(running=True, done_books=0, total_books=len(QUEUE), percent=0, step="Starting", messages=[])
        manifest = load_manifest()
        _forget_deleted(manifest)
        n = 0
        while QUEUE:
            name = QUEUE.pop(0)
            pdf = BOOKS_DIR / name
            _set(book=name, step="Reading pages", percent=0, total_books=n + 1 + len(QUEUE))
            if pdf.exists():
                try:
                    _add_one(book_id_for(pdf), pdf, manifest)
                    INDEX.loaded = False           # new book is searchable straight away
                except Exception as e:  # one bad book must not stop the others
                    _say(f"FAILED {name}: {e}")
            n += 1
            _set(done_books=n)
        INDEX.reload()
        _set(step="Finished", percent=100, book="")
    finally:
        _set(running=False)
        _lock.release()
        if QUEUE and _lock.acquire(blocking=False):   # something was queued at the last moment
            threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True).start()


def _auto_subject(pdf: Path, title: str, matrix: np.ndarray) -> str:
    """Title words win when they are clear (they are reliable for textbooks); otherwise the
    book's content decides - this is how a novel or a scripture with a vague title is sorted."""
    by_title = guess_category(title, pdf.name)
    if by_title in TRUSTED_TITLE_SUBJECTS:
        return by_title                       # "Head First Java" really is about Java
    by_content = guess_category_from_content(matrix)
    if by_content:
        return by_content                     # "...Philosopher's Stone" reads like fantasy, not philosophy
    if by_title != "Other":
        return by_title
    return "My Notes" if pdf.suffix.lower() in (".txt", ".md", ".docx", ".pptx") else "Other"


def _add_one(bid: str, pdf: Path, manifest: dict) -> None:
    t0 = time.time()
    pages, meta_title = extract_pages(pdf)
    empty = sum(1 for _, t in pages if len(t) < 20)
    passages = make_passages(bid, pages)
    if not passages:
        raise ValueError("no text found - it may be a scanned book (pictures of pages)")
    _set(step=f"Building search index ({len(passages)} passages)")
    vectors = []
    batch = 16
    for i in range(0, len(passages), batch):
        vectors.append(llm.embed([p["text"] for p in passages[i:i + batch]]))
        _set(percent=int(100 * min(i + batch, len(passages)) / len(passages)))
    matrix = np.vstack(vectors).astype(np.float32)
    np.save(LIBRARY_DIR / f"{bid}.npy", matrix)
    with open(LIBRARY_DIR / f"{bid}.jsonl", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for p in passages:
            f.write(json.dumps(p, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    st = pdf.stat()
    manifest[bid] = {
        "file": pdf.name, "title": pretty_title(pdf, meta_title),
        "pages": len(pages), "empty_pages": empty, "passages": len(passages),
        "size": st.st_size, "mtime": st.st_mtime,
        "added": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
        "seconds": round(time.time() - t0),
        "category_auto": _auto_subject(pdf, pretty_title(pdf, meta_title), matrix),
    }
    write_json_atomic(MANIFEST, manifest)
    warn = f" ({empty} pages had no text - pictures or scans)" if empty > 5 else ""
    _say(f"Added {manifest[bid]['title']}: {len(pages)} pages, {len(passages)} passages "
         f"in {time.time() - t0:.0f} s{warn}")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- searching
_STOP = set("""a an and are as at be been but by can could did do does for from had has have how i if in into
is it its may more most must no not of on or our should so such than that the their them then there these
they this those to was we were what when where which while who why will with would you your about also""".split())


# everyday words that may not appear in your books but are not typos
_COMMON = set("""explain explained explaining example examples difference differences between please tell show
give make write simple simply simpler meaning means mean define definition describe understand understanding
help helping question questions answer answers about would could should whats hows thing things really
using used uses work works working different same similar compare comparison summary summarise summarize
teach learn learning step steps easy hard quick quickly detail details detailed""".split())


def _terms(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return [w for w in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+(?:[-'][a-z0-9]+)*", text.lower()) if len(w) > 1 and w not in _STOP]


class Index:
    """All passages of all books, held in memory for fast searching.

    Search uses TWO methods and combines them:
      - meaning search (vectors): finds passages that say the same thing in other words
      - keyword search (BM25): finds passages that use the exact terms, names and formulas
    Combining them finds the right pages far more often than either alone."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.matrix = np.zeros((0, 384), dtype=np.float32)
        self.passages: list[dict] = []
        self.titles: dict[str, str] = {}
        self.postings: dict[str, tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = {}
        self.doc_len = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float32)
        self.book_cat: dict[str, str] = {}
        self.cat_of_row = np.array([], dtype=object)
        self.last_categories: list[str] = []
        self.last_corrections: dict[str, str] = {}
        self.loaded = False

    def _build_keywords(self) -> None:
        from collections import Counter, defaultdict
        raw: dict[str, list[tuple[int, int]]] = defaultdict(list)
        lengths = []
        for i, p in enumerate(self.passages):
            words = _terms(p["text"])
            lengths.append(len(words))
            for w, n in Counter(words).items():
                raw[w].append((i, n))
        self.doc_len = np.asarray(lengths, dtype=np.float32)
        self.postings = {w: (np.fromiter((d for d, _ in v), np.int32, len(v)),
                             np.fromiter((n for _, n in v), np.float32, len(v))) for w, v in raw.items()}

    def _bm25(self, question: str) -> np.ndarray:
        scores = np.zeros(len(self.passages), dtype=np.float32)
        if not len(scores):
            return scores
        n, avg = len(scores), float(self.doc_len.mean() or 1.0)
        k1, b = 1.4, 0.75
        for w in set(_terms(question)):
            if w not in self.postings:
                continue
            docs, tf = self.postings[w]
            idf = np.log(1 + (n - len(docs) + 0.5) / (len(docs) + 0.5))
            norm = tf + k1 * (1 - b + b * self.doc_len[docs] / avg)
            scores[docs] += idf * tf * (k1 + 1) / norm
        return scores

    def reload(self) -> None:
        manifest = load_manifest()
        mats, passages = [], []
        for bid, info in manifest.items():
            npy, jsonl = LIBRARY_DIR / f"{bid}.npy", LIBRARY_DIR / f"{bid}.jsonl"
            if not (npy.exists() and jsonl.exists()):
                continue
            m = np.load(npy)
            with open(jsonl, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                rows = [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]
            if len(rows) != len(m):
                continue  # damaged pair of files; skip rather than give wrong pages
            mats.append(m)
            passages.extend(rows)
        self.matrix = np.vstack(mats) if mats else np.zeros((0, 384), dtype=np.float32)
        self.passages = passages
        self.titles = {bid: info["title"] for bid, info in manifest.items()}
        self.book_cat = {bid: category_of(info["file"], info["title"], info.get("category_auto"))
                         for bid, info in manifest.items()}
        self.cat_of_row = np.array([self.book_cat.get(p["book_id"], "Other") for p in passages])
        self._build_keywords()
        self.loaded = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ spelling
    _LETTERS = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"

    def _edits1(self, w: str) -> set[str]:
        splits = [(w[:i], w[i:]) for i in range(len(w) + 1)]
        return ({a + b[1:] for a, b in splits if b}                                   # missing letter
                | {a + b[1] + b[0] + b[2:] for a, b in splits if len(b) > 1}          # swapped letters
                | {a + c + b[1:] for a, b in splits if b for c in self._LETTERS}      # wrong letter
                | {a + c + b for a, b in splits for c in self._LETTERS})              # extra letter

    def _best_spelling(self, w: str) -> str | None:
        """Closest word that actually appears in your books (1 or 2 typing mistakes away),
        preferring words that appear in many passages."""
        df = lambda x: len(self.postings[x][0]) if x in self.postings else 0
        near = [x for x in self._edits1(w) if df(x) >= 2]
        if not near and len(w) >= 6:
            near = [y for x in self._edits1(w) for y in self._edits1(x) if df(y) >= 3]
        return max(near, key=df) if near else None

    def correct(self, question: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str]]:
        """Fix likely spelling mistakes in a question, for searching only. Words that appear
        in your books, short words, numbers and common English words are left alone."""
        fixes: dict[str, str] = {}
        if not self.postings:
            return question, fixes

        def fix(m: re.Match) -> str:
            word = m.group(0)
            w = word.lower()
            if len(w) < 4 or w in self.postings or w in _STOP or not w.isalpha() or w in _COMMON:
                return word
            if w not in fixes:
                best = self._best_spelling(w)
                if not best:
                    return word
                fixes[w] = best
            return fixes[w]

        fixed = re.sub(r"[A-Za-z]+", fix, question)
        return fixed, fixes

    def pick_categories(self, meaning: np.ndarray, keyword: np.ndarray) -> list[str]:
        """Decide which subject(s) the question belongs to: each category is scored by how
        well its best few passages match, and close runners-up are kept too."""
        cats = sorted(set(self.cat_of_row.tolist()))
        if len(cats) <= 1:
            return cats
        kw = keyword / (keyword.max() or 1.0)
        scores = {}
        for c in cats:
            mask = self.cat_of_row == c
            best = np.sort(meaning[mask] + 0.15 * kw[mask])[-3:]
            scores[c] = float(best.mean())
        ranked = sorted(scores, key=scores.get, reverse=True)
        top = scores[ranked[0]]
        return [c for c in ranked[:3] if scores[c] >= top - 0.03]

    def search(self, question: str, k: int, category: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
        if not self.loaded:
            self.reload()
        if not self.passages:
            return []
        question, self.last_corrections = self.correct(question)    # "inheritence" -> "inheritance"
        q = llm.embed([question], is_query=True)[0]
        meaning = self.matrix @ q
        keyword = self._bm25(question)
        # 1. choose the subject area: the one you picked, or the best match automatically
        if category and category != "auto" and category in set(self.cat_of_row.tolist()):
            chosen = [category]
        else:
            chosen = self.pick_categories(meaning, keyword)
        self.last_categories = chosen
        allowed = np.isin(self.cat_of_row, chosen) if chosen else np.ones(len(meaning), bool)
        meaning = np.where(allowed, meaning, -1.0)
        keyword = np.where(allowed, keyword, 0.0)
        pool = max(30, k * 6)
        by_meaning = np.argsort(-meaning)[:pool]
        by_keyword = np.argsort(-keyword)[:pool] if keyword.max() > 0 else np.array([], dtype=int)
        # reciprocal rank fusion: a passage ranked high by either method rises to the top
        fused: dict[int, float] = {}
        for rank, i in enumerate(by_meaning):
            fused[int(i)] = fused.get(int(i), 0.0) + 1.0 / (60 + rank)
        for rank, i in enumerate(by_keyword):
            fused[int(i)] = fused.get(int(i), 0.0) + 1.0 / (60 + rank)
        top_kw = float(keyword.max()) or 1.0
        hits: list[dict] = []
        for i in sorted(fused, key=fused.get, reverse=True):
            if not allowed[i]:
                continue
            p = self.passages[i]
            # skip near-duplicates: overlapping passages from the same pages of the same book
            if any(h["book_id"] == p["book_id"] and p["page"] <= h["page_end"] and h["page"] <= p["page_end"]
                   for h in hits):
                continue
            hit = dict(p)
            hit["score"] = round(float(meaning[i]), 3)            # meaning match, 0 to 1
            hit["keyword"] = round(float(keyword[i]) / top_kw, 3)  # keyword match, 0 to 1
            hit["title"] = self.titles.get(p["book_id"], p["book_id"])
            hit["category"] = self.book_cat.get(p["book_id"], "Other")
            hits.append(hit)
            if len(hits) >= k:
                break
        return hits


INDEX = Index()


_PAGE_CACHE: dict[tuple, int | None] = {}


def _page_count(path: Path) -> int | None:
    """Quick page count without reading the text (cached), used for time estimates."""
    st = path.stat()
    key = (path.name, st.st_size, int(st.st_mtime))
    if key not in _PAGE_CACHE:
        n = None
        try:
            kind = path.suffix.lower()
            if kind in (".pdf", ".epub"):
                import pymupdf
                with pymupdf.open(path) as doc:
                    n = doc.page_count
            elif kind == ".pptx":
                import zipfile
                with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
                    n = sum(1 for x in z.namelist() if re.fullmatch(r"ppt/slides/slide\d+\.xml", x))
            else:                                   # .docx/.txt/.md: about 400 words per page
                n = max(1, int(st.st_size / (25_000 if kind == ".docx" else 2_400)))
        except Exception:
            n = None
        _PAGE_CACHE[key] = n
    return _PAGE_CACHE[key]


def seconds_per_page(manifest: dict) -> float:
    """Learned from the books already added (falls back to about 1.1 s per page)."""
    timed = [(b["seconds"], b["pages"]) for b in manifest.values() if b.get("seconds") and b.get("pages")]
    if not timed:
        return 1.1
    return max(0.2, sum(s for s, _ in timed) / max(1, sum(p for _, p in timed)))


def list_books() -> list[dict]:
    manifest = load_manifest()
    rate = seconds_per_page(manifest)
    indexed_files = {info["file"] for info in manifest.values()}
    books = [dict(info, book_id=bid, status="ready") for bid, info in manifest.items()]
    for pdf in sorted(source_files()):
        if pdf.name not in indexed_files:
            books.append({"book_id": book_id_for(pdf), "file": pdf.name,
                          "title": pretty_title(pdf, None), "status": "new"})
    for b in books:
        b["category"] = category_of(b["file"], b["title"], b.get("category_auto"))
        path = BOOKS_DIR / b["file"]
        if path.exists():
            b["size_mb"] = round(path.stat().st_size / 1_048_576, 1)
            if b["status"] == "new":
                pages = _page_count(path)
                if pages:
                    b["pages"] = pages
                    b["estimate_seconds"] = int(pages * rate)
        if b["file"] == PROGRESS.get("book") and PROGRESS.get("running"):
            b["status"] = "adding"
        elif b["file"] in QUEUE:
            b["status"] = "queued"
    return sorted(books, key=lambda b: (category_rank(b["category"]), b["title"].lower()))

9. app\memory.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\memory.py143 lines4,785 bytesSHA-256 f07c6c67a12b5aff…

Long-term memory: facts about the user in data\memory.json, each with its search vector.

  • "remember that ..." (regex REMEMBER_RE) saves straight away; auto-learn asks the model after each Chat-mode reply (MEMORY_EXTRACT).
  • A new fact with similarity 0.92 or more to an old one is not saved twice.
  • relevant() returns up to memory_top_k facts scoring at least memory_min_score. Facts saved while the search engine was off get their vector later.
  • The Memory page can add, edit and delete facts (PUT and DELETE /api/memory/<id>).

app\memory.py143 lines
"""memory.py - the chatbot's long-term memory about you.

A language model cannot retrain itself while you talk to it. Instead, facts are
written to data\\memory.json (with a search vector each). Before every reply,
the memories that best match your message are found and handed to the model,
so it "remembers" them in later conversations.

Facts get in two ways:
  * you say "remember that ..."         -> saved straight away
  * auto-learn (can be switched off)    -> after each chat reply the model is
                                           asked to list new facts you stated
You can read, edit and delete everything on the Memory page.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import threading
import time
import uuid

import numpy as np

from . import llm
from .prompts import MEMORY_EXTRACT
from .settings import CONFIG, MEMORY_FILE, write_json_atomic

_lock = threading.Lock()
DUPLICATE_SCORE = 0.92   # a new fact this similar to an old one is not saved twice

REMEMBER_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:please\s+)?remember(?:\s+that)?[\s,:]+(.{3,})$", re.I | re.S)


def _load() -> list[dict]:
    import json
    try:
        return json.loads(MEMORY_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return []


def _save(items: list[dict]) -> None:
    write_json_atomic(MEMORY_FILE, items)


def list_all() -> list[dict]:
    return [{k: v for k, v in m.items() if k != "vec"} for m in _load()]


def _vector(text: str) -> list[float] | None:
    try:
        return [round(float(x), 5) for x in llm.embed([text])[0]]
    except Exception:
        return None  # search engine down: keep the fact, add its vector later


def add(text: str, source: str = "manual") -> dict | None:
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip().rstrip(".") + "."
    if len(text) < 4:
        return None
    vec = _vector(text)
    with _lock:
        items = _load()
        if vec is not None:
            for m in items:
                if m.get("vec") and float(np.dot(m["vec"], vec)) >= DUPLICATE_SCORE:
                    return None  # already known
        item = {"id": uuid.uuid4().hex[:10], "text": text, "source": source,
                "created": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"), "vec": vec}
        items.append(item)
        _save(items)
    return {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k != "vec"}


def update(mem_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    with _lock:
        items = _load()
        for m in items:
            if m["id"] == mem_id:
                m["text"], m["vec"] = text.strip(), _vector(text)
                _save(items)
                return True
    return False


def delete(mem_id: str) -> bool:
    with _lock:
        items = _load()
        kept = [m for m in items if m["id"] != mem_id]
        _save(kept)
        return len(kept) != len(items)


def relevant(message: str) -> list[str]:
    """The stored facts that best match this message."""
    items = _load()
    if not items:
        return []
    # Fill in vectors that are missing (saved while the search engine was off).
    if any(m.get("vec") is None for m in items):
        with _lock:
            items = _load()
            for m in items:
                if m.get("vec") is None:
                    m["vec"] = _vector(m["text"])
            _save(items)
    usable = [m for m in items if m.get("vec")]
    if not usable:
        return []
    if len(usable) <= int(CONFIG["memory_top_k"]):
        return [m["text"] for m in usable]
    try:
        q = llm.embed([message], is_query=True)[0]
    except Exception:
        return [m["text"] for m in usable[-int(CONFIG["memory_top_k"]):]]
    scores = np.asarray([m["vec"] for m in usable], dtype=np.float32) @ q
    order = np.argsort(-scores)[: int(CONFIG["memory_top_k"])]
    return [usable[i]["text"] for i in order if scores[i] >= float(CONFIG["memory_min_score"])]


def explicit_request(message: str) -> str | None:
    """'remember that my exam is on 3 Dec' -> 'my exam is on 3 Dec'."""
    m = REMEMBER_RE.match(message)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else None


def learn_from_turn(user_message: str, reply: str) -> list[str]:
    """Ask the model which new, lasting facts the user stated. Runs after the reply."""
    prompt = MEMORY_EXTRACT.format(user=user_message[:1500], reply=reply[:800])
    try:
        out = llm.chat_once([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}], max_tokens=120, temperature=0.0)
    except Exception:
        return []
    saved = []
    for line in out.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line.startswith("-"):
            continue
        fact = line.lstrip("- ").strip()
        if fact.upper().startswith("NONE") or len(fact) < 8:
            continue
        if add(fact, source="auto"):
            saved.append(fact)
    return saved

10. app\web.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\web.py111 lines4,506 bytesSHA-256 5300fc142cf16039…

Online mode: searches DuckDuckGo's plain-HTML page, reads the top 3 pages in parallel and keeps the paragraphs that best match the question.

  • No API key and no extra packages: urllib + regular expressions.
  • Search results come from html.duckduckgo.com/html/; redirect links (uddg=) are unwrapped.
  • From each page, scripts, menus and footers are dropped and paragraphs, list items and h2/h3 headings of 8+ words are kept, then the ones sharing the most words with the question, up to 170 words a page.
  • If a page is blocked or empty, the search snippet is used instead. No internet raises WebError with a friendly message.
Functions and classes in this file 8
NameWhat it does
class WebError
_get(url, timeout, data)
_clean(fragment)
search(query, n)
_page_text(url)
_words(text)
_best_parts(text, query, max_words)
gather(query, pages, words_per_page)Search, read the top pages, and return short relevant extracts with their links.
app\web.py111 lines
"""web.py - ONLINE mode: searches the internet and reads the top pages.

Uses only Python's built-in tools (no extra packages, no API key):
  1. asks DuckDuckGo's plain-HTML search page for results
  2. downloads the top few pages and strips them down to readable text
  3. keeps the paragraphs that share the most words with your question
If there is no internet, it raises WebError and the chat says so.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import html
import re
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

UA = ("Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 "
      "(KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0 Safari/537.36")
SEARCH_URL = "https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/"
STOP = set("the a an and or of to in on for is are was were be how what why when which who does do "
           "with by as at it this that from can i you me my your about explain".split())


class WebError(Exception):
    pass


def _get(url: str, timeout: float = 8, data: bytes | None = None) -> str:
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers={"User-Agent": UA, "Accept-Language": "en"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
        ctype = r.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
        if "html" not in ctype and "text" not in ctype:
            return ""
        raw = r.read(1_500_000)
        charset = r.headers.get_content_charset() or "utf-8"
    return raw.decode(charset, errors="replace")


def _clean(fragment: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", fragment)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", html.unescape(text)).strip()


def search(query: str, n: int = 6) -> list[dict]:
    try:
        page = _get(SEARCH_URL, data=urllib.parse.urlencode({"q": query}).encode())
    except Exception as e:
        raise WebError(f"Could not reach the internet ({e.__class__.__name__}). Check your connection, "
                       "or switch to Offline mode to use your books.")
    results = []
    for m in re.finditer(r'<a[^>]+class="result__a"[^>]+href="([^"]+)"[^>]*>(.*?)</a>(.*?)(?=<a[^>]+class="result__a"|$)',
                         page, re.S):
        href, title, rest = m.group(1), _clean(m.group(2)), m.group(3)
        href = html.unescape(href)
        q = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlparse(href).query)
        if "uddg" in q:
            href = q["uddg"][0]
        if not href.startswith("http") or "duckduckgo.com" in href:
            continue
        snip = re.search(r'class="result__snippet"[^>]*>(.*?)</a>', rest, re.S)
        results.append({"title": title, "url": href, "snippet": _clean(snip.group(1)) if snip else ""})
        if len(results) >= n:
            break
    return results


def _page_text(url: str) -> str:
    try:
        page = _get(url)
    except Exception:
        return ""
    page = re.sub(r"(?is)<(script|style|nav|header|footer|aside|form|noscript|svg)[^>]*>.*?</\1>", " ", page)
    paras = re.findall(r"(?is)<(?:p|li|h2|h3)[^>]*>(.*?)</(?:p|li|h2|h3)>", page)
    return "\n".join(t for t in (_clean(p) for p in paras) if len(t.split()) >= 8)


def _words(text: str) -> set[str]:
    return {w for w in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", text.lower()) if w not in STOP and len(w) > 2}


def _best_parts(text: str, query: str, max_words: int) -> str:
    qw = _words(query)
    paras = [p for p in text.split("\n") if p]
    scored = sorted(((len(qw & _words(p)), i, p) for i, p in enumerate(paras)), reverse=True)
    chosen, total = [], 0
    for score, i, p in scored:
        if score == 0 and chosen:
            break
        w = len(p.split())
        if total + w > max_words:
            continue
        chosen.append((i, p)); total += w
    return " ".join(p for _, p in sorted(chosen))


def gather(query: str, pages: int = 3, words_per_page: int = 170) -> list[dict]:
    """Search, read the top pages, and return short relevant extracts with their links."""
    results = search(query)
    if not results:
        return []
    top = results[:pages]
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=pages) as pool:
        texts = list(pool.map(_page_text, [r["url"] for r in top]))
    out = []
    for r, text in zip(top, texts):
        extract = _best_parts(text, query, words_per_page) if text else ""
        if len(extract.split()) < 25:          # page blocked or empty: fall back to the search snippet
            extract = r["snippet"]
        if extract:
            out.append({"title": r["title"], "url": r["url"], "text": extract})
    return out

11. app\runner.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\runner.py80 lines3,302 bytesSHA-256 bd9a081876cbabb1…

Runs Python or Java code from an answer on this laptop (the Run button) and returns its output.

  • Python uses QuantaKai's own portable Python in isolated mode (-I), so numpy is available.
  • Java needs a JDK on PATH or in JAVA_HOME; one .java file is launched directly (Java 11+ single-file mode).
  • Each run gets a fresh temporary folder in data, no keyboard input, and is stopped after run_timeout_seconds (20). Output is capped at 20,000 characters.
  • This is why server.py checks the Origin header: no other website may call /api/run.
Functions and classes in this file 4

app\runner.py80 lines
"""runner.py - runs code that QuantaKai wrote, on this laptop, and returns the output.

Python uses QuantaKai's own portable Python (numpy is available).
Java needs a JDK installed on the laptop (java 11 or newer can run one .java file directly).
Each run happens in a fresh temporary folder, with no keyboard input, and is stopped
after a time limit so a never-ending loop can't freeze anything.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path

from .settings import CONFIG, DATA_DIR

NO_WINDOW = 0x08000000 if os.name == "nt" else 0
MAX_OUTPUT = 20_000


def _python() -> str:
    return sys.executable   # the portable Python QuantaKai itself runs on


def _java() -> str | None:
    found = shutil.which("java")
    if found:
        return found
    home = os.environ.get("JAVA_HOME")
    if home and (Path(home) / "bin" / "java.exe").exists():
        return str(Path(home) / "bin" / "java.exe")
    return None


def languages() -> dict:
    return {"python": True, "java": _java() is not None}


def run(code: str, language: str) -> dict:
    language = (language or "").lower()
    if language in ("py", "python", "python3"):
        language = "python"
    if language not in ("python", "java"):
        return {"ok": False, "output": "", "error": f"Running {language or 'this'} code isn't supported. Python and Java are."}
    if len(code) > 100_000:
        return {"ok": False, "output": "", "error": "That code is too long to run."}
    timeout = int(CONFIG.get("run_timeout_seconds", 20))
    work = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="run-", dir=DATA_DIR))
    try:
        if language == "python":
            src = work / "main.py"
            src.write_text(code, encoding="utf-8")
            cmd = [_python(), "-I", str(src)]
        else:
            java = _java()
            if not java:
                return {"ok": False, "output": "", "error": "Java isn't installed (or isn't on PATH). Install a JDK 17+ to run Java code."}
            m = re.search(r"public\s+(?:final\s+)?class\s+(\w+)", code)
            src = work / f"{m.group(1) if m else 'Main'}.java"
            src.write_text(code, encoding="utf-8")
            cmd = [java, str(src)]           # single-file launch, no separate compile step
        started = time.time()
        try:
            p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(work), capture_output=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                               timeout=timeout, creationflags=NO_WINDOW,
                               env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            out = (e.stdout or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace")[:MAX_OUTPUT]
            return {"ok": False, "output": out, "error": f"Stopped after {timeout} seconds (an endless loop, or waiting for input?).",
                    "seconds": timeout}
        out = p.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
        err = p.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").replace(str(work) + os.sep, "")
        return {"ok": p.returncode == 0, "output": out[:MAX_OUTPUT], "error": err[-MAX_OUTPUT:],
                "exit_code": p.returncode, "seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2)}
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)

12. app\ocr.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\ocr.py78 lines3,867 bytesSHA-256 054d5718d45ee062…

Reads the text in a picture (a screenshot of a question, a photo of notes) with the text recognition built into Windows 10/11.

  • Writes a small PowerShell script to data\tmp that calls Windows.Media.Ocr; works offline, needs no download.
  • Accepts PNG, JPG, WebP, BMP and GIF data URLs. A picture-capable model (with its mmproj file) also receives the picture itself.
Functions and classes in this file 2

app\ocr.py78 lines
"""ocr.py - reads the text in a picture (a screenshot of a test question, a photo of notes).

It uses the text recognition built into Windows 10 and 11 (Windows.Media.Ocr), so it
works offline and needs no download. If the loaded chat model can see pictures (a
Qwen 3.5 model with its vision file), the picture is also sent to the model itself.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import os
import re
import subprocess
import uuid

from .settings import DATA_DIR

NO_WINDOW = 0x08000000 if os.name == "nt" else 0
TMP_DIR = DATA_DIR / "tmp"

# PowerShell 5 (built into Windows) can call the Windows text-recognition engine directly.
_SCRIPT = r'''
param([string]$path)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
[Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Runtime.WindowsRuntime
$null = [Windows.Storage.StorageFile, Windows.Storage, ContentType = WindowsRuntime]
$null = [Windows.Media.Ocr.OcrEngine, Windows.Foundation, ContentType = WindowsRuntime]
$null = [Windows.Graphics.Imaging.BitmapDecoder, Windows.Graphics, ContentType = WindowsRuntime]
$asTask = ([System.WindowsRuntimeSystemExtensions].GetMethods() | Where-Object {
    $_.Name -eq 'AsTask' -and $_.GetParameters().Count -eq 1 -and
    $_.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType.Name -eq 'IAsyncOperation`1' })[0]
function Await($op, [Type]$type) {
    $task = $asTask.MakeGenericMethod($type).Invoke($null, @($op)); $task.Wait(-1) | Out-Null; $task.Result }
$file = Await ([Windows.Storage.StorageFile]::GetFileFromPathAsync($path)) ([Windows.Storage.StorageFile])
$stream = Await ($file.OpenAsync([Windows.Storage.FileAccessMode]::Read)) ([Windows.Storage.Streams.IRandomAccessStream])
$decoder = Await ([Windows.Graphics.Imaging.BitmapDecoder]::CreateAsync($stream)) ([Windows.Graphics.Imaging.BitmapDecoder])
$bitmap = Await ($decoder.GetSoftwareBitmapAsync()) ([Windows.Graphics.Imaging.SoftwareBitmap])
$engine = [Windows.Media.Ocr.OcrEngine]::TryCreateFromUserProfileLanguages()
if ($null -eq $engine) { throw 'No text-recognition language is installed in Windows.' }
$result = Await ($engine.RecognizeAsync($bitmap)) ([Windows.Media.Ocr.OcrResult])
foreach ($line in $result.Lines) { $line.Text }
$stream.Dispose()
'''


def decode_data_url(data_url: str) -> tuple[bytes, str]:
    m = re.match(r"data:image/(png|jpe?g|webp|bmp|gif);base64,(.+)", data_url or "", re.S)
    if not m:
        raise ValueError("Not a picture the app can read (use PNG or JPG).")
    ext = "jpg" if m.group(1) in ("jpg", "jpeg") else m.group(1)
    return base64.b64decode(m.group(2)), ext


def read_text(data_url: str) -> str:
    """Return the text found in the picture ('' if none). Raises RuntimeError on failure."""
    if os.name != "nt":
        raise RuntimeError("Reading text from pictures uses Windows' built-in text recognition.")
    raw, ext = decode_data_url(data_url)
    TMP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    img = TMP_DIR / f"ocr-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.{ext}"
    script = TMP_DIR / "ocr.ps1"
    try:
        img.write_bytes(raw)
        if not script.exists() or script.read_text(encoding="utf-8") != _SCRIPT:
            script.write_text(_SCRIPT, encoding="utf-8")
        run = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-File", str(script), str(img)],
                             capture_output=True, timeout=90, creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
        if run.returncode != 0:
            err = run.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip().splitlines()
            raise RuntimeError("Could not read the picture: " + (err[-1] if err else "unknown error"))
        return run.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        raise RuntimeError("Reading the picture took too long.")
    finally:
        try:
            img.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass

13. app\docs.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\docs.py429 lines17,637 bytesSHA-256 eaa6d0f8da0f8cf2…

Documents attached to one chat: read once, saved beside the chat, and their most relevant parts sent with each question in that chat.

  • Reads PDF/EPUB, Word, PowerPoint, Excel, OpenDocument, RTF, HTML/XML, CSV/TSV, JSON, notebooks, text, subtitles, logs and source code; old .doc/.ppt/.xls as best it can.
  • Limits: 18 MB per file, 12 documents per chat, 3 million characters per document.
  • Stored as data\chat_docs\<chat id>\<doc id>.txt; deleting the chat deletes them.
  • context_block(): if everything fits the token budget it is sent whole; otherwise 1000-character chunks are ranked with BM25 plus a bonus for the document's name and its first chunk.

"""
QuantaKai - documents attached to one conversation (its "context").

You attach files to a chat; their text is read once and saved next to that chat.
For every question in that chat, the most relevant parts are given to the model
along with your question, so it can answer from them. Other chats don't see them.

Reads: PDF, EPUB, Word (.docx, and old .doc as best it can), PowerPoint (.pptx, old .ppt),
Excel (.xlsx, old .xls), OpenDocument (.odt .ods .odp), RTF, HTML/XML, CSV/TSV, JSON,
Jupyter notebooks, Markdown/plain text, subtitles, logs, and source code of any language.
Anything else that is mostly text is read as text too.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import html
import json
import math
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import time
import uuid
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from .settings import DATA_DIR

CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 3.5

DOCS_DIR = DATA_DIR / "chat_docs"
MAX_FILE_BYTES = 18 * 1024 * 1024          # about 18 MB per file
MAX_TEXT_CHARS = 3_000_000                 # very long files are cut after this
MAX_DOCS_PER_CHAT = 12

ZIP_XML = {".docx", ".pptx", ".xlsx", ".odt", ".ods", ".odp"}


class DocError(Exception):
    pass


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- reading files
def _decode(data: bytes) -> str:
    if data[:3] == b"\xef\xbb\xbf":
        return data[3:].decode("utf-8", "replace")
    if data[:2] in (b"\xff\xfe", b"\xfe\xff"):
        return data.decode("utf-16", "replace")
    try:
        return data.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return data.decode("cp1252", "replace")


def _looks_like_text(data: bytes) -> bool:
    head = data[:8192]
    if not head:
        return False
    if head[:2] in (b"\xff\xfe", b"\xfe\xff"):
        return True
    if b"\x00" in head:
        return False
    printable = sum(32 <= b < 127 or b in (9, 10, 13) or b >= 128 for b in head)
    return printable / len(head) > 0.92


def _strip_html(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"(?is)<(script|style|head)[^>]*>.*?</\1>", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(?i)<br\s*/?>|</(p|div|li|tr|h[1-6]|section|article)>", "\n", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(?i)</t[dh]>", "\t", text)
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text)
    return html.unescape(text)


_RTF_SKIP = ("fonttbl", "colortbl", "stylesheet", "info", "listtable", "listoverridetable", "pict",
             "themedata", "colorschememapping", "latentstyles", "datastore", "xmlnstbl", "rsidtbl", "generator")


def _strip_rtf(text: str) -> str:
    # first drop whole groups that hold fonts, colours, styles, pictures and other settings
    out, i, n = [], 0, len(text)
    skip = re.compile(r"\{\\(\*|(%s)\b)" % "|".join(_RTF_SKIP))
    while i < n:
        ch = text[i]
        if ch == "\\":
            out.append(text[i:i + 2]); i += 2; continue
        if ch == "{" and skip.match(text, i):
            depth = 0
            while i < n:
                c = text[i]
                if c == "\\":
                    i += 2; continue
                depth += (c == "{") - (c == "}")
                i += 1
                if depth == 0:
                    break
            continue
        out.append(ch); i += 1
    text = "".join(out)
    text = re.sub(r"\\'([0-9a-fA-F]{2})", lambda m: bytes([int(m.group(1), 16)]).decode("cp1252", "replace"), text)
    text = re.sub(r"\\u(-?\d+)\??", lambda m: chr(int(m.group(1)) % 65536), text)
    text = re.sub(r"\\(par|line|row)\b ?", "\n", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\\tab\b ?|\\cell\b ?", "\t", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\\([{}\\])", r"\1", re.sub(r"\\[a-zA-Z]+-?\d* ?", "", text))
    return re.sub(r"(?<!\\)[{}]", "", text)


def _xml_text(xml: bytes, para_tags=("p", "h", "list-item"), cell_tags=("table-cell",)) -> str:
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    root = ET.fromstring(xml)
    out: list[str] = []

    def walk(el, in_cell=False):
        tag = el.tag.split("}")[-1]
        cell = in_cell or tag in cell_tags
        if el.text:
            out.append(el.text)
        for child in el:
            walk(child, cell)
            if child.tail:
                out.append(child.tail)
        if tag == "tab":
            out.append("\t")
        elif tag == "s":
            out.append(" ")
        elif tag in para_tags or tag in ("line-break", "br"):
            out.append(" " if in_cell else "\n")
        if tag in cell_tags:
            out.append("\t")
        elif tag == "table-row":
            out.append("\n")

    walk(root)
    return "".join(out)


def _xlsx_text(z: zipfile.ZipFile) -> str:
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    ns = {"m": "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main"}
    shared: list[str] = []
    if "xl/sharedStrings.xml" in z.namelist():
        for si in ET.fromstring(z.read("xl/sharedStrings.xml")).findall("m:si", ns):
            shared.append("".join(t.text or "" for t in si.iter(f"{{{ns['m']}}}t")))
    names = {}
    try:
        wb = ET.fromstring(z.read("xl/workbook.xml"))
        for i, sh in enumerate(wb.iter(f"{{{ns['m']}}}sheet"), start=1):
            names[i] = sh.get("name")
    except (KeyError, ET.ParseError):
        pass
    sheets = sorted((n for n in z.namelist() if re.fullmatch(r"xl/worksheets/sheet\d+\.xml", n)),
                    key=lambda n: int(re.search(r"(\d+)", n.rsplit("/", 1)[1]).group(1)))
    parts = []
    for n in sheets:
        idx = int(re.search(r"(\d+)\.xml$", n).group(1))
        rows = []
        for row in ET.fromstring(z.read(n)).iter(f"{{{ns['m']}}}row"):
            cells = []
            for c in row.findall("m:c", ns):
                t, v = c.get("t"), c.find("m:v", ns)
                if t == "s" and v is not None and v.text and v.text.isdigit() and int(v.text) < len(shared):
                    cells.append(shared[int(v.text)])
                elif t == "inlineStr":
                    cells.append("".join(x.text or "" for x in c.iter(f"{{{ns['m']}}}t")))
                elif v is not None and v.text is not None:
                    cells.append(v.text)
                else:
                    cells.append("")
            if any(x.strip() for x in cells):
                rows.append("\t".join(cells).rstrip())
        if rows:
            parts.append(f"## Sheet: {names.get(idx, idx)}\n" + "\n".join(rows))
    return "\n\n".join(parts)


_OFFICE_JUNK = re.compile(
    r"^(Microsoft.*|Times New Roman|Arial.*|Calibri.*|Cambria.*|Symbol|Wingdings.*|Courier New|Liberation .*|DejaVu .*|"
    r"OpenSymbol|Lucida .*|Normal.*|Default Paragraph Font|Table Normal|No List|Root Entry|WordDocument|"
    r"(Document)?SummaryInformation|CompObj|Workbook|Book|[01]Table|Data|ObjectPool|Header.*|Footer.*|Heading( \d)?( Char)?|"
    r".* Char|ListLabel \d+|Title|Subtitle|Body Text.*|Caption|Index|List.*|Strong|Emphasis|Intense .*|Subtle .*|"
    r"Book Title|Quote|Macro Text|General|openpyxl|AppVersion|Calc|Sheet\d*|PowerPoint Document|Current User|Pictures|Biff8|Ole|3\.1)$", re.I)


def _legacy_office_text(data: bytes, ext: str = "") -> str:
    """Old .doc / .xls / .ppt files: pull out the readable text (best effort)."""
    u16 = [(m.start(), m.group().decode("utf-16-le", "ignore"))
           for m in re.finditer(rb"(?:[\x20-\x7e\xa0-\xff\r\t\x07\x0b]\x00){3,}", data)]
    if ext in (".doc", ".dot", ".wps"):
        # Word keeps the whole body as one long run of text; settings and style names are short runs
        longest = max((len(t) for _, t in u16), default=0)
        runs = [r for r in u16 if len(r[1]) >= max(30, longest * 0.25)] or u16
    else:
        a8 = [(m.start(), m.group().decode("cp1252", "ignore"))
              for m in re.finditer(rb"[\x20-\x7e\t]{3,}", data)]
        runs = sorted(u16 + a8)
    lines, seen = [], set()
    for _, t in runs:
        for line in t.replace("\x07", "\t").replace("\x0b", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").split("\n"):
            line = line.strip()
            if len(line) < 2 or _OFFICE_JUNK.match(line) or not re.search(r"[A-Za-z0-9]", line):
                continue
            if sum(ch.isalnum() or ch in " .,;:'\"-()!?%/&\t=+" for ch in line) / len(line) < 0.8:
                continue
            if max(line.count(c) for c in set(line)) > len(line) * 0.6 and len(line) > 6:   # 'ÿÿÿÿ' style filler
                continue
            if ext not in (".doc", ".dot", ".wps"):
                if line in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(line)
            lines.append(line)
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _via_library(name: str, data: bytes) -> str:
    """PDF, EPUB, DOCX, PPTX: use the same readers as the book library."""
    from . import library
    folder = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(dir=str(DATA_DIR)))
    try:
        p = folder / ("doc" + Path(name).suffix.lower())
        p.write_bytes(data)
        pages, _ = library.extract_pages(p)
        suffix = p.suffix
        label = "Slide" if suffix == ".pptx" else "Page"
        if suffix in (".pdf", ".pptx"):
            return "\n\n".join(f"[{label} {n}]\n{t}" for n, t in pages if t.strip())
        return "\n\n".join(t for _, t in pages if t.strip())
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)


def extract_text(name: str, data: bytes) -> str:
    ext = Path(name).suffix.lower()
    if ext in (".pdf", ".epub", ".docx", ".pptx", ".xps", ".fb2", ".mobi", ".cbz"):
        try:
            if ext in (".pdf", ".epub", ".docx", ".pptx"):
                return _via_library(name, data)
            import pymupdf
            with pymupdf.open(stream=data, filetype=ext[1:]) as doc:
                return "\n\n".join(p.get_text("text") for p in doc)
        except Exception as e:
            raise DocError(f"Could not read {name}: {e}")
    if ext in ZIP_XML or (data[:2] == b"PK" and ext in (".odg", ".ott", ".dotx", ".potx", ".xltx")):
        try:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(__import__("io").BytesIO(data)) as z:
                if ext == ".xlsx" or ext == ".xltx":
                    return _xlsx_text(z)
                return _xml_text(z.read("content.xml"))
        except (zipfile.BadZipFile, KeyError) as e:
            raise DocError(f"{name} looks damaged ({e}).")
    if ext in (".doc", ".xls", ".ppt", ".dot", ".pps", ".wps"):
        text = _legacy_office_text(data, ext)
        if len(text) < 20:
            raise DocError(f"Could not read the old-style file {name}. Save it as .docx/.xlsx/.pptx and attach it again.")
        return "(Read from an old-style Office file; some formatting or tables may be jumbled.)\n" + text
    if ext == ".ipynb":
        try:
            nb = json.loads(_decode(data))
            cells = []
            for c in nb.get("cells", []):
                src = "".join(c.get("source", []))
                cells.append(f"```python\n{src}\n```" if c.get("cell_type") == "code" else src)
            return "\n\n".join(cells)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    if not _looks_like_text(data):
        raise DocError(f"{name} doesn't look like a text document, so it can't be read. "
                       "Pictures go in as pictures; other files can be saved as PDF, Word or text first.")
    text = _decode(data)
    if ext in (".html", ".htm", ".xhtml", ".mhtml") or (ext in ("", ".xml") and "<html" in text[:2000].lower()):
        return _strip_html(text)
    if ext == ".rtf" or text.startswith("{\\rtf"):
        return _strip_rtf(text)
    if ext == ".svg":
        return _strip_html(text)
    return text


def _clean(text: str) -> str:
    text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").replace("\x00", "")
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t\u00a0]+\n", "\n", text)
    text = re.sub(r" +\t", "\t", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    return text.strip()[:MAX_TEXT_CHARS]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- storage per chat
def _folder(conv_id: str) -> Path:
    return DOCS_DIR / re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]", "", conv_id)


def decode_upload(data: str) -> bytes:
    if data.startswith("data:"):
        data = data.split(",", 1)[1] if "," in data else ""
    try:
        raw = base64.b64decode(data, validate=False)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        raise DocError("The file did not arrive correctly. Try attaching it again.")
    if not raw:
        raise DocError("That file is empty.")
    if len(raw) > MAX_FILE_BYTES:
        raise DocError(f"That file is {len(raw) / 1048576:.0f} MB. Files up to {MAX_FILE_BYTES // 1048576} MB can be attached.")
    return raw


def add(conv: dict, name: str, raw: bytes) -> dict:
    docs = conv.setdefault("docs", [])
    if len(docs) >= MAX_DOCS_PER_CHAT:
        raise DocError(f"A chat can hold up to {MAX_DOCS_PER_CHAT} documents. Remove one first.")
    name = Path(name or "document.txt").name[:120]
    text = _clean(extract_text(name, raw))
    if len(text) < 2:
        raise DocError(f"No readable text was found in {name}. If it is a scanned PDF, attach the pages as pictures instead.")
    doc = {"id": uuid.uuid4().hex[:10], "name": name, "chars": len(text),
           "words": len(text.split()), "added": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")}
    folder = _folder(conv["id"])
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (folder / f"{doc['id']}.txt").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    docs.append(doc)
    return doc


def remove(conv: dict, doc_id: str) -> bool:
    docs = conv.get("docs", [])
    keep = [d for d in docs if d["id"] != doc_id]
    if len(keep) == len(docs):
        return False
    conv["docs"] = keep
    try:
        (_folder(conv["id"]) / f"{re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]', '', doc_id)}.txt").unlink()
    except OSError:
        pass
    return True


def delete_all(conv_id: str) -> None:
    shutil.rmtree(_folder(conv_id), ignore_errors=True)


def _read(conv_id: str, doc: dict) -> str:
    try:
        return (_folder(conv_id) / f"{doc['id']}.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        return ""


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- choosing what to show the model
def _terms(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return [w for w in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9_]+", text.lower()) if len(w) > 1]


def _chunks(text: str, size: int = 1000, overlap: int = 150) -> list[str]:
    if len(text) <= size:
        return [text]
    out, start = [], 0
    while start < len(text):
        end = min(len(text), start + size)
        if end < len(text):                                     # finish at a line or sentence end if close by
            cut = max(text.rfind("\n", start + size // 2, end), text.rfind(". ", start + size // 2, end))
            if cut > 0:
                end = cut + 1
        out.append(text[start:end].strip())
        if end >= len(text):
            break
        start = max(end - overlap, start + 1)
    return [c for c in out if c]


def context_block(conv: dict, question: str, token_budget: int) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """The attached documents (or their most relevant parts) as text for the model.
    Returns (text, names of the documents used)."""
    docs = conv.get("docs") or []
    if not docs or token_budget < 80:
        return "", []
    char_budget = int(token_budget * CHARS_PER_TOKEN)
    texts = [(d, _read(conv["id"], d)) for d in docs]
    texts = [(d, t) for d, t in texts if t]
    if not texts:
        return "", []
    header = ("Documents attached to this conversation by the user. Use them as background context and as the "
              "main source when the question is about them; say which document you used. "
              "If the answer is not in them, say so and answer from general knowledge.\n")
    if sum(len(t) for _, t in texts) + 40 * len(texts) <= char_budget:      # small enough: send everything
        body = "\n\n".join(f"=== {d['name']} ===\n{t}" for d, t in texts)
        return header + body, [d["name"] for d, _ in texts]

    # too big: pick the most relevant parts (keyword scoring, like the book search)
    pieces = []   # (doc index, chunk index, text)
    for di, (d, t) in enumerate(texts):
        for ci, c in enumerate(_chunks(t)):
            pieces.append((di, ci, c))
    q = set(_terms(question))
    df: dict[str, int] = {}
    toks = []
    for _, _, c in pieces:
        ts = _terms(c)
        toks.append(ts)
        for w in set(ts) & q:
            df[w] = df.get(w, 0) + 1
    n = len(pieces)
    avg = sum(len(t) for t in toks) / max(1, n)
    scores = []
    for i, ts in enumerate(toks):
        s, counts = 0.0, {}
        for w in ts:
            if w in q:
                counts[w] = counts.get(w, 0) + 1
        for w, f in counts.items():
            idf = math.log(1 + (n - df[w] + 0.5) / (df[w] + 0.5))
            s += idf * f * 2.2 / (f + 1.2 * (0.25 + 0.75 * len(ts) / max(1, avg)))
        name_bonus = 0.6 if q & set(_terms(texts[pieces[i][0]][0]["name"])) else 0.0
        scores.append(s + name_bonus + (0.35 if pieces[i][1] == 0 else 0.0))   # the start of a document often says what it is
    order = sorted(range(n), key=lambda i: -scores[i])
    chosen, used = [], 40 * len(texts)
    for i in order:
        cost = len(pieces[i][2]) + 8
        if used + cost > char_budget:
            continue
        chosen.append(i)
        used += cost
    chosen.sort(key=lambda i: (pieces[i][0], pieces[i][1]))
    blocks, last_doc, names = [], None, []
    for i in chosen:
        di, ci, c = pieces[i]
        if di != last_doc:
            blocks.append(f"=== {texts[di][0]['name']} (relevant parts) ===")
            names.append(texts[di][0]["name"])
            last_doc = di
        blocks.append(c + "\n[...]")
    return header + "\n".join(blocks), names

14. app\organize.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\organize.py67 lines2,761 bytesSHA-256 bb229879c41735bd…

Tidies downloads: files dropped in a "new stuff" or "inbox" folder inside QuantaKai are moved to the right folder at start-up.

  • .gguf to models; ggml-*.bin, Piper .onnx/.onnx.json, whisper-bin/piper zips and sherpa-onnx/kokoro .tar.bz2 to voice.
  • Unfinished downloads (.part, .crdownload, .tmp) are skipped. A smaller existing copy (an incomplete download) is replaced.
Functions and classes in this file 2
NameWhat it does
_target(f)
tidy_downloads()
app\organize.py67 lines
"""organize.py - tidies downloads into the right QuantaKai folders.

Drop downloaded files into a folder called "new stuff" (or "inbox") inside
QuantaKai, and the next time QuantaKai starts it moves each one where it belongs:
  - chat models (.gguf)                      -> models
  - Whisper speech model (ggml-*.bin)        -> voice
  - Piper voices (.onnx + .onnx.json)        -> voice
  - whisper-bin-x64.zip / piper_*.zip        -> voice (unpacked automatically)
  - sherpa-onnx-*.tar.bz2 / kokoro-*.tar.bz2  -> voice (Kokoro voice, unpacked automatically)
Files it doesn't recognise are left alone. Moving on the same drive is instant.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

from .settings import CONFIG, MODELS_DIR, ROOT

VOICE_DIR = ROOT / "voice"
INBOXES = ("new stuff", "inbox")


def _target(f: Path) -> Path | None:
    name = f.name.lower()
    if name.endswith(".gguf"):
        return MODELS_DIR          # chat models and their vision ("mmproj") files
    if name.startswith("ggml-") and name.endswith(".bin"):
        return VOICE_DIR
    if name.endswith(".onnx.json"):
        return VOICE_DIR
    if name.endswith(".onnx"):
        wanted = CONFIG.get("tts_voice", "en_US-hfc_female-medium").lower() + ".onnx"
        has_settings = (f.parent / (f.name + ".json")).exists() or (VOICE_DIR / (f.name + ".json")).exists()
        return VOICE_DIR if (name == wanted or has_settings) else None
    if name.endswith(".zip") and (name.startswith("whisper-bin") or name.startswith("piper")):
        return VOICE_DIR
    if name.endswith(".tar.bz2") and (name.startswith("sherpa-onnx") or name.startswith("kokoro")):
        return VOICE_DIR
    return None


def tidy_downloads() -> list[str]:
    moved = []
    for box in INBOXES:
        folder = ROOT / box
        if not folder.is_dir():
            continue
        for f in sorted(folder.iterdir()):
            if not f.is_file() or f.name.endswith((".part", ".crdownload", ".tmp")):
                continue
            dest_dir = _target(f)
            if dest_dir is None:
                continue
            dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            dest = dest_dir / f.name
            if dest.exists():
                if dest.stat().st_size >= f.stat().st_size:
                    continue      # already have it; leave the spare copy for you to delete
                dest.unlink()     # the one already there is smaller: an incomplete download
            try:
                os.replace(f, dest)
                moved.append(f"{f.name} -> {dest_dir.name}")
            except OSError as e:
                print(f"  tidy: could not move {f.name}: {e}")
    for m in moved:
        print(f"  tidy: moved {m}")
    return moved

15. app\kokoro_worker.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\kokoro_worker.py174 lines7,649 bytesSHA-256 c9da9aea89062804…

A helper program that keeps the Kokoro voice loaded in memory and turns each line of text it receives into a WAV file.

  • Runs as its own process (started by voice.py) so a crash in the voice library cannot take down QuantaKai.
  • Calls sherpa-onnx-c-api.dll through ctypes. The structure layouts are copied from sherpa-onnx v1.13.5 c-api.h: use exactly that release.
  • Protocol: one JSON object per line in; replies are lines starting with @@ followed by JSON; other lines are library messages and are ignored.

app\kokoro_worker.py174 lines
"""
QuantaKai - Kokoro voice worker.

Runs as its own small program next to QuantaKai. It loads the Kokoro voice model ONCE,
then keeps it in memory and turns each line of text it is sent into speech. Before this,
the whole 114 MB model was reloaded for every single sentence, which is why Kokoro was so slow.

It runs in a separate process on purpose: if the voice library ever crashes, only this
helper stops, and QuantaKai simply starts it again (or falls back to the old way).

Talking to it (one JSON object per line):
    in : {"text": "...", "sid": 3, "speed": 1.0, "out": "C:/.../tts-1234.wav"}
    out: @@{"ok": true, "seconds": 0.8, "audio_seconds": 2.6}
Lines that do not start with @@ are just messages from the library and are ignored.

Usage: python -I kokoro_worker.py <folder with sherpa-onnx-c-api.dll> <kokoro folder> <lexicon file or -> <threads>
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ctypes
import json
import os
import struct
import sys
import time
from ctypes import POINTER, Structure, c_char_p, c_float, c_int32, c_void_p
from pathlib import Path


def say(obj: dict) -> None:
    sys.stdout.write("@@" + json.dumps(obj) + "\n")
    sys.stdout.flush()


# ---- The exact layouts from sherpa-onnx v1.13.5 include/sherpa-onnx/c-api/c-api.h ----
class Vits(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("model", c_char_p), ("lexicon", c_char_p), ("tokens", c_char_p), ("data_dir", c_char_p),
                ("noise_scale", c_float), ("noise_scale_w", c_float), ("length_scale", c_float),
                ("dict_dir", c_char_p)]


class Matcha(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("acoustic_model", c_char_p), ("vocoder", c_char_p), ("lexicon", c_char_p), ("tokens", c_char_p),
                ("data_dir", c_char_p), ("noise_scale", c_float), ("length_scale", c_float), ("dict_dir", c_char_p)]


class Kokoro(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("model", c_char_p), ("voices", c_char_p), ("tokens", c_char_p), ("data_dir", c_char_p),
                ("length_scale", c_float), ("dict_dir", c_char_p), ("lexicon", c_char_p), ("lang", c_char_p)]


class Kitten(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("model", c_char_p), ("voices", c_char_p), ("tokens", c_char_p), ("data_dir", c_char_p),
                ("length_scale", c_float)]


class Zipvoice(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("tokens", c_char_p), ("encoder", c_char_p), ("decoder", c_char_p), ("vocoder", c_char_p),
                ("data_dir", c_char_p), ("lexicon", c_char_p), ("feat_scale", c_float), ("t_shift", c_float),
                ("target_rms", c_float), ("guidance_scale", c_float)]


class Pocket(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("lm_flow", c_char_p), ("lm_main", c_char_p), ("encoder", c_char_p), ("decoder", c_char_p),
                ("text_conditioner", c_char_p), ("vocab_json", c_char_p), ("token_scores_json", c_char_p),
                ("voice_embedding_cache_capacity", c_int32)]


class Supertonic(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("duration_predictor", c_char_p), ("text_encoder", c_char_p), ("vector_estimator", c_char_p),
                ("vocoder", c_char_p), ("tts_json", c_char_p), ("unicode_indexer", c_char_p),
                ("voice_style", c_char_p)]


class ModelConfig(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("vits", Vits), ("num_threads", c_int32), ("debug", c_int32), ("provider", c_char_p),
                ("matcha", Matcha), ("kokoro", Kokoro), ("kitten", Kitten), ("zipvoice", Zipvoice),
                ("pocket", Pocket), ("supertonic", Supertonic)]


class TtsConfig(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("model", ModelConfig), ("rule_fsts", c_char_p), ("max_num_sentences", c_int32),
                ("rule_fars", c_char_p), ("silence_scale", c_float)]


class GeneratedAudio(Structure):
    _fields_ = [("samples", POINTER(c_float)), ("n", c_int32), ("sample_rate", c_int32)]


def b(path) -> bytes | None:
    return str(path).encode("utf-8") if path else None


def write_wav(path: str, samples, n: int, rate: int) -> None:
    """Float samples in [-1, 1] -> 16-bit mono WAV."""
    try:
        import numpy as np
        pcm = (np.clip(np.ctypeslib.as_array(samples, shape=(n,)), -1.0, 1.0) * 32767).astype("<i2").tobytes()
    except Exception:
        from array import array
        pcm = array("h", (int(max(-1.0, min(1.0, samples[i])) * 32767) for i in range(n))).tobytes()
    header = (b"RIFF" + struct.pack("<I", 36 + len(pcm)) + b"WAVEfmt " +
              struct.pack("<IHHIIHH", 16, 1, 1, rate, rate * 2, 2, 16) + b"data" + struct.pack("<I", len(pcm)))
    tmp = path + ".part"
    with open(tmp, "wb") as f:
        f.write(header + pcm)
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def main() -> None:
    lib_dir, kdir, lexicon, threads = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
    if hasattr(os, "add_dll_directory"):
        os.add_dll_directory(str(lib_dir))
    lib = ctypes.CDLL(str(lib_dir / ("sherpa-onnx-c-api.dll" if os.name == "nt" else "libsherpa-onnx-c-api.so")))
    lib.SherpaOnnxCreateOfflineTts.argtypes = [POINTER(TtsConfig)]
    lib.SherpaOnnxCreateOfflineTts.restype = c_void_p
    lib.SherpaOnnxOfflineTtsSampleRate.argtypes = [c_void_p]
    lib.SherpaOnnxOfflineTtsSampleRate.restype = c_int32
    lib.SherpaOnnxOfflineTtsGenerate.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_char_p, c_int32, c_float]
    lib.SherpaOnnxOfflineTtsGenerate.restype = POINTER(GeneratedAudio)
    lib.SherpaOnnxDestroyOfflineTtsGeneratedAudio.argtypes = [POINTER(GeneratedAudio)]
    lib.SherpaOnnxDestroyOfflineTtsGeneratedAudio.restype = None

    model = kdir / "model.int8.onnx" if (kdir / "model.int8.onnx").exists() else kdir / "model.onnx"
    cfg = TtsConfig()                       # ctypes fills every field with zero / NULL, like memset
    k = cfg.model.kokoro
    keep = [b(model), b(kdir / "voices.bin"), b(kdir / "tokens.txt"), b(kdir / "espeak-ng-data"),
            b(lexicon) if lexicon and lexicon != "-" else None, b"cpu"]
    k.model, k.voices, k.tokens, k.data_dir, k.lexicon = keep[0], keep[1], keep[2], keep[3], keep[4]
    k.length_scale = 1.0
    cfg.model.num_threads = threads
    cfg.model.debug = 0
    cfg.model.provider = keep[5]
    cfg.max_num_sentences = 1
    cfg.silence_scale = 0.2

    started = time.time()
    tts = lib.SherpaOnnxCreateOfflineTts(ctypes.byref(cfg))
    if not tts:
        say({"ready": False, "error": "The Kokoro voice could not be loaded."})
        return
    rate = lib.SherpaOnnxOfflineTtsSampleRate(tts)
    say({"ready": True, "sample_rate": rate, "load_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 2)})

    for line in sys.stdin:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            job = json.loads(line)
            t0 = time.time()
            audio = lib.SherpaOnnxOfflineTtsGenerate(tts, str(job["text"]).encode("utf-8"),
                                                     int(job.get("sid", 0)), float(job.get("speed", 1.0)))
            if not audio:
                say({"ok": False, "error": "Kokoro returned no audio."})
                continue
            a = audio.contents
            n, sr = a.n, a.sample_rate
            if n > 0:
                write_wav(job["out"], a.samples, n, sr)
            lib.SherpaOnnxDestroyOfflineTtsGeneratedAudio(audio)
            if n <= 0:
                say({"ok": False, "error": "Kokoro made empty audio."})
                continue
            say({"ok": True, "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 3), "audio_seconds": round(n / sr, 3)})
        except Exception as e:  # keep running for the next sentence
            say({"ok": False, "error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except Exception as e:
        say({"ready": False, "error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"})

16. app\voice.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\voice.py608 lines25,217 bytesSHA-256 9ef531c2a48f09b7…

Offline voice: speech-to-text with whisper.cpp, and reading answers aloud with Kokoro or Piper (Windows voices are the page's last resort).

  • Voice typing: the page records 16 kHz mono WAV, POST /api/voice/transcribe, whisper-cli with ggml-base.en.bin, one transcription at a time.
  • Reading aloud: tts_engine() picks Kokoro if you chose a Kokoro voice, otherwise Piper (fast) when installed, otherwise none (the page uses the Windows voice).
  • Piper runs as one long-lived process started with --output_dir: each line of text written to it produces a WAV file whose path Piper prints back. Voice files whose phoneme map groups two sounds are patched once for the 2023.11 Windows Piper (a backup .original is kept).
  • Kokoro uses kokoro_worker.py when sherpa-onnx-c-api.dll is present (model stays loaded), else sherpa-onnx-offline-tts.exe once per sentence (slow fallback). Only the English lexicon is loaded.
  • Kokoro is switched off automatically in Auto mode if it runs more than 2.5 times slower than real time twice.
  • 28 English Kokoro voices (KOKORO_VOICES); speaker 3 "Heart" is the default. Spoken audio is cached (120 entries).
  • unpack_downloads() unpacks whisper/piper zips and sherpa-onnx/kokoro .tar.bz2 archives dropped into the voice folder.

app\voice.py608 lines
"""voice.py - offline VOICE TYPING: turns your recorded speech into text.

It uses whisper.cpp (whisper-cli.exe) and a small Whisper speech model, both kept
in the QuantaKai\\voice folder. Nothing is sent to the internet.
Run VOICE_SETUP.bat once to download them (about 150 MB).

Reading answers ALOUD uses the best offline voice engine it finds:
  1. Kokoro (through sherpa-onnx) - very natural, bright young American female voices
  2. Piper - fast, decent quality
  3. the voices built into Windows (handled by the chat page) as a last resort
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import subprocess
import threading
import time
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

from .settings import CONFIG, DATA_DIR, ROOT, load_state, save_state, user_name

VOICE_DIR = ROOT / "voice"
TMP_DIR = DATA_DIR / "tmp"
NO_WINDOW = 0x08000000 if os.name == "nt" else 0   # don't flash a black window
_lock = threading.Lock()                           # one transcription at a time


def unpack_downloads() -> list[str]:
    """If you drop piper_windows_amd64.zip or whisper-bin-x64.zip into the voice folder,
    unpack them into the right place automatically (no manual unzipping needed)."""
    import zipfile
    done = []
    if not VOICE_DIR.exists():
        return done
    import tarfile
    for t in VOICE_DIR.glob("*.tar.bz2"):
        name = t.name.lower()
        if not (name.startswith("sherpa-onnx") or name.startswith("kokoro")):
            continue
        folder = VOICE_DIR / t.name[:-len(".tar.bz2")]
        if folder.exists():
            continue
        try:
            print(f"  voice: unpacking {t.name} (this can take a minute)...")
            with tarfile.open(t, "r:bz2") as f:
                f.extractall(VOICE_DIR, filter="data")
            done.append(t.name)
        except (tarfile.TarError, OSError, EOFError) as e:
            print(f"  voice: could not unpack {t.name} (incomplete download?): {e}")
            import shutil
            shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)   # don't leave a half-unpacked folder behind
            try:
                t.replace(t.with_name(t.name + ".broken"))   # so a good copy can be dropped in later
            except OSError:
                pass
    for z in VOICE_DIR.glob("*.zip"):
        name = z.name.lower()
        try:
            if name.startswith("piper"):
                if _find_piper():
                    continue
                with zipfile.ZipFile(z) as f:
                    f.extractall(VOICE_DIR / "piper")
                done.append("Piper voice engine")
            elif name.startswith("whisper-bin"):
                if _find_cli():
                    continue
                tmp = VOICE_DIR / "_unpack"
                with zipfile.ZipFile(z) as f:
                    f.extractall(tmp)
                cli = next(tmp.rglob("whisper-cli.exe"), None)
                if cli:
                    for item in cli.parent.iterdir():
                        target = VOICE_DIR / item.name
                        if item.is_file() and not target.exists():
                            item.replace(target)
                    done.append("whisper.cpp voice typing engine")
                import shutil
                shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
        except (zipfile.BadZipFile, OSError) as e:
            print(f"  voice: could not unpack {z.name}: {e}")
    for d in done:
        print(f"  voice: unpacked the {d}")
    return done


def _find_cli() -> Path | None:
    if not VOICE_DIR.exists():
        return None
    for name in ("whisper-cli.exe", "whisper-cli", "main.exe"):
        hit = next(VOICE_DIR.rglob(name), None)
        if hit:
            return hit
    return None


def _find_model() -> Path | None:
    if not VOICE_DIR.exists():
        return None
    wanted = CONFIG.get("voice_model", "ggml-base.en.bin")
    exact = VOICE_DIR / wanted
    if exact.is_file() and exact.stat().st_size > 10_000_000:
        return exact
    models = [p for p in VOICE_DIR.rglob("ggml-*.bin") if p.stat().st_size > 10_000_000]
    return min(models, key=lambda p: p.stat().st_size) if models else None


_unpacking = threading.Event()


def start_unpacking() -> None:
    """Unpack any dropped-in voice downloads in the background (big ones take a minute)."""
    def work():
        try:
            unpack_downloads()
        finally:
            _unpacking.set()
        if tts_engine():
            prewarm_preview()          # loads the reading voice now (Piper or Kokoro), so the first answer speaks quickly
    threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True).start()


KOKORO_STATS = {"calls": 0, "slow_calls": 0, "last_rtf": None, "disabled": False}


def _busy() -> bool:
    """True while books are being indexed: the processor is shared, so use the faster voice."""
    try:
        from . import library
        return bool(library.PROGRESS.get("running"))
    except Exception:
        return False
