def voice_choice() -> dict:
    """Your pick from the voice menu (saved in data/state.json), or the config defaults."""
    v = load_state().get("voice") or {}
    return {"engine": v.get("engine", str(CONFIG.get("tts_engine", "auto")).lower()),
            "kokoro_sid": int(v.get("kokoro_sid", CONFIG.get("tts_kokoro_voice", 3))),
            "piper_voice": v.get("piper_voice", CONFIG.get("tts_voice", "en_US-hfc_female-medium"))}


def set_voice(engine: str, voice_id=None) -> dict:
    engine = engine.lower()
    if engine not in ("auto", "kokoro", "piper", "windows"):
        raise ValueError("Unknown voice engine")
    v = voice_choice()
    v["engine"] = engine
    if engine == "kokoro" and voice_id is not None:
        v["kokoro_sid"] = int(voice_id)
        KOKORO_STATS.update(disabled=False, slow_calls=0)   # you chose it, so try it again
    if engine == "piper" and voice_id:
        v["piper_voice"] = str(voice_id)
    save_state(voice=v)
    return v


def _kokoro_group() -> str:
    if _find_c_api():
        return "Kokoro - most natural (loads in a few seconds the first time)"
    return "Kokoro - natural, but slow on this laptop"


def voice_list() -> dict:
    options = []
    if _find_sherpa() and _find_kokoro():
        options += [{"engine": "kokoro", "id": sid, "name": f"{name} - {desc}", "group": _kokoro_group()}
                    for sid, name, desc in KOKORO_VOICES]
    if _find_piper():
        for onnx in sorted(VOICE_DIR.glob("*.onnx")):
            if onnx.with_suffix(".onnx.json").exists():
                label = onnx.stem.replace("en_US-", "").replace("en_GB-", "UK ").replace("-medium", "").replace("_", " ")
                options.append({"engine": "piper", "id": onnx.stem, "name": label.title(), "group": "Piper - fastest"})
    options.append({"engine": "windows", "id": "windows", "name": "Windows built-in voice", "group": "Other"})
    return {"options": options, "choice": voice_choice(), "active": tts_engine(), "kokoro": dict(KOKORO_STATS)}


def tts_engine() -> str | None:
    pref = voice_choice()["engine"]
    if pref == "windows":
        return None
    kokoro_ok = _find_sherpa() and _find_kokoro()
    if pref == "kokoro" and kokoro_ok:
        return "kokoro"
    # Automatic = the FAST voice (Piper) when it is installed; Kokoro sounds more natural but is
    # several times slower on a 4-core laptop, so it is used only when you pick a Kokoro voice
    if pref == "auto" and not (_find_piper() and _find_voice()) and kokoro_ok \
            and not KOKORO_STATS["disabled"] and not _busy():
        return "kokoro"
    if pref in ("auto", "piper", "kokoro") and _find_piper() and _find_voice():
        return "piper"
    return None


def status() -> dict:
    cli, model = _find_cli(), _find_model()
    engine = tts_engine()
    tts = engine is not None
    if cli and model:
        return {"available": True, "model": model.name, "tts": tts, "tts_engine": engine,
                "kokoro": dict(KOKORO_STATS, fast=bool(_find_c_api()), loaded=_KOKORO.proc is not None)}
    missing = "the speech engine" if not cli else "the speech model"
    return {"available": False, "tts": tts, "tts_engine": engine,
            "reason": f"Voice typing is not set up yet ({missing} is missing). "
                      "Double-click VOICE_SETUP.bat once (needs internet, about 230 MB)."}


# =========================================================================== speaking (Piper)
PIPER_DIR = VOICE_DIR / "piper"
_tts_lock = threading.Lock()
_tts_proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None


def _find_piper() -> Path | None:
    return next(PIPER_DIR.rglob("piper.exe"), None) if PIPER_DIR.exists() else None


def _make_compatible(cfg: Path) -> None:
    """Newer Piper voices group some vowel pairs (like "aɪ") under one id. The Piper
    engine for Windows (2023.11) only accepts single sounds and refuses to start, so
    drop those grouped entries; the engine then sends the two sounds separately."""
    import json
    try:
        data = json.loads(cfg.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        ids = data.get("phoneme_id_map", {})
        multi = [k for k in ids if len(k) != 1]
        if not multi:
            return
        backup = cfg.with_name(cfg.name + ".original")
        if not backup.exists():
            backup.write_text(cfg.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8")
        for k in multi:
            del ids[k]
        data.pop("vowel_clusters", None)
        cfg.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"  voice: made {cfg.name} work with this Piper engine")
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        print(f"  voice: could not check {cfg.name}: {e}")


def _find_voice() -> Path | None:
    if not VOICE_DIR.exists():
        return None
    wanted = VOICE_DIR / (voice_choice()["piper_voice"] + ".onnx")
    if wanted.is_file() and wanted.with_suffix(".onnx.json").is_file():
        _make_compatible(wanted.with_suffix(".onnx.json"))
        return wanted
    return next((p for p in VOICE_DIR.glob("*.onnx") if p.with_suffix(".onnx.json").is_file()), None)


def _piper_args() -> list[str]:
    # length_scale < 1 = faster speech (0.88 is about 165 words a minute for this voice);
    # noise settings add natural, lively pitch movement; short pauses keep it snappy.
    return ["--length_scale", str(CONFIG.get("tts_speed", 0.88)),
            "--noise_scale", str(CONFIG.get("tts_expression", 0.72)),
            "--noise_w", str(CONFIG.get("tts_rhythm", 0.85)),
            "--sentence_silence", str(CONFIG.get("tts_pause", 0.12))]


def _start_piper() -> subprocess.Popen:
    """Keep one Piper running with the voice loaded, so each sentence starts fast."""
    out_dir = TMP_DIR / "tts"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    piper, voice = _find_piper(), _find_voice()
    return subprocess.Popen([str(piper), "--model", str(voice), "--output_dir", str(out_dir)] + _piper_args(),
                            stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                            cwd=str(piper.parent), creationflags=NO_WINDOW)


def _speak_oneshot(text: str) -> bytes:
    piper, voice = _find_piper(), _find_voice()
    TMP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out = TMP_DIR / f"tts-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.wav"
    try:
        subprocess.run([str(piper), "--model", str(voice), "--output_file", str(out)] + _piper_args(),
                       input=text.encode("utf-8"), capture_output=True, timeout=60,
                       cwd=str(piper.parent), creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
        return out.read_bytes()
    finally:
        try:
            out.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass


# =========================================================================== speaking (Kokoro)
def _find_sherpa() -> Path | None:
    return next(VOICE_DIR.rglob("sherpa-onnx-offline-tts.exe"), None) if VOICE_DIR.exists() else None


def _find_kokoro() -> Path | None:
    """The unpacked Kokoro folder (it holds model.onnx or model.int8.onnx, voices.bin, tokens.txt)."""
    if not VOICE_DIR.exists():
        return None
    for voices in VOICE_DIR.rglob("voices.bin"):
        d = voices.parent
        if (d / "tokens.txt").exists() and ((d / "model.int8.onnx").exists() or (d / "model.onnx").exists()):
            return d
    return None


def _find_c_api() -> Path | None:
    """sherpa-onnx-c-api.dll from the sherpa-onnx download (lets Kokoro stay loaded in memory)."""
    if not VOICE_DIR.exists() or os.name != "nt":
        return None
    return next(VOICE_DIR.rglob("sherpa-onnx-c-api.dll"), None)


class _KokoroWorker:
    """A helper program that keeps the Kokoro model loaded, so each sentence takes a fraction
    of a second instead of reloading 114 MB every time. If it fails, the old way is used."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None
        self.lexicon = None
        self.failures = 0
        self.load_seconds = None

    def _read(self, timeout: float) -> dict:
        proc = self.proc
        timer = threading.Timer(timeout, lambda: proc.kill() if proc.poll() is None else None)
        timer.start()
        try:
            while True:
                line = proc.stdout.readline()
                if not line:
                    raise RuntimeError("the Kokoro helper stopped")
                line = line.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
                if line.startswith("@@"):
                    import json
                    return json.loads(line[2:])
        finally:
            timer.cancel()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        if self.proc and self.proc.poll() is None:
            self.proc.kill()
        self.proc = None

    def _start(self, lexicon: str) -> None:
        import sys
        self.stop()
        dll, d = _find_c_api(), _find_kokoro()
        cmd = [sys.executable, "-I", str(Path(__file__).with_name("kokoro_worker.py")), str(dll.parent), str(d),
               lexicon or "-", str(CONFIG.get("threads", 4))]
        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                                     stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, cwd=str(dll.parent), creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
        self.lexicon = lexicon
        reply = self._read(120)
        if not reply.get("ready"):
            self.stop()
            raise RuntimeError(reply.get("error") or "the Kokoro helper did not start")
        self.load_seconds = reply.get("load_seconds")
        print(f"  voice: Kokoro loaded and kept in memory ({self.load_seconds} s)")

    def speak(self, text: str, sid: int, lexicon: str, speed: float) -> tuple[bytes, float]:
        """Returns (wav bytes, seconds spent making it)."""
        import json
        if self.proc is None or self.proc.poll() is not None or lexicon != self.lexicon:
            self._start(lexicon)
        TMP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out = TMP_DIR / f"tts-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.wav"
        try:
            self.proc.stdin.write((json.dumps({"text": text, "sid": sid, "speed": speed, "out": str(out)}) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
            self.proc.stdin.flush()
            reply = self._read(max(30.0, len(text) * 0.25))
            if not reply.get("ok") or not out.exists():
                raise RuntimeError(reply.get("error") or "no audio")
            return out.read_bytes(), float(reply.get("seconds", 0))
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            self.stop()
            raise RuntimeError(f"the Kokoro helper failed ({e})")
        finally:
            try:
                out.unlink()
            except OSError:
                pass


_KOKORO = _KokoroWorker()


def _kokoro_lexicon() -> str:
    d = _find_kokoro()
    british = 20 <= voice_choice()["kokoro_sid"] <= 27
    p = d / ("lexicon-gb-en.txt" if british else "lexicon-us-en.txt")
    return str(p) if p.exists() else ""


def _speak_kokoro_fast(text: str) -> tuple[bytes, float] | None:
    """Try the kept-in-memory Kokoro. None means 'use the old way'."""
    if not _find_c_api() or _KOKORO.failures >= 3 or CONFIG.get("tts_kokoro_fast", True) is False:
        return None
    scale = float(CONFIG.get("tts_kokoro_length_scale") or 1.0)
    try:
        result = _KOKORO.speak(text, voice_choice()["kokoro_sid"], _kokoro_lexicon(), 1.0 / max(0.3, scale))
        _KOKORO.failures = 0
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        _KOKORO.failures += 1
        print(f"  voice: fast Kokoro failed ({e}); using the slower way for now")
        return None


def _speak_kokoro(text: str) -> bytes:
    exe, d = _find_sherpa(), _find_kokoro()
    model = d / "model.int8.onnx" if (d / "model.int8.onnx").exists() else d / "model.onnx"
    # English only: the Chinese word lists in the multi-language pack are huge and were being
    # loaded for every sentence, which made speech many times slower than it needs to be
    british = 20 <= voice_choice()["kokoro_sid"] <= 27
    lexicons = ",".join(str(p) for p in (d / ("lexicon-gb-en.txt" if british else "lexicon-us-en.txt"),) if p.exists())
    TMP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out = TMP_DIR / f"tts-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.wav"
    cmd = [str(exe), f"--kokoro-model={model}", f"--kokoro-voices={d / 'voices.bin'}",
           f"--kokoro-tokens={d / 'tokens.txt'}", f"--kokoro-data-dir={d / 'espeak-ng-data'}",
           f"--num-threads={CONFIG.get('threads', 4)}",
           f"--sid={voice_choice()['kokoro_sid']}", f"--output-filename={out}"]
    if lexicons:
        cmd.append(f"--kokoro-lexicon={lexicons}")
    if CONFIG.get("tts_kokoro_chinese") and (d / "dict").is_dir():
        cmd.append(f"--kokoro-dict-dir={d / 'dict'}")
        zh = d / "lexicon-zh.txt"
        if zh.exists():
            cmd = [c + "," + str(zh) if c.startswith("--kokoro-lexicon=") else c for c in cmd]
    if CONFIG.get("tts_kokoro_length_scale"):
        cmd.append(f"--kokoro-length-scale={CONFIG['tts_kokoro_length_scale']}")
    cmd.append(text)
    try:
        run = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, timeout=120, cwd=str(exe.parent), creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
        if not out.exists():
            err = run.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip().splitlines()[-3:]
            raise RuntimeError("Kokoro voice failed: " + " | ".join(err))
        return out.read_bytes()
    finally:
        try:
            out.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass


_CACHE: "dict[tuple, bytes]" = {}
_CACHE_ORDER: list[tuple] = []


def _voice_key(engine: str) -> str:
    c = voice_choice()
    return str(c["kokoro_sid"]) if engine == "kokoro" else c["piper_voice"]


def preview_text() -> str:
    c, engine = voice_choice(), tts_engine()
    if engine == "kokoro":
        name = next((n for sid, n, _ in KOKORO_VOICES if sid == c["kokoro_sid"]), "Kokoro")
    else:
        name = "your fast voice"
    return f"Hi {user_name()}! This is {name}. Here's how I'll read your answers."


def speak(text: str) -> bytes:
    """Turn a piece of text into WAV audio. Anything already spoken (previews, re-reads)
    comes from a small memory cache instantly."""
    engine = tts_engine()
    if engine:
        key = (engine, _voice_key(engine), re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip())
        if key in _CACHE:
            return _CACHE[key]
        audio = _speak_now(text)
        _CACHE[key] = audio
        _CACHE_ORDER.append(key)
        while len(_CACHE_ORDER) > 120:
            _CACHE.pop(_CACHE_ORDER.pop(0), None)
        return audio
    return _speak_now(text)


def prewarm_preview() -> None:
    """Make the preview for the voice you just picked in the background, so Preview is instant."""
    def work():
        try:
            speak(preview_text())
        except Exception:
            pass
    threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True).start()


def _speak_now(text: str) -> bytes:
    global _tts_proc
    engine = tts_engine()
    if not engine:
        raise RuntimeError("The offline voice is not installed. Run VOICE_SETUP.bat once.")
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()[:1500]
    if not text:
        raise RuntimeError("Nothing to say.")
    if engine == "kokoro":
        with _tts_lock:
            started = time.time()
            fast = _speak_kokoro_fast(text)
            if fast:
                audio, took = fast            # time spent speaking only, not the one-off loading
            else:
                audio = _speak_kokoro(text)
                took = time.time() - started
        seconds = max(0.1, (len(audio) - 44) / 48000)          # 24 kHz, 16-bit mono
        rtf = took / seconds                                     # 1.0 = as fast as it talks
        KOKORO_STATS["calls"] += 1
        KOKORO_STATS["last_rtf"] = round(rtf, 2)
        if rtf > float(CONFIG.get("tts_kokoro_max_slowness", 2.5)):
            KOKORO_STATS["slow_calls"] += 1
            if KOKORO_STATS["slow_calls"] >= 2 and voice_choice()["engine"] == "auto":
                KOKORO_STATS["disabled"] = True                  # too slow on this laptop: use Piper
                print(f"  voice: Kokoro is too slow here ({rtf:.1f}x slower than speech) - using Piper")
        print(f"  voice: Kokoro made {seconds:.1f} s of speech in {took:.1f} s")
        return audio
    with _tts_lock:
        for _ in range(2):   # if the running Piper has stopped, start it again once
            try:
                if _tts_proc is None or _tts_proc.poll() is not None:
                    _tts_proc = _start_piper()
                _tts_proc.stdin.write((text + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
                _tts_proc.stdin.flush()
                wav_path = Path(_tts_proc.stdout.readline().decode("utf-8", "replace").strip())
                if wav_path.is_file():
                    data = wav_path.read_bytes()
                    try:
                        wav_path.unlink()
                    except OSError:
                        pass
                    if data[:4] == b"RIFF":
                        return data
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                pass
            if _tts_proc and _tts_proc.poll() is None:
                _tts_proc.kill()
            _tts_proc = None
        return _speak_oneshot(text)   # last resort: one Piper run just for this text


def _clean(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"\[[^\]]*\]|\([^)]*(music|noise|silence|blank)[^)]*\)", " ", text, flags=re.I)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()


def transcribe(wav: bytes) -> str:
    """wav = 16 kHz mono 16-bit WAV recorded by the chat page. Returns the words spoken."""
    cli, model = _find_cli(), _find_model()
    if not (cli and model):
        raise RuntimeError(status()["reason"])
    if len(wav) < 1000 or wav[:4] != b"RIFF":
        raise RuntimeError("The recording was empty. Hold the microphone button a little longer.")
    TMP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = TMP_DIR / f"voice-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.wav"
    path.write_bytes(wav)
    cmd = [str(cli), "-m", str(model), "-f", str(path), "-nt", "-np",
           "-t", str(CONFIG.get("threads", 4)),
           "-l", CONFIG.get("voice_language", "en")]
    try:
        with _lock:
            started = time.time()
            run = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, timeout=180, cwd=str(cli.parent),
                                 creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
        if run.returncode != 0:
            err = run.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip().splitlines()[-1:] or ["unknown error"]
            raise RuntimeError(f"Voice typing failed: {err[0]}")
        text = _clean(run.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
        print(f"  voice: {len(wav) // 32000} s of audio -> text in {time.time() - started:.1f} s")
        return text
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        raise RuntimeError("Voice typing took too long. Try a shorter recording.")
    finally:
        try:
            path.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass

17. app\chat.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\chat.py323 lines16,171 bytesSHA-256 674c4f1f84c93581…

Builds every reply: loads the chat, gathers memories, book passages, web extracts or attached documents, fits everything into the context window, streams, saves.

  • Modes: chat, study (shown as Offline), online, create. Styles: explain (Detailed, default), quick, code, teach, quiz; Create uses story or character.
  • Token budget: about 3.5 characters per token; old messages are dropped from the start until system + history + question + answer fit in context_window.
  • Short follow-ups (under 6 words) are searched together with the previous question. "Simpler please" reuses the last answer's sources.
  • Voice mode: Chat, Detailed and Quick answers are capped at 220 tokens and get VOICE_NOTE; Code, Teach, Quiz and Create are never shortened.
  • Pictures: text is read with ocr.py and added to the question; a vision model also gets the image.
  • Every question and its answer share a turn tag, so Retry, Resend and Edit can rewind the chat to that point.
  • GENERATE_LOCK: one reply at a time. Stop keeps what was written. Auto-learn runs after the reply, waiting for the lock.
  • Yields events: meta, status, notice, ocr, memory, sources, context, token, done, error.

app\chat.py323 lines
"""chat.py - puts each reply together.

For every message it:
  1. loads the conversation so far (data\\chats\\<id>.json)
  2. CHAT mode : finds relevant memories and adds them to the instructions
     STUDY mode: searches your books and hands the best passages to the model
  3. trims old messages so everything fits in the model's 4096-token window
  4. streams the reply back piece by piece, then saves the conversation
  5. (chat mode, auto-learn on) asks the model which new facts to remember
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import threading
import time
import uuid
from typing import Iterator

from . import docs, engine, library, llm, memory, ocr, web
from .prompts import (CHAT_SYSTEM, MEMORY_BLOCK, NO_PASSAGES, SIMPLER_REQUEST,
                      STUDY_CODE, STUDY_EXPLAIN, STUDY_QUICK, STUDY_QUIZ, STUDY_TEACH, NO_WEB, TYPO_NOTE, WEB_CODE, WEB_EXPLAIN,
                      WEB_QUICK, WEB_QUIZ, WEB_TEACH, VOICE_NOTE, STORY_RULES, CHARACTER_RULES)
from .settings import CHATS_DIR, CONFIG, load_state, user_name, write_json_atomic

GENERATE_LOCK = threading.Lock()   # one reply at a time; others wait their turn
CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 3.5              # rough rule of thumb for English text


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- storage
def _path(conv_id: str):
    safe = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]", "", conv_id)
    return CHATS_DIR / f"{safe}.json"


def load_conversation(conv_id: str | None) -> dict:
    if conv_id:
        try:
            conv = json.loads(_path(conv_id).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            # every question and its answer share a 'turn' tag, so Retry and Edit know what to replace.
            # Older chats get a tag from their position (the same every time they are loaded).
            turn = None
            for i, m in enumerate(conv.get("messages", [])):
                if m.get("role") == "user":
                    turn = m.setdefault("turn", f"t{i}")
                elif turn and "turn" not in m:
                    m["turn"] = turn
            return conv
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            pass
    now = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
    return {"id": time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-") + uuid.uuid4().hex[:4],
            "title": "New chat", "created": now, "updated": now, "messages": []}


def save_conversation(conv: dict) -> None:
    conv["updated"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
    write_json_atomic(_path(conv["id"]), conv)


def list_conversations() -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for p in CHATS_DIR.glob("*.json"):
        try:
            c = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            out.append({"id": c["id"], "title": c["title"], "updated": c["updated"]})
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
            continue
    return sorted(out, key=lambda c: c["updated"], reverse=True)


def delete_conversation(conv_id: str) -> bool:
    p = _path(conv_id)
    docs.delete_all(conv_id)          # its attached documents go too
    if p.exists():
        p.unlink()
        return True
    return False


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- prompt building
def _tokens(text: str) -> int:
    return int(len(text) / CHARS_PER_TOKEN) + 4


def _fit_history(history: list[dict], budget: int, max_turns: int) -> list[dict]:
    """Keep the newest messages that fit in the token budget."""
    kept: list[dict] = []
    for m in reversed(history[-max_turns * 2:]):
        cost = _tokens(m["content"])
        if cost > budget:
            break
        kept.insert(0, {"role": m["role"], "content": m["content"]})
        budget -= cost
    return kept


def _format_passages(hits: list[dict]) -> str:
    if not hits:
        return NO_PASSAGES
    parts = []
    for n, h in enumerate(hits, start=1):
        pages = f"p. {h['page']}" if h["page"] == h["page_end"] else f"pp. {h['page']}-{h['page_end']}"
        parts.append(f"[{n}] ({h['title']}, {pages})\n{h['text']}")
    return "\n\n".join(parts)


def _search_query(message: str, history: list[dict]) -> str:
    """Short follow-ups ("why?", "give an example") are searched together with the last question."""
    if len(message.split()) < 6:
        previous = [m["content"] for m in history if m["role"] == "user"]
        if previous:
            return previous[-1] + " " + message
    return message


def _public_source(n: int, h: dict) -> dict:
    return {"n": n, "title": h["title"], "page": h["page"], "page_end": h["page_end"],
            "score": h["score"], "text": h["text"], "category": h.get("category", "")}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- one turn
def reply(conv_id: str | None, mode: str, style: str, message: str,
          simpler: bool = False, book_category: str = "auto",
          images: list[str] | None = None, turn: str | None = None,
          rewind: str | None = None, voice: bool = False) -> Iterator[dict]:
    """Yield events for the web page: meta, sources, token..., done (or error).
    rewind = the tag of an earlier question: it and everything after it are removed first
    (used by Retry, Resend and Edit, so the old answer doesn't stay in the conversation)."""
    conv = load_conversation(conv_id)
    history = conv["messages"]
    if rewind:
        cut = next((i for i, m in enumerate(history) if m.get("turn") == rewind), None)
        if cut is not None:
            del history[cut:]
            save_conversation(conv)
    turn = re.sub(r"[^\w-]", "", turn or "")[:40] or uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]
    names = {"name": CONFIG["app_name"], "user": user_name()}   # your nickname, if you set one
    mode = mode if mode in ("study", "online", "create") else "chat"   # study = Offline (books)
    if mode == "create":
        style = "character" if style == "character" else "story"
    else:
        style = style if style in ("quick", "code", "teach", "quiz") else "explain"   # detailed by default
    if simpler:
        message = SIMPLER_REQUEST
    message = message.strip()
    images = [i for i in (images or []) if i.startswith("data:image/")]
    typed = message
    picture_note = ""
    if images:
        texts = []
        for n, img in enumerate(images, start=1):
            yield {"type": "status", "text": f"Reading picture {n} of {len(images)}..."}
            try:
                found = ocr.read_text(img)
            except (RuntimeError, ValueError) as e:
                found = ""
                yield {"type": "notice", "text": str(e)}
            label = f"Picture {n}" if len(images) > 1 else "The picture"
            texts.append(f"[{label} - text read from it]\n{found}" if found else f"[{label} - no readable text found]")
        picture_note = "\n\n".join(texts)
        yield {"type": "ocr", "text": picture_note}
        if not message:
            message = "Answer the question in the picture. Show your working step by step."
        message = message + "\n\n" + picture_note
    if not message:
        yield {"type": "error", "message": "Type a message first."}
        return
    if conv["title"] == "New chat":
        conv["title"] = (message[:48] + "...") if len(message) > 48 else message

    yield {"type": "meta", "conv_id": conv["id"], "title": conv["title"]}

    remembered = None
    if mode == "chat":
        explicit = memory.explicit_request(message)
        if explicit:
            remembered = memory.add(explicit, source="manual")
            yield {"type": "memory", "saved": [explicit] if remembered else [],
                   "note": "Saved to memory." if remembered else "I already knew that."}

    window = int(CONFIG["context_window"])
    caps = CONFIG["max_answer_tokens"]
    max_tokens = int(caps.get("chat" if mode == "chat" else style, caps.get("explain", 800)))
    # Voice mode: a reply that will be read aloud is kept short, so you hear it in seconds, not minutes
    spoken = voice and (mode == "chat" or style in ("explain", "quick"))
    if spoken:
        max_tokens = min(max_tokens, int(caps.get("voice", 220)))
    sources: list[dict] = []

    try:
        if mode == "chat":
            facts = memory.relevant(message)
            block = MEMORY_BLOCK.format(user=names["user"], facts="\n".join(f"- {f}" for f in facts)) if facts else ""
            system = CHAT_SYSTEM.format(memory_block=block, **names)
            budget = window - max_tokens - _tokens(system) - _tokens(message) - 64
            messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system + TYPO_NOTE.format(**names)}]
            messages += _fit_history(history, budget, max_turns=12)
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": message})
            temperature = float(CONFIG["temperature"]["chat"])
        elif mode == "create":
            # story writing and character creation: no books, no web, more imagination
            system = (CHARACTER_RULES if style == "character" else STORY_RULES).format(**names)
            budget = window - max_tokens - _tokens(system) - _tokens(message) - 64
            messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system + TYPO_NOTE.format(**names)}]
            messages += _fit_history(history, max(0, budget), max_turns=10)
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": message})
            temperature = float(CONFIG["temperature"].get("create", 0.9))
        elif mode == "online":
            last = next((m for m in reversed(history) if m["role"] == "assistant" and m.get("sources")), None)
            if simpler and last:
                found = last["sources"]
            else:
                yield {"type": "status", "text": "Searching the web..."}
                try:
                    found = web.gather(_search_query(message, history))
                except web.WebError as e:
                    yield {"type": "error", "message": str(e)}
                    return
                found = [{"n": n, "title": f["title"], "url": f["url"], "text": f["text"]}
                         for n, f in enumerate(found, start=1)]
            sources = found
            yield {"type": "sources", "sources": sources}
            extracts = "\n\n".join(f"[{s['n']}] ({s['title']})\n{s['text']}" for s in sources) or NO_WEB
            system = {"explain": WEB_EXPLAIN, "code": WEB_CODE, "teach": WEB_TEACH, "quiz": WEB_QUIZ}.get(style, WEB_QUICK).format(**names)
            content = f"Web extracts:\n{extracts}\n\nQuestion: {message}"
            budget = window - max_tokens - _tokens(system) - _tokens(content) - 64
            messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system + TYPO_NOTE.format(**names)}]
            messages += _fit_history(history, max(0, budget), max_turns=4 if style == "teach" else 2)
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": content})
            temperature = float(CONFIG["temperature"]["study"])
        else:
            if simpler:
                last = next((m for m in reversed(history) if m["role"] == "assistant" and m.get("sources")), None)
                hits = [dict(s, book_id="") for s in (last or {}).get("sources", [])]
            else:
                hits = library.INDEX.search(_search_query(message, history), int(CONFIG["study_top_k"]),
                                            category=book_category)
                hits = [h for h in hits if h["score"] >= float(CONFIG["study_min_score"])
                        or h.get("keyword", 0) >= 0.6]   # strong exact-term match counts too
            sources = [_public_source(n, h) for n, h in enumerate(hits, start=1)]
            fixes = {} if simpler else dict(library.INDEX.last_corrections)
            yield {"type": "sources", "sources": sources,
                   "categories": [] if simpler else list(library.INDEX.last_categories),
                   "corrections": fixes}
            system = {"explain": STUDY_EXPLAIN, "code": STUDY_CODE, "teach": STUDY_TEACH, "quiz": STUDY_QUIZ}.get(style, STUDY_QUICK).format(**names)
            content = f"Book passages:\n{_format_passages(hits)}\n\nQuestion: {message}"
            if fixes:
                content += "\n(Probable spelling fixes: " + ", ".join(f"{k} -> {v}" for k, v in fixes.items()) + ")"
            budget = window - max_tokens - _tokens(system) - _tokens(content) - 64
            messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system + TYPO_NOTE.format(**names)}]
            messages += _fit_history(history, max(0, budget), max_turns=4 if style == "teach" else 2)
            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": content})
            temperature = float(CONFIG["temperature"]["study"])
    except llm.EngineNotReady as e:
        yield {"type": "error", "message": str(e)}
        return

    if spoken:
        messages[0] = {"role": "system", "content": messages[0]["content"] + VOICE_NOTE.format(**names)}

    # documents attached to this chat: add their most relevant parts, then trim older history to fit
    if conv.get("docs"):
        fixed = _tokens(messages[0]["content"]) + _tokens(messages[-1]["content"])
        room = window - max_tokens - fixed - 64
        block, used = docs.context_block(conv, _search_query(typed or message, history), max(0, min(1500, int(room * 0.75))))
        if block:
            middle = messages[1:-1]
            messages = ([{"role": "system", "content": messages[0]["content"] + "\n\n" + block}]
                        + _fit_history(middle, max(0, room - _tokens(block)), max_turns=len(middle) or 1)
                        + [messages[-1]])
            yield {"type": "context", "names": used}

    pieces: list[str] = []
    stats: dict = {}
    started = time.time()
    stopped_early = False
    with GENERATE_LOCK:
        if images and getattr(engine.CHAT, 'vision', False) and messages and messages[-1]['role'] == 'user':
            # this model can see pictures: send the picture itself along with the text read from it
            messages[-1] = {'role': 'user', 'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': messages[-1]['content']}] +
                            [{'type': 'image_url', 'image_url': {'url': i}} for i in images]}
        stream = llm.chat_stream(messages, max_tokens, temperature)
        try:
            for event in stream:
                if "text" in event:
                    pieces.append(event["text"])
                    yield {"type": "token", "text": event["text"]}
                else:
                    stats = event["stats"]
        except llm.EngineNotReady as e:
            yield {"type": "error", "message": str(e)}
            return
        except GeneratorExit:      # you pressed Stop: keep what was written so far
            stopped_early = True
        except Exception as e:
            yield {"type": "error", "message": f"The engine stopped answering: {e}"}
            return
        finally:
            stream.close()   # closing the connection makes the engine stop writing
            text = "".join(pieces).strip()
            if text:
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": message, "mode": mode, "style": style, "turn": turn,
                                "display": "Explain that more simply" if simpler
                                else (typed or "Answer the question in the picture") + (" (with picture)" if images else "")})
                history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": text, "mode": mode, "style": style, "turn": turn,
                                "sources": sources,
                                "stats": {"tokens_per_second": round(stats.get("predicted_per_second", 0), 1),
                                          "seconds": round(time.time() - started, 1)}})
                save_conversation(conv)
    if stopped_early:
        return

    yield {"type": "done", "stats": history[-1]["stats"] if pieces else {}}

    auto = load_state().get("auto_learn", CONFIG["auto_learn"])
    if mode == "chat" and auto and not remembered and pieces:
        threading.Thread(target=_learn_later, args=(message, "".join(pieces)), daemon=True).start()


def _learn_later(message: str, text: str) -> None:
    with GENERATE_LOCK:   # wait until no reply is being written
        memory.learn_from_turn(message, text)

18. app\server.py

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\app\server.py390 lines17,331 bytesSHA-256 f9b7a1bf3b2adda1…

The local web server: serves the page and the JSON API, starts the engines, opens the browser.

  • Python's built-in ThreadingHTTPServer, bound to 127.0.0.1 only (not reachable from Wi-Fi).
  • Host check against DNS rebinding (only 127.0.0.1:8000 and localhost:8000), and Origin check against cross-site requests on every POST, PUT and DELETE.
  • /api/chat answers as Server-Sent Events: one data: {json} line per event.
  • Wallpaper upload: base64 data URL, 15 MB max, type checked by magic bytes (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP), saved as data\wallpaper.<ext>.
  • On start: kill leftovers, register the Job, tidy the inbox, unpack voice downloads, start the search engine, load the index, load the last model.
  • Every endpoint is listed in the HTTP API section of this guide.

app\server.py390 lines
"""server.py - the local web server you chat through.

Start it with:   runtime\\python\\python.exe -m app.server   (START.bat does this)
It serves the chat page (ui\\index.html) at http://127.0.0.1:8000 and a small
JSON API the page uses. Only this computer can reach it (it listens on
127.0.0.1, not on your Wi-Fi network).

On start-up it also launches the search engine and your last-used chat model.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import atexit
import base64
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import webbrowser
from http import HTTPStatus
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from . import chat, docs, engine, library, memory, organize, runner, voice
from .settings import BOOKS_DIR, CONFIG, DATA_DIR, MODELS_DIR, UI_DIR, load_state, save_state, user_name

PORT = int(CONFIG["ui_port"])
ALLOWED_HOSTS = {f"127.0.0.1:{PORT}", f"localhost:{PORT}"}
ALLOWED_ORIGINS = {f"http://{h}" for h in ALLOWED_HOSTS}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ wallpaper
WALL_TYPES = {b"\x89PNG": ("png", "image/png"), b"\xff\xd8\xff": ("jpg", "image/jpeg"),
              b"GIF8": ("gif", "image/gif"), b"RIFF": ("webp", "image/webp")}
MAX_WALL_BYTES = 15_000_000


def _wall_file():
    return next((p for p in DATA_DIR.glob("wallpaper.*") if p.suffix in (".png", ".jpg", ".gif", ".webp")), None)


def _save_wall(data_url: str) -> str:
    raw = base64.b64decode(data_url.split(",", 1)[-1], validate=False)
    if len(raw) > MAX_WALL_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("That picture is too big (15 MB at most).")
    kind = next((v for k, v in WALL_TYPES.items() if raw.startswith(k)), None)
    if not kind or (kind[0] == "webp" and raw[8:12] != b"WEBP"):
        raise ValueError("Use a PNG, JPG, GIF or WebP picture.")
    old = _wall_file()
    if old:
        old.unlink()
    (DATA_DIR / f"wallpaper.{kind[0]}").write_bytes(raw)
    return kind[0]


def _open_folder(path) -> None:
    if os.name == "nt":
        os.startfile(str(path))  # opens File Explorer
    elif sys.platform == "darwin":
        subprocess.Popen(["open", str(path)])
    else:
        subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", str(path)])


def status() -> dict:
    books = library.load_manifest()
    return {
        "app_name": CONFIG["app_name"],
        "user_name": user_name(),                     # your nickname, if you set one
        "nickname": str(load_state().get("nickname") or ""),
        "config_name": CONFIG["user_name"],
        "wallpaper": int(_wall_file().stat().st_mtime) if _wall_file() else 0,
        "wall_dim": load_state().get("wall_dim", 35),
        "wall_blur": load_state().get("wall_blur", 0),
        "chat": {"state": engine.CHAT.state, "model": engine.CHAT.model, "error": engine.CHAT.error},
        "search": {"state": engine.EMBED.state, "error": engine.EMBED.error},
        "books": len(books),
        "passages": sum(b.get("passages", 0) for b in books.values()),
        "memories": len(memory.list_all()),
        "auto_learn": load_state().get("auto_learn", CONFIG["auto_learn"]),
        "max_model_gb": CONFIG["max_model_gb"],
        "voice": voice.status(),
        "run": runner.languages(),
        "images": {"ocr": os.name == "nt", "vision": bool(getattr(engine.CHAT, "vision", False))},
    }


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server_version = "OfflineChatbot/1.0"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ plumbing
    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # keep the console quiet
        pass

    def _host_ok(self) -> bool:
        # Blocks "DNS rebinding": a web page on the internet pretending to be localhost.
        if self.headers.get("Host", "") not in ALLOWED_HOSTS:
            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Wrong host")
            return False
        return True

    def _origin_ok(self) -> bool:
        # Blocks "cross-site request forgery": any website you visit could otherwise send
        # requests to this app in the background (for example to /api/run, which runs code).
        # Browsers always say which site a request comes from in the Origin header.
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        if origin and origin not in ALLOWED_ORIGINS:
            self.send_error(HTTPStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Requests from other websites are not allowed")
            return False
        return True

    def _json(self, data, status_code: int = 200) -> None:
        body = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status_code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _body(self) -> dict:
        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
        if length <= 0:
            return {}
        try:
            return json.loads(self.rfile.read(min(length, 32_000_000)).decode("utf-8"))   # room for pictures
        except ValueError:
            return {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ GET
    def do_GET(self):
        if not self._host_ok():
            return
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            body = (UI_DIR / "index.html").read_bytes()
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        elif path == "/api/status":
            self._json(status())
        elif path == "/api/wallpaper":
            f = _wall_file()
            if not f:
                self.send_error(404)
                return
            body = f.read_bytes()
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", next(v[1] for v in WALL_TYPES.values() if v[0] == f.suffix[1:]))
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        elif path == "/api/models":
            self._json({"models": engine.list_models(), "active": engine.CHAT.model,
                        "state": engine.CHAT.state, "max_gb": CONFIG["max_model_gb"]})
        elif path == "/api/books":
            self._json({"books": library.list_books(), "progress": library.PROGRESS,
                        "categories": library.all_categories(),
                        "category_groups": library.category_groups(), "queue": list(library.QUEUE)})
        elif path == "/api/memory":
            self._json({"memories": list(reversed(memory.list_all()))})
        elif path == "/api/voices":
            self._json(voice.voice_list())
        elif path == "/api/chats":
            self._json({"chats": chat.list_conversations()})
        elif m := re.fullmatch(r"/api/chats/([\w-]+)", path):
            conv = chat.load_conversation(m.group(1))
            found = conv["messages"] or conv.get("docs")
            self._json(conv if found else {"error": "not found"}, 200 if found else 404)
        else:
            self.send_error(404)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ POST
    def do_POST(self):
        if not (self._host_ok() and self._origin_ok()):
            return
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path == "/api/voice/transcribe":      # raw WAV audio, not JSON
            self._transcribe()
            return
        data = self._body()
        if path == "/api/chat":
            self._stream_reply(data)
        elif path == "/api/docs":                     # attach a document to a chat
            conv = chat.load_conversation(str(data.get("conv_id") or "") or None)
            try:
                doc = docs.add(conv, str(data.get("name", "document.txt")), docs.decode_upload(str(data.get("data", ""))))
            except docs.DocError as e:
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}, 200)
                return
            except Exception as e:                    # an unusual file the readers choked on
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": f"Could not read that file ({type(e).__name__})."}, 200)
                return
            chat.save_conversation(conv)
            self._json({"ok": True, "conv_id": conv["id"], "doc": doc, "docs": conv["docs"]})
        elif path == "/api/models/load":
            name = str(data.get("name", ""))
            threading.Thread(target=engine.load_chat_model, args=(name,), daemon=True).start()
            self._json({"ok": True})
        elif path == "/api/books/scan":
            if engine.EMBED.state != "ready":
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": engine.EMBED.error or "Search engine not ready."}, 409)
                return
            threading.Thread(target=library.scan_and_add, daemon=True).start()
            self._json({"ok": True})
        elif path == "/api/books/add":
            if engine.EMBED.state != "ready":
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": engine.EMBED.error or "Search engine not ready."}, 409)
                return
            files = data.get("files") or ([data["file"]] if data.get("file") else [])
            self._json({"ok": True, "queued": library.request_add([str(f) for f in files])})
        elif path == "/api/books/category":
            try:
                library.set_category(str(data.get("file", "")), str(data.get("category", "")))
                self._json({"ok": True})
            except ValueError as e:
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}, 400)
        elif path == "/api/run":
            self._json(runner.run(str(data.get("code", "")), str(data.get("language", ""))))
        elif path == "/api/voices":
            try:
                voice.set_voice(str(data.get("engine", "auto")), data.get("id"))
                voice.prewarm_preview()            # so the Preview button plays straight away
                self._json({"ok": True, **voice.voice_list()})
            except ValueError as e:
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}, 400)
        elif path == "/api/voice/speak":
            self._speak(voice.preview_text() if data.get("preview") else str(data.get("text", "")))
        elif path == "/api/memory":
            item = memory.add(str(data.get("text", "")), source="manual")
            self._json({"ok": bool(item), "item": item})
        elif path == "/api/settings":
            if "auto_learn" in data:
                save_state(auto_learn=bool(data["auto_learn"]))
            if "nickname" in data:
                save_state(nickname=" ".join(str(data["nickname"]).split())[:40])
            if "wall_dim" in data:
                save_state(wall_dim=max(0, min(90, int(data["wall_dim"]))))
            if "wall_blur" in data:
                save_state(wall_blur=max(0, min(30, int(data["wall_blur"]))))
            self._json(status())
        elif path == "/api/wallpaper":
            try:
                kind = _save_wall(str(data.get("data", "")))
                self._json({"ok": True, "type": kind, **status()})
            except (ValueError, OSError) as e:
                self._json({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}, 400)
        elif path == "/api/open-folder":
            folder = {"books": BOOKS_DIR, "models": MODELS_DIR}.get(data.get("which"))
            if folder:
                _open_folder(folder)
            self._json({"ok": bool(folder)})
        else:
            self.send_error(404)

    def do_PUT(self):
        if not (self._host_ok() and self._origin_ok()):
            return
        m = re.fullmatch(r"/api/memory/(\w+)", urlparse(self.path).path)
        if not m:
            return self.send_error(404)
        self._json({"ok": memory.update(m.group(1), str(self._body().get("text", "")))})

    def do_DELETE(self):
        if not (self._host_ok() and self._origin_ok()):
            return
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path == "/api/wallpaper":
            f = _wall_file()
            if f:
                f.unlink()
            self._json({"ok": True})
        elif m := re.fullmatch(r"/api/memory/(\w+)", path):
            self._json({"ok": memory.delete(m.group(1))})
        elif m := re.fullmatch(r"/api/chats/([\w-]+)", path):
            self._json({"ok": chat.delete_conversation(m.group(1))})
        elif m := re.fullmatch(r"/api/docs/([\w-]+)/(\w+)", path):
            conv = chat.load_conversation(m.group(1))
            ok = docs.remove(conv, m.group(2))
            if ok:
                chat.save_conversation(conv)
            self._json({"ok": ok, "docs": conv.get("docs", [])})
        else:
            self.send_error(404)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ voice typing
    def _transcribe(self) -> None:
        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
        if length <= 0 or length > 30_000_000:          # 30 MB is about 15 minutes of speech
            self._json({"ok": False, "error": "The recording was empty or too long."}, 400)
            return
        audio = self.rfile.read(length)
        try:
            self._json({"ok": True, "text": voice.transcribe(audio)})
        except RuntimeError as e:
            self._json({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}, 200)

    def _speak(self, text: str) -> None:
        try:
            audio = voice.speak(text)
        except (RuntimeError, OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
            self._json({"ok": False, "error": str(e)}, 503)
            return
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "audio/wav")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(audio)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(audio)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ streaming
    def _stream_reply(self, data: dict) -> None:
        """Send the reply as Server-Sent Events: one 'data: {...}' line per piece."""
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.send_header("Connection", "close")
        self.end_headers()
        events = chat.reply(data.get("conv_id"), data.get("mode", "chat"), data.get("style", "quick"),
                            str(data.get("message", "")), bool(data.get("simpler")),
                            str(data.get("book_category", "auto")),
                            [str(x) for x in (data.get("images") or [])][:4],
                            turn=str(data.get("turn") or "") or None, rewind=str(data.get("rewind") or "") or None,
                            voice=bool(data.get("voice")))
        try:
            for event in events:
                self.wfile.write(("data: " + json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n\n").encode("utf-8"))
                self.wfile.flush()
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, ConnectionAbortedError):
            pass  # you pressed Stop or closed the tab
        finally:
            events.close()
        self.close_connection = True


def _start_engines(model: str | None) -> None:
    engine.start_embed_engine()
    library.INDEX.reload()
    if model:
        engine.load_chat_model(model)
    else:
        engine.CHAT.state = "error"
        engine.CHAT.error = (f"No usable model in the models folder (each must be a .gguf file "
                             f"of {CONFIG['max_model_gb']} GB or less).")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="QuantaKai")
    parser.add_argument("--no-browser", action="store_true", help="do not open the browser")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    engine.kill_leftover_engines()
    atexit.register(engine.stop_all)
    engine.remember_pid("ui", os.getpid())   # lets STOP.bat find this window's program
    try:
        httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", PORT), Handler)
    except OSError:
        print(f"Port {PORT} is busy - the chatbot may already be running. Run STOP.bat, then try again.")
        sys.exit(1)
    httpd.daemon_threads = True

    organize.tidy_downloads()             # files dropped in "new stuff" go to the right folders
    voice.start_unpacking()               # unpack voice engines dropped into the voice folder
    model = engine.pick_startup_model()
    threading.Thread(target=_start_engines, args=(model,), daemon=True).start()

    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}/"
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"  {CONFIG['app_name']} is running at {url}")
    print(f"  Loading model: {model or 'none found'}")
    print("  Keep this window open. Close it (or run STOP.bat) to quit.")
    print("=" * 60)
    if not args.no_browser:
        threading.Timer(1.0, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()
    try:
        httpd.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        engine.stop_all()
        httpd.server_close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""stop.py - used by STOP.bat. Stops the app and both AI engines if they are running."""
from . import engine

if __name__ == "__main__":
    engine.kill_leftover_engines(roles=("chat", "embed", "ui"))

20. ui\index.html

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\ui\index.html2,269 lines147,620 bytesSHA-256 103fd410c1ef3956…

The whole interface in one file: HTML, CSS and JavaScript, no libraries, no internet.

  • Pages: Chat, Library, Memory, Models, Personalise. Light theme by default (Light / Dark / Match Windows on Personalise).
  • Composer: mode buttons (Chat, Offline, Online, Create), styles (Detailed, Quick, Code, Teach, Quiz; Story or Character in Create), subject picker, pictures, attachments, voice.
  • Answers: a small Markdown renderer (all model text is escaped first, so nothing a model writes can run), [n] citations linked to sources, Copy, Simpler please, Read aloud, Retry, Edit, Run for Python/Java code, interactive quiz + flashcards + Save study guide.
  • Voice: hold-to-talk recording to WAV, live read-aloud sentence by sentence while the answer streams (Voice.feed), a Stop talking pill, Esc to stop.
  • Highlight any text on the page: a small menu offers Read aloud and Explain in detail.
  • The Blob: a canvas mascot whose colour follows the mode and whose accessories follow the style (see the Interface section).
  • Personalise: nickname (with a Hear it button), theme, wallpaper with fade and blur sliders.
