"use strict";
/* ================================================================ helpers */
const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);
const $$ = (s, r = document) => [...r.querySelectorAll(s)];
const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));
const store = {  // browser storage can be unavailable; never let that break the page
  get(k, d) { try { const v = localStorage.getItem(k); return v === null ? d : v; } catch { return d; } },
  set(k, v) { try { localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch {} }
};
async function api(path, method = "GET", body) {
  const res = await fetch(path, {method, headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
                                 body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body)});
  return res.json().catch(() => ({}));
}
function toast(text) {
  const t = document.createElement("div");
  t.className = "toast"; t.textContent = text; document.body.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 2600);
}
const ICON = {
  doc: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M14 3H7a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V8z"/><path d="M14 3v5h5M9 13h6M9 17h6"/></svg>',
  retry: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 3-6.7"/><path d="M3 4v5h5"/></svg>',
  edit: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 20h4L19 9l-4-4L4 16z"/><path d="M14 6l4 4"/></svg>',
  speak: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M11 5 6 9H3v6h3l5 4z"/><path d="M15.5 8.5a5 5 0 0 1 0 7"/></svg>',
  copy: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="9" y="9" width="11" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M5 15V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h10"/></svg>',
  simple: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3l2.4 5 5.6.8-4 3.9 1 5.5L12 15.6 7 18.2l1-5.5-4-3.9 5.6-.8z"/></svg>',
  book: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 19V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h13v16H6a2 2 0 0 0-2 2zM4 19a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h13"/></svg>',
  send: '<path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/>',
  stop: '<rect x="7" y="7" width="10" height="10" rx="2"/>'
};

/* ================================================================ markdown
   Turns the model's **bold**, lists, `code` and [1] citations into HTML.
   Text is escaped FIRST, so nothing the model writes can run as code.   */
function inline(s) {
  return s
    .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, "<code>$1</code>")
    .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, "<strong>$1</strong>")
    .replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\s][^*]*)\*/g, "$1<em>$2</em>")
    .replace(/\[(\d{1,2})\]/g, '<button class="cite" data-n="$1">$1</button>');
}
function markdown(src) {
  let text = esc(src);
  const fences = (text.match(/```/g) || []).length;
  if (fences % 2) text += "\n```";               // close a code block that is still being written
  const out = [];
  const parts = text.split(/```/);
  parts.forEach((part, i) => {
    if (i % 2) {
      const lang = ((part.match(/^(\w*)\n/) || [])[1] || "").toLowerCase();
      out.push(`<pre data-lang="${lang}"><code>` + part.replace(/^\w*\n/, "") + "</code></pre>");
      return;
    }
    let list = null, para = [];
    const flushPara = () => { if (para.length) { out.push("<p>" + inline(para.join("<br>")) + "</p>"); para = []; } };
    const flushList = () => { if (list) { out.push(`<${list.type}>` + list.items.map(x => "<li>" + inline(x) + "</li>").join("") + `</${list.type}>`); list = null; } };
    for (const line of part.split("\n")) {
      let m;
      if (!line.trim()) { flushPara(); flushList(); continue; }
      if ((m = line.match(/^\s*#{1,4}\s+(.*)/))) { flushPara(); flushList(); out.push("<h3>" + inline(m[1]) + "</h3>"); continue; }
      if ((m = line.match(/^\s*(?:[-*•])\s+(.*)/)) || (m = line.match(/^\s*\d+[.)]\s+(.*)/))) {
        const type = /^\s*\d/.test(line) ? "ol" : "ul";
        flushPara();
        if (!list || list.type !== type) { flushList(); list = {type, items: []}; }
        list.items.push(m[1]); continue;
      }
      flushList(); para.push(line);
    }
    flushPara(); flushList();
  });
  return out.join("");
}

/* ================================================================ state */
const S = {
  conv: null, mode: store.get("mode", "chat"), style: store.get("style2", "explain"), cstyle: store.get("cstyle", "story"),
  busy: false, ctrl: null, status: null
};

/* ================================================================ theme */
function applyTheme(t) {
  if (t === "system") delete document.documentElement.dataset.theme;
  else if (t) document.documentElement.dataset.theme = t;
  $$("#themeSeg button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.theme === (t || "light")));
}
if (store.get("themeDefault", "") !== "light") {   // light is the default (once; your later choice is kept)
  store.set("theme", "light"); store.set("themeDefault", "light");
}
applyTheme(store.get("theme", "light"));
$("#themeBtn").onclick = () => {
  const dark = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).colorScheme.includes("dark");
  const next = dark ? "light" : "dark";
  applyTheme(next); store.set("theme", next);
};
$$("#themeSeg button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { applyTheme(b.dataset.theme); store.set("theme", b.dataset.theme); });

/* ================================================================ navigation */
function show(view) {
  $$(".nav button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.view === view));
  $$(".view").forEach(v => v.classList.add("hidden"));
  $("#view-" + view).classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#composer").classList.toggle("hidden", view !== "chat");
  $("#modeSeg").classList.toggle("hidden", view !== "chat");
  $("#styleSeg").classList.toggle("hidden", view !== "chat" || S.mode === "chat" || S.mode === "create");
  $("#side").classList.remove("open");
  if (view === "library") loadBooks();
  if (view === "memory") loadMemory();
  if (view === "models") loadModels();
  if (view === "personal") loadPersonal();
}
$$(".nav button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => show(b.dataset.view));
$("#menuBtn").onclick = () => $("#side").classList.toggle("open");
$$("[data-open]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => api("/api/open-folder", "POST", {which: b.dataset.open}));

function setMode(mode) {
  S.mode = mode; store.set("mode", mode); window.Blobs && Blobs.refresh();
  $$("#modeSeg button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.mode === mode));
  $("#styleSeg").classList.toggle("hidden", mode === "chat" || mode === "create");
  $("#createSeg").classList.toggle("hidden", mode !== "create");
  $("#toolrow").classList.toggle("hidden", mode === "chat");
  $("#bookbar").classList.toggle("hidden", mode !== "study");
  const story = S.cstyle !== "character";
  $("#input").placeholder = mode === "study" ? "Ask a question about your books..."
    : mode === "online" ? "Ask anything - I'll search the web..."
    : mode === "create" ? (story ? "Describe a story: genre, characters, setting, length..." : "Describe a character: who they are, their world, their role...")
    : "Message your assistant...";
  $("#hint").textContent = mode === "study"
    ? "Offline mode answers only from your books and shows the pages it used"
    : mode === "online" ? "Online mode searches the web (your question is sent to DuckDuckGo) and links its sources"
    : mode === "create" ? (story ? "Story: say \"continue\" for the next part, or ask for changes" : "Character: ask for changes and the full sheet is rewritten")
    : "Enter to send - Shift+Enter for a new line - everything stays on this laptop";
}
function setStyle(style) {
  S.style = style; store.set("style2", style); window.Blobs && Blobs.refresh();
  $$("#styleSeg button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.style === style));
}
$$("#modeSeg button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setMode(b.dataset.mode));
$$("#styleSeg button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setStyle(b.dataset.style));
function setCreateStyle(cs) {
  S.cstyle = cs; store.set("cstyle", cs);
  $$("#createSeg button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.cstyle === cs));
  if (S.mode === "create") setMode("create");
}
$$("#createSeg button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setCreateStyle(b.dataset.cstyle));

/* ================================================================ status + model picker */
async function refreshStatus() {
  try {
    const s = await api("/api/status");
    S.status = s;
    const wk = `${s.wallpaper}/${s.wall_dim}/${s.wall_blur}`;
    if (S.wallKey !== wk) { S.wallKey = wk; applyWall(s); }
    $("#appName").textContent = s.app_name;
    document.title = s.app_name;
    const dot = $("#engineDot");
    dot.className = "dot " + (s.chat.state === "ready" ? "ready" : s.chat.state === "loading" ? "loading" : s.chat.state === "error" ? "error" : "");
    $("#engineName").textContent = s.chat.state === "loading" ? "Loading " + (s.chat.model || "model") + "..."
      : s.chat.state === "ready" ? s.chat.model : (s.chat.error || "No model loaded");
    $("#engineName").title = s.chat.error || s.chat.model || "";
    $("#countBooks").textContent = s.books || "";
    $("#countMem").textContent = s.memories || "";
    if (!S.busy) $("#sendBtn").disabled = s.chat.state !== "ready";
  } catch { $("#engineName").textContent = "App not running"; $("#engineDot").className = "dot error"; }
}
async function fillModelSelect() {
  const r = await api("/api/models");
  const sel = $("#modelSelect");
  sel.innerHTML = r.models.length ? "" : "<option>No models in folder</option>";
  for (const m of r.models) {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.name; o.disabled = !m.allowed;
    const short = m.name.replace(/\.gguf$/i, "").replace(/^[A-Za-z]+_(?=[A-Z])/, "").replace(/[-_.](i?q\d\w*|f16|bf16)$/i, "").replace(/-instruct/i, "");
    o.textContent = `${short} - ${m.size_gb} GB${m.allowed ? "" : " (blocked)"}`; o.title = m.name;
    if (m.name === r.active) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  }
}
$("#modelSelect").onchange = async e => {
  await api("/api/models/load", "POST", {name: e.target.value});
  toast("Loading " + e.target.value + "...");
  setTimeout(refreshStatus, 300);
};

/* ================================================================ chat history */
async function loadHistory() {
  const r = await api("/api/chats");
  const box = $("#history");
  box.innerHTML = r.chats.length ? "" : '<div class="hitem" style="cursor:default"><span>No chats yet</span></div>';
  for (const c of r.chats) {
    const d = document.createElement("div");
    d.className = "hitem" + (c.id === S.conv ? " on" : "");
    d.innerHTML = `<span title="${esc(c.title)}">${esc(c.title)}</span><button class="x" title="Delete">&times;</button>`;
    d.onclick = () => openChat(c.id);
    d.querySelector(".x").onclick = async ev => {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      if (!confirm("Delete this chat?")) return;
      await api("/api/chats/" + c.id, "DELETE");
      if (S.conv === c.id) newChat(); else loadHistory();
    };
    box.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function welcome() {
  const name = S.status ? S.status.user_name : "";
  $("#thread").innerHTML = `<div class="welcome"><canvas class="qfield" aria-hidden="true"></canvas><canvas class="qblob big" data-size="150" aria-label="QuantaKai"></canvas>
    <h1>Hi${name ? " " + esc(name) : ""}, what's on your mind?</h1>
    <p>Chat about anything, switch to Offline to learn from your books, or Online to search the web.</p>
    <div class="chips">
      <button class="chip" data-q="Help me plan my study week">Plan my study week</button>
      <button class="chip" data-q="Explain overfitting in machine learning" data-m="study">Offline: overfitting</button>
      <button class="chip" data-q="What do you remember about me?">What do you remember about me?</button>
    </div></div>`;
  $$(".chip").forEach(c => c.onclick = () => { if (c.dataset.m) setMode(c.dataset.m); $("#input").value = c.dataset.q; send(); });
  if (Blobs) Blobs.mount($("#thread"));
}
function newChat() { S.conv = null; Docs.set([]); welcome(); loadHistory(); show("chat"); $("#input").focus(); }
$("#newChat").onclick = newChat;

async function openChat(id) {
  const c = await api("/api/chats/" + id);
  if (!c.messages && !c.docs) return;
  c.messages = c.messages || [];
  S.conv = id; show("chat"); Docs.set(c.docs);
  const thread = $("#thread"); thread.innerHTML = "";
  if (!c.messages.length) welcome();
  let u = null;
  for (const m of c.messages) {
    if (m.role === "user") {
      const shown = m.display || m.content;
      u = addUser(shown); u.dataset.turn = m.turn || "";
      u._payload = {message: m.content, text: shown.replace(/ \(with picture\)$/, ""), images: [],
                    mode: m.mode || "chat", style: m.style || "explain", simpler: shown === "Explain that more simply"};
    }
    else { const b = addBot(m.mode); b._user = u; finishBot(b, m.content, m.sources || [], m.stats || {}, m.mode); }
  }
  loadHistory(); scrollDown(true);
}

/* ================================================================ messages */
function scrollDown(force) {
  const v = $("#view-chat");
  if (force || v.scrollHeight - v.scrollTop - v.clientHeight < 160) v.scrollTop = v.scrollHeight;
}
function addUser(text, images) {
  if ($(".welcome")) $("#thread").innerHTML = "";
  const d = document.createElement("div");
  const pics = (images || []).map(src => `<img class="upic" src="${src}" alt="attached picture">`).join("");
  d.className = "msg user";
  d.innerHTML = `<div class="ucol"><div class="bubble">${pics}${esc(text)}</div>
    <div class="uacts"><button class="act" data-u="edit" title="Change this question and ask again">${ICON.edit}Edit</button>
    <button class="act" data-u="copy">${ICON.copy}Copy</button></div></div>`;
  $("#thread").appendChild(d); scrollDown(true);
  bindUser(d, text);
  return d;
}
function bindUser(d, text) {
  d.querySelector('[data-u="edit"]').onclick = () => editUser(d);
  d.querySelector('[data-u="copy"]').onclick = () => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(d._payload ? (d._payload.text ?? d._payload.message) : text); toast("Copied"); };
}
/* Retry, Resend and Edit: remove that question and everything after it, then ask again */
function removeFrom(el) { while (el.nextSibling) el.nextSibling.remove(); el.remove(); }
function busyNote() { toast("Wait for the current answer to finish, or press Stop first"); }
function retry(b) {
  if (S.busy) return busyNote();
  const u = b._user;
  if (!u || !u._payload) return toast("This answer can't be retried");
  removeFrom(u);
  send({resend: u._payload, rewind: u.dataset.turn});
}
function editUser(u) {
  if (S.busy) return busyNote();
  const p = u._payload; if (!p) return;
  const col = u.querySelector(".ucol"), before = col.innerHTML;
  col.innerHTML = `<div class="uedit"><textarea aria-label="Edit your question"></textarea>
    <div class="row"><button class="btn small" data-e="cancel">Cancel</button><button class="btn small primary" data-e="send">Send</button></div></div>`;
  const ta = col.querySelector("textarea");
  ta.value = p.simpler ? "Explain that more simply" : (p.text ?? p.message);
  const fit = () => { ta.style.height = "auto"; ta.style.height = Math.min(ta.scrollHeight + 2, 320) + "px"; };
  fit(); ta.addEventListener("input", fit); ta.focus(); ta.setSelectionRange(ta.value.length, ta.value.length);
  const cancel = () => { col.innerHTML = before; bindUser(u, p.text ?? p.message); };
  const go = () => {
    const v = ta.value.trim();
    if (!v && !(p.images || []).length) return;
    if (S.busy) return busyNote();
    removeFrom(u);
    send({resend: {...p, message: v, text: v, simpler: false}, rewind: u.dataset.turn});
  };
  col.querySelector('[data-e="cancel"]').onclick = cancel;
  col.querySelector('[data-e="send"]').onclick = go;
  ta.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); go(); }
    else if (e.key === "Escape") cancel();
  });
}
function showResend(b, note) {
  const a = b.querySelector(".actions");
  const w = document.createElement("div"); w.className = "cut";
  w.innerHTML = `<span>${esc(note)}</span><button class="act resend">${ICON.retry}Resend</button>`;
  w.querySelector("button").onclick = () => retry(b);
  a.after(w);
}
function addBot(mode) {
  const d = document.createElement("div");
  d.className = "msg bot";
  d.dataset.mode = mode;
  d.innerHTML = `<div class="av"></div><div class="body">
    <div class="tag">${mode === "study" ? "Offline" : mode === "online" ? "Online" : mode === "create" ? "Create" : "Chat"}</div>
    <div class="srcwrap"></div>
    <div class="md"><div class="thinking"><span class="dot loading"></span>${mode === "study" ? "Searching your books..." : mode === "online" ? "Searching the web..." : mode === "create" ? "Writing..." : "Thinking..."}</div></div>
    <div class="actions"></div></div>`;
  $("#thread").appendChild(d); scrollDown(true);
  return d;
}
function renderSources(b, sources, categories, corrections) {
  const fixes = corrections && Object.keys(corrections).length
    ? `<div class="fixes" title="Spelling was corrected for the book search only">Searched for ${Object.entries(corrections)
        .map(([a, c]) => `<b>${esc(c)}</b> <s>${esc(a)}</s>`).join(", ")}</div>` : "";
  const online = sources.length && sources[0].url;
  if (!sources.length) {
    b.querySelector(".srcwrap").innerHTML = fixes + '<div class="note" style="margin:6px 0">No matching sources found.</div>';
    return;
  }
  if (online) {
    const items = sources.map(s => `<div class="src" data-n="${s.n}"><div class="h"><span class="n">[${s.n}]</span>
       <span class="t"><a href="${esc(s.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(s.title)}</a></span>
       <span class="p">${esc(new URL(s.url).hostname)}</span></div><div class="x">${esc(s.text)}</div></div>`).join("");
    b.querySelector(".srcwrap").innerHTML = `<details class="sources"><summary>${sources.length} web source${sources.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}</summary>${items}</details>`;
    $$(".src .x", b).forEach(x => x.onclick = () => x.classList.toggle("open"));
    return;
  }
  const items = sources.map(s => `<div class="src" data-n="${s.n}"><div class="h"><span class="n">[${s.n}]</span>
     <span class="t">${esc(s.title)}</span>${s.category ? `<span class="ctag">${esc(s.category)}</span>` : ""}<span class="p">${s.page === s.page_end ? "p. " + s.page : "pp. " + s.page + "-" + s.page_end} - match ${Math.round(s.score * 100)}%</span></div>
     <div class="x">${esc(s.text)}</div></div>`).join("");
  const searched = categories && categories.length ? ` <span class="cats">- searched: ${categories.map(esc).join(", ")}</span>` : "";
  b.querySelector(".srcwrap").innerHTML = fixes + `<details class="sources"><summary>${ICON.book}${sources.length} passage${sources.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} from your books${searched}</summary>${items}</details>`;
  $$(".src .x", b).forEach(x => x.onclick = () => x.classList.toggle("open"));
}
function finishBot(b, text, sources, stats, mode) {
  if (mode === "study" || mode === "online") renderSources(b, sources);
  b.querySelector(".md").innerHTML = markdown(text);
  $$(".cite", b).forEach(c => c.onclick = () => {
    const det = b.querySelector("details"); if (!det) return;
    det.open = true;
    const row = b.querySelector(`.src[data-n="${c.dataset.n}"]`);
    if (row) { row.classList.remove("flash"); void row.offsetWidth; row.classList.add("flash"); row.scrollIntoView({block: "nearest", behavior: "smooth"}); }
  });
  const a = b.querySelector(".actions");
  a.innerHTML = `<button class="act" data-a="copy">${ICON.copy}Copy</button>
    <button class="act" data-a="simple">${ICON.simple}Simpler please</button>
    <button class="act" data-a="speak">${ICON.speak}Read aloud</button>
    <button class="act" data-a="retry" title="Ask the same question again for a new answer">${ICON.retry}Retry</button>
    <span class="stats">${stats.seconds ? stats.seconds + " s" : ""}${stats.tokens_per_second ? " - " + stats.tokens_per_second + " tokens/s" : ""}</span>`;
  a.querySelector('[data-a="copy"]').onclick = () => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text); toast("Copied"); };
  a.querySelector('[data-a="simple"]').onclick = () => send({simpler: true, mode});
  const sp = a.querySelector('[data-a="speak"]');
  sp.onclick = () => Voice.speaking === sp ? Voice.stopSpeaking() : Voice.speak(text, sp);
  a.querySelector('[data-a="retry"]').onclick = () => retry(b);
  a.querySelector(".stats").title = "Tokens are word pieces. More per second = faster.";
  addRunButtons(b);
}

/* ================================================================ running code */
function addRunButtons(b) {
  const can = (S.status && S.status.run) || {python: true};
  $$("pre[data-lang]", b).forEach(pre => {
    let lang = pre.dataset.lang;
    if (lang === "py" || lang === "python3") lang = "python";
    if (!(lang === "python" || lang === "java")) return;
    const bar = document.createElement("div"); bar.className = "runbar";
    bar.innerHTML = `<button class="act" data-r="run">&#9654; Run ${lang === "java" ? "Java" : "Python"}</button>
      <button class="act" data-r="copy">${ICON.copy}Copy code</button>`;
    pre.after(bar);
    const code = pre.innerText;
    bar.querySelector('[data-r="copy"]').onclick = () => { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(code); toast("Code copied"); };
    bar.querySelector('[data-r="run"]').onclick = async e => {
      if (lang === "java" && !can.java) { toast("Java isn't installed on this laptop. Install a JDK 17+ to run Java."); return; }
      const btn = e.currentTarget; btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = "Running...";
      let out = bar.nextElementSibling;
      if (!out || !out.classList.contains("runout")) { out = document.createElement("div"); out.className = "runout"; bar.after(out); }
      try {
        const r = await (await fetch("/api/run", {method: "POST", headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
          body: JSON.stringify({code, language: lang})})).json();
        const text = (r.output || "") + (r.error ? (r.output ? "\n" : "") + r.error : "");
        out.className = "runout " + (r.ok ? "good" : "bad");
        out.innerHTML = `<div class="rh">${r.ok ? "Output" : "Error"}${r.seconds != null ? " - " + r.seconds + " s" : ""}</div><pre>${esc(text || "(no output)")}</pre>`;
        if (!r.ok) {
          const fix = document.createElement("button"); fix.className = "act"; fix.textContent = "Fix this error";
          fix.onclick = () => {
            $("#input").value = "This code gave an error when I ran it. Please fix it and give the complete corrected code.\n\nCode:\n```" + lang + "\n" + code + "\n```\n\nError:\n```\n" + text.slice(-3000) + "\n```";
            setStyle && S.mode !== "chat" && setStyle("code"); send();
          };
          out.appendChild(fix);
        }
      } catch (err) { out.className = "runout bad"; out.textContent = "Could not reach the app to run the code."; }
      btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = `&#9654; Run ${lang === "java" ? "Java" : "Python"}`;
    };
  });
}

/* ================================================================ quizzes and flashcards */
function parseQuiz(md) {
  const title = ((md.match(/##\s*Quiz:\s*(.+)/i) || [])[1] || "Revision quiz").trim();
  const qs = [];
  const blocks = md.split(/\n(?=\s*\**Q\d+[.)])/);
  for (const blk of blocks) {
    const q = blk.match(/^\s*\**Q\d+[.)]\**\s*(.+)/);
    if (!q) continue;
    const opts = [...blk.matchAll(/^\s*\**([A-D])[).]\**\s*(.+)$/gm)].map(m => ({k: m[1], t: m[2].trim()}));
    const ans = (blk.match(/Answer:\**\s*\(?([A-D])/i) || [])[1];
    const why = ((blk.match(/Why:\**\s*([\s\S]*?)(?=\n\s*\n|\n##|$)/i) || [])[1] || "").trim();
    if (opts.length >= 2 && ans) qs.push({q: q[1].trim(), opts, ans: ans.toUpperCase(), why});
  }
  const cards = [...md.matchAll(/^\s*[-*]?\s*Card:\s*(.+?)\s*\|\s*(.+)$/gim)].map(m => ({f: m[1].trim(), b: m[2].trim()}));
  return {title, qs, cards};
}
function renderQuiz(b, md, sources) {
  const quiz = parseQuiz(md);
  if (!quiz.qs.length && !quiz.cards.length) return;
  const box = document.createElement("div"); box.className = "quizbox";
  let score = 0, answered = 0;
  box.innerHTML = `<div class="qhead"><b>${esc(quiz.title)}</b><span class="qscore">0 / ${quiz.qs.length}</span>
      <button class="act" data-q="save">Save as study guide (HTML)</button></div>` +
    quiz.qs.map((q, i) => `<div class="qq" data-i="${i}"><div class="qt">${i + 1}. ${inline(esc(q.q))}</div>` +
      q.opts.map(o => `<button class="qo" data-k="${o.k}"><b>${o.k}</b> ${inline(esc(o.t))}</button>`).join("") +
      `<div class="qwhy hidden">${inline(esc(q.why))}</div></div>`).join("") +
    (quiz.cards.length ? `<div class="qcards"><div class="qt">Flashcards - click to flip</div>` +
      quiz.cards.map(c => `<div class="fc"><div class="f">${inline(esc(c.f))}</div><div class="bk">${inline(esc(c.b))}</div></div>`).join("") + "</div>" : "");
  $$(".qq", box).forEach(el => {
    const q = quiz.qs[+el.dataset.i];
    $$(".qo", el).forEach(btn => btn.onclick = () => {
      if (el.classList.contains("done")) return;
      el.classList.add("done"); answered++;
      const right = btn.dataset.k === q.ans; if (right) score++;
      btn.classList.add(right ? "right" : "wrong");
      el.querySelector(`.qo[data-k="${q.ans}"]`).classList.add("right");
      el.querySelector(".qwhy").classList.remove("hidden");
      box.querySelector(".qscore").textContent = `${score} / ${quiz.qs.length}` + (answered === quiz.qs.length ? (score === quiz.qs.length ? " - perfect!" : "") : "");
    });
  });
  $$(".fc", box).forEach(c => c.onclick = () => c.classList.toggle("flip"));
  box.querySelector('[data-q="save"]').onclick = () => saveStudyGuide(quiz, sources);
  b.querySelector(".md").replaceWith(box);
}
function saveStudyGuide(quiz, sources) {
  const e = s => esc(s);
  const levels = ["Easy", "Easy", "Medium", "Medium", "Hard", "Hard"];
  const html = `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>${e(quiz.title)} - study guide</title><style>
body{font-family:Segoe UI,system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:820px;margin:40px auto;padding:0 20px;line-height:1.6;color:#1b2333;background:#f7f9fc}
h1{color:#5b4bd6;margin-bottom:4px}.sub{color:#667;margin-top:0}
section{background:#fff;border-radius:14px;padding:18px 22px;margin:18px 0;box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,.06);border-left:6px solid #5b4bd6}
section.cards{border-left-color:#0ea5a0}section.src{border-left-color:#db2777}
.q{margin:14px 0 18px}.lvl{display:inline-block;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:99px;margin-right:6px}
.Easy{background:#dcfce7;color:#166534}.Medium{background:#fef3c7;color:#92400e}.Hard{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b}
ol{margin:6px 0}details{margin-top:6px;background:#f2f0ff;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px}summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;color:#5b4bd6}
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}td{border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;padding:8px;vertical-align:top}td:first-child{font-weight:600;width:38%}
@media print{details{display:block}details>*{display:block}}</style></head><body>
<h1>${e(quiz.title)}</h1><p class="sub">Study guide made by QuantaKai on ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}</p>
<section><h2>Practice questions</h2>${quiz.qs.map((q, i) => `<div class="q"><span class="lvl ${levels[i] || "Hard"}">${levels[i] || "Hard"}</span><b>${i + 1}. ${e(q.q)}</b>
<ol type="A">${q.opts.map(o => `<li>${e(o.t)}</li>`).join("")}</ol><details><summary>Show answer</summary><p><b>${q.ans}</b> - ${e(q.why)}</p></details></div>`).join("")}</section>
${quiz.cards.length ? `<section class="cards"><h2>Flashcards</h2><table>${quiz.cards.map(c => `<tr><td>${e(c.f)}</td><td>${e(c.b)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table></section>` : ""}
${sources && sources.length ? `<section class="src"><h2>Sources</h2><ol>${sources.map(s => `<li>${e(s.title)}${s.page ? ", p. " + s.page : ""}${s.url ? " - " + e(s.url) : ""}</li>`).join("")}</ol></section>` : ""}
</body></html>`;
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([html], {type: "text/html"}));
  a.download = quiz.title.replace(/[^\w\- ]+/g, "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, "-").slice(0, 60) + "-study-guide.html";
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  toast("Study guide saved to your Downloads folder");
}

/* ================================================================ sending */
function setBusy(busy) {
  S.busy = busy;
  $("#sendIcon").innerHTML = busy ? ICON.stop : ICON.send;
  $("#sendBtn").title = busy ? "Stop" : "Send (Enter)";
  $("#sendBtn").disabled = !busy && !(S.status && S.status.chat.state === "ready");
}
async function send(opts = {}) {
  if (S.busy) { S.ctrl?.abort(); return; }
  Voice.stopSpeaking();
  const input = $("#input");
  const re = opts.resend;          // set when retrying, resending or editing an earlier question
  const simpler = re ? !!re.simpler : !!opts.simpler;
  const message = re ? (re.message || "") : (simpler ? "" : input.value.trim());
  const images = re ? (re.images || []) : (simpler ? [] : Pics.list.slice());
  if (!message && !images.length && !simpler) return;
  if (Docs.busy) { toast("Still reading your document - send again in a moment"); return; }
  const mode = re ? re.mode : (opts.mode || S.mode);
  const style = re ? (re.style || S.style) : (opts.style || (mode === "create" ? S.cstyle : S.style));
  if (!re) { input.value = ""; grow(); Pics.clear(); }
  const turn = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 12);
  const u = addUser(simpler ? "Explain that more simply" : (message || "Answer the question in the picture"), images);
  u.dataset.turn = turn;
  u._payload = {message, text: re ? (re.text ?? message) : message, images, mode, style, simpler};
  const b = addBot(mode); b._user = u;
  setBusy(true);
  S.ctrl = new AbortController();
  let text = "", sources = [], stats = {}, pending = false, failed = false, stopped = false, finished = false;
  let closed = false;
  const paint = () => { pending = false; if (closed) return; b.querySelector(".md").innerHTML = markdown(text); b.querySelector(".md").classList.add("cursor"); scrollDown(); };
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/chat", {method: "POST", headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"}, signal: S.ctrl.signal,
      body: JSON.stringify({conv_id: S.conv, mode, style, message, simpler, turn, rewind: opts.rewind || null,
        book_category: $("#bookCat").value || "auto", images, voice: Voice.on})});
    const reader = res.body.getReader(), dec = new TextDecoder();
    let buf = "";
    for (;;) {
      const {value, done} = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buf += dec.decode(value, {stream: true});
      let i;
      while ((i = buf.indexOf("\n\n")) >= 0) {
        const line = buf.slice(0, i); buf = buf.slice(i + 2);
        if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;
        const ev = JSON.parse(line.slice(6));
        if (ev.type === "meta") S.conv = ev.conv_id;
        else if (ev.type === "ocr") {
          const w = document.createElement("details"); w.className = "ocrbox";
          w.innerHTML = `<summary>Text read from the picture</summary><pre>${esc(ev.text)}</pre>`;
          b.querySelector(".srcwrap").before(w);
        }
        else if (ev.type === "notice") toast(ev.text);
        else if (ev.type === "context" && ev.names.length) {
          const w = document.createElement("div"); w.className = "ctxused";
          w.innerHTML = `${ICON.doc}Using this chat's documents: ${ev.names.map(esc).join(", ")}`;
          b.querySelector(".srcwrap").before(w);
        }
        else if (ev.type === "status") { const t = b.querySelector(".thinking span, .thinking"); if (t && !text) t.lastChild && (t.lastChild.textContent = " " + ev.text); }
        else if (ev.type === "sources") { sources = ev.sources; renderSources(b, sources, ev.categories, ev.corrections); }
        else if (ev.type === "memory") toast(ev.note);
        else if (ev.type === "token" && !failed) {
          text += ev.text; if (!pending) { pending = true; requestAnimationFrame(paint); }
          if (Voice.on && mode !== "create") Voice.feed(text, false);   // start talking after the first sentence
        }
        else if (ev.type === "done") { stats = ev.stats || {}; finished = true; }
        else if (ev.type === "error") { failed = true; b.querySelector(".md").innerHTML = `<div class="err">${esc(ev.message)}</div>`; }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.name === "AbortError") stopped = true;
    else { failed = true; b.querySelector(".md").innerHTML = `<div class="err">Could not reach the app. Is the black START window still open?</div>`; }
  }
  closed = true;
  b.querySelector(".md").classList.remove("cursor");
  if (Voice.live) { if (stopped || failed) Voice.stopSpeaking(); else Voice.feed(text, true); }
  if (!failed) {
    if (text) {
      finishBot(b, text, sources, stats, mode);
      if (!finished) showResend(b, stopped ? "You stopped this answer early." : "This answer was cut off before it finished.");
      else {
        if (mode !== "chat" && style === "quiz") renderQuiz(b, text, sources);
        if (Voice.live) Voice.attachButton(b.querySelector('[data-a="speak"]'));
      }
    }
    else { b.querySelector(".md").innerHTML = '<div class="thinking">Stopped before an answer was written.</div>'; showResend(b, ""); }
  }
  else showResend(b, "Something went wrong.");
  setBusy(false); refreshStatus(); loadHistory(); scrollDown();
  setTimeout(refreshStatus, 8000);   // auto-learn may have added memories
}
$("#sendBtn").onclick = () => send();
const grow = () => { const t = $("#input"); t.style.height = "auto"; t.style.height = Math.min(t.scrollHeight, 200) + "px"; };
$("#input").addEventListener("input", grow);
$("#input").addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); send(); } });

/* ================================================================ voice
   LISTENING: the microphone is recorded in the browser, turned into a small
   16 kHz WAV file and sent to the app, which runs whisper.cpp on this laptop.
   SPEAKING: uses the voices built into Windows (offline ones only).          */
/* ---------- highlight any text on the page -> Read aloud / Explain */
const SelPop = {
  el: null, text: "",
  init() {
    const el = this.el = document.createElement("div");
    el.className = "selpop"; el.hidden = true; el.setAttribute("role", "toolbar");
    el.innerHTML = `<button data-s="read" title="Read the highlighted text aloud"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M11 5 6 9H3v6h3l5 4z"/><path d="M15.5 8.5a5 5 0 0 1 0 7M18.5 5.5a9 9 0 0 1 0 13"/></svg>Read aloud</button>` +
      `<button data-s="explain" title="Ask for a detailed explanation of the highlighted text"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/><path d="m20 20-4-4M11 8v3l2 2"/></svg>Explain in detail</button>`;
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    el.addEventListener("mousedown", e => e.preventDefault());        // keep the highlight while clicking
    el.querySelector('[data-s="read"]').onclick = () => { const t = this.text; this.hide(); Voice.speak(t, null); };
    el.querySelector('[data-s="explain"]').onclick = () => this.explain();
    let timer;
    document.addEventListener("selectionchange", () => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => this.check(), 180); });
    window.addEventListener("scroll", () => this.hide(), true);
    window.addEventListener("resize", () => this.hide());
    document.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Escape" && !this.el.hidden) this.hide(); });
  },
  check() {
    const sel = window.getSelection();
    const text = sel && !sel.isCollapsed ? sel.toString().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() : "";
    const a = document.activeElement;
    const inBox = a && /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(a.tagName) && a.selectionStart !== a.selectionEnd;   // selecting inside a text box
    if (text.length < 2 || inBox || this.el.contains(sel.anchorNode)) return this.hide();
    const r = sel.getRangeAt(0).getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!r.width && !r.height) return this.hide();
    this.text = text.slice(0, 4000);
    const el = this.el; el.hidden = false;
    const w = el.offsetWidth, h = el.offsetHeight;
    let x = r.left + r.width / 2 - w / 2, y = r.top - h - 8;
    if (y < 8) y = r.bottom + 8;                                       // no room above: show it below
    x = Math.max(8, Math.min(x, innerWidth - w - 8));
    el.style.left = x + "px"; el.style.top = Math.min(y, innerHeight - h - 8) + "px";
  },
  hide() { if (this.el) this.el.hidden = true; },
  explain() {
    const t = this.text; this.hide();
    if (S.busy) return busyNote();
    window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges();
    const mode = S.mode === "create" ? "chat" : S.mode;                 // books stay in Study, web in Online
    show("chat");
    $("#input").value = "Explain this in detail, step by step, so I can really understand it:\n\n\"" + t + "\"";
    send({mode, style: mode === "chat" ? undefined : "explain"});
  }
};
SelPop.init();

const Voice = {
  on: String(store.get("voiceMode", false)) === "true", rec: null, speaking: null, voices: [], PREVIEW: "\u0000preview", live: null,

  setMode(on) {
    this.on = on; store.set("voiceMode", on); window.Blobs && Blobs.refresh();
    $("#voiceBtn").classList.toggle("on", on);
    $("#voiceMenuBtn").classList.toggle("on", on);
    if (!on) this.stopSpeaking();
  },

  /* ---------- speaking */
  loadVoices() {
    // fallback only: offline Windows voices, preferring a US female one (Aria, Jenny, Zira)
    const all = speechSynthesis.getVoices();
    const offline = all.filter(v => v.localService);
    const pool = (offline.length ? offline : all).filter(v => /^en/i.test(v.lang));
    const score = v => (/en-US/i.test(v.lang) ? 2 : 0) + (/aria|jenny|zira|female|ava|emma/i.test(v.name) ? 3 : 0);
    this.voices = pool.sort((a, b) => score(b) - score(a));
  },
  clean(md) {
    return md.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, " The code is shown on screen. ")
      .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, "$1").replace(/\s*\[(\d+)\]/g, "")
      .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]+\)/g, "$1").replace(/^#+\s*(.+?)[.:]?\s*$/gm, "$1.")
      .replace(/[*_>|#]+/g, " ").replace(/^\s*[-•]\s+/gm, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  },
  chunk(text) {
    const parts = text.match(/[^.!?]+[.!?]+["')]*|\S[^.!?]*$/g) || [text];
    const chunks = [];
    for (const p of parts) {
      if (chunks.length && (chunks[chunks.length - 1] + p).length < 220) chunks[chunks.length - 1] += " " + p.trim();
      else chunks.push(p.trim());
    }
    return chunks.filter(Boolean);
  },
  speak(md, btn) {
    this.stopSpeaking();
    const neural = S.status && S.status.voice && S.status.voice.tts;
    if (neural) return this.speakNeural(this.clean(md), btn);
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) { toast("This browser cannot read aloud."); return; }
    if (!this.voices.length) this.loadVoices();
    const text = this.clean(md);
    // short pieces: browsers stop long speeches halfway
    const parts = text.match(/[^.!?]+[.!?]+|\S[^.!?]*$/g) || [text];
    const chunks = [];
    for (const p of parts) {
      if (chunks.length && (chunks[chunks.length - 1] + p).length < 220) chunks[chunks.length - 1] += " " + p;
      else chunks.push(p.trim());
    }
    this.speaking = btn || true;
    btn?.classList.add("speaking"); this.ui();
    chunks.forEach((c, i) => {
      const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(c);
      if (this.voices[0]) u.voice = this.voices[0];
      u.rate = 1.08; u.pitch = 1.15;   // brighter and a little brisker
      if (i === chunks.length - 1) u.onend = () => { if (this.speaking === (btn || true)) this.stopSpeaking(); };
      speechSynthesis.speak(u);
    });
  },
  /* Piper neural voice: one request per sentence or two, the next one is fetched
     while the current one plays, so speech starts fast and flows without gaps. */
  async speakNeural(text, btn) {
    const token = {}; this.token = token;
    this.speaking = btn || true; btn?.classList.add("speaking"); this.ui();
    const fetchAudio = c => fetch("/api/voice/speak", {method: "POST", headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
      body: JSON.stringify(c === this.PREVIEW ? {preview: true} : {text: c})}).then(r => r.ok ? r.blob() : Promise.reject(r));
    // a short first piece so speech starts quickly, then longer pieces (fewer joins)
    const sentences = text === this.PREVIEW ? [text] : (text.match(/[^.!?]+[.!?]+["')]*|\S[^.!?]*$/g) || [text]).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const chunks = sentences.length ? [sentences[0]] : [];
    for (const s of sentences.slice(1)) {
      const lastIdx = chunks.length - 1;
      if (lastIdx >= 1 && (chunks[lastIdx] + " " + s).length <= 420) chunks[lastIdx] += " " + s;
      else chunks.push(s);
    }
    // gapless playback: each piece is scheduled to start exactly when the previous one ends
    const ctx = this.ctx || (this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)());
    if (ctx.state === "suspended") await ctx.resume();
    let at = ctx.currentTime + 0.05, last = null;
    let next = chunks.length ? fetchAudio(chunks[0]) : null;
    try {
      for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) {
        const blob = await next;
        if (this.token !== token) return;
        next = i + 1 < chunks.length ? fetchAudio(chunks[i + 1]) : null;
        const buf = await ctx.decodeAudioData(await blob.arrayBuffer());
        if (this.token !== token) return;
        const src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.connect(ctx.destination);
        at = Math.max(at, ctx.currentTime + 0.02);
        src.start(at); at += buf.duration;
        (this.sources || (this.sources = [])).push(src); last = src;
      }
      if (last) await new Promise(res => { last.onended = res; });
    } catch (e) {
      if (this.token === token) {
        // the neural voice failed: switch to the Windows voice for the rest of this session
        if (S.status && S.status.voice) S.status.voice.tts = false;
        toast("The offline neural voice isn't working, so I'm using the Windows voice.");
        this.token = null; this.speaking = null; btn?.classList.remove("speaking");
        return this.speak(text, btn);
      }
    }
    if (this.token === token) this.stopSpeaking();
  },
  /* ---------- speaking WHILE the answer is being written (voice mode)
     feed() is called with the whole answer so far; each finished sentence is sent to
     the voice straight away, so you hear the start within seconds instead of waiting
     for the full answer. */
  feed(md, final) {
    if (!this.live) { this.stopSpeaking(); this.live = {token: {}, pos: 0, queue: [], done: false, btn: null, at: 0, next: null}; this.token = this.live.token; this.speaking = true; this.ui(); }
    const L = this.live;
    if ((md.match(/```/g) || []).length % 2) md = md.slice(0, md.lastIndexOf("```"));   // skip half-written code
    const rest = this.clean(md).slice(L.pos);
    let take = "";
    if (final) take = rest;
    else {
      const cut = [...rest.matchAll(/[.!?]["')]*\s/g)].pop();             // last finished sentence
      if (cut && cut.index + cut[0].length >= (L.pos ? 60 : 12)) take = rest.slice(0, cut.index + cut[0].length);
    }
    if (take.trim()) { L.queue.push(take.trim()); L.pos += take.length; }
    if (final) L.done = true;
    this.pump();
  },
  async pump() {
    const L = this.live; if (!L || L.busy) return;
    L.busy = true;
    const neural = S.status && S.status.voice && S.status.voice.tts;
    try {
      while (L.queue.length && this.token === L.token) {
        const piece = L.queue.shift();
        if (!neural) {                                       // Windows voice
          const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(piece);
          if (this.voices[0]) u.voice = this.voices[0];
          u.rate = 1.08; u.pitch = 1.15;
          u.onend = () => { if (L.done && !L.queue.length && !speechSynthesis.pending && this.token === L.token) this.stopSpeaking(); };
          speechSynthesis.speak(u); continue;
        }
        const res = await fetch("/api/voice/speak", {method: "POST", headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"}, body: JSON.stringify({text: piece})});
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("voice");
        const ctx = this.ctx || (this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)());
        if (ctx.state === "suspended") await ctx.resume();
        const buf = await ctx.decodeAudioData(await (await res.blob()).arrayBuffer());
        if (this.token !== L.token) return;
        const src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf; src.connect(ctx.destination);
        L.at = Math.max(L.at, ctx.currentTime + 0.03); src.start(L.at); L.at += buf.duration;
        (this.sources || (this.sources = [])).push(src);
        src.onended = () => { if (L.done && !L.queue.length && !L.busy && this.token === L.token && this.ctx.currentTime >= L.at - 0.05) this.stopSpeaking(); };
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (this.token === L.token) { toast("The reading voice failed, so this answer won't be read aloud."); this.stopSpeaking(); }
    } finally { L.busy = false; }
    if (L.done && !L.queue.length && !neural && !speechSynthesis.speaking && this.token === L.token) this.stopSpeaking();
    if (L.queue.length && this.token === L.token) this.pump();
  },
  attachButton(btn) { if (this.live && btn) { this.live.btn = btn; this.speaking = btn; btn.classList.add("speaking"); } },
  ui() {   // the floating "Stop talking" button
    let pill = $("#stopTalk");
    if (this.speaking && !pill) {
      pill = document.createElement("button"); pill.id = "stopTalk"; pill.className = "stoptalk";
      pill.innerHTML = '<span class="bars"><span></span><span></span><span></span></span>Stop talking <small>Esc</small><i></i>';
      pill.onclick = () => this.stopSpeaking();
      document.body.appendChild(pill);
    } else if (!this.speaking && pill) pill.remove();
  },
  stopSpeaking() {
    if (this.live && this.live.btn) this.live.btn.classList.remove("speaking");
    this.live = null;
    this.token = null;
    if (this.audio) { this.audio.pause(); this.audio = null; }
    if (this.sources) { this.sources.forEach(s => { try { s.stop(); } catch (e) {} }); this.sources = []; }
    if ("speechSynthesis" in window) speechSynthesis.cancel();
    if (this.speaking && this.speaking !== true) this.speaking.classList.remove("speaking");
    this.speaking = null;
    this.ui();
  },

  /* ---------- listening */
  async toggleRecording() {
    if (this.rec) return this.stopRecording();
    const vs = S.status && S.status.voice;
    if (vs && !vs.available) { toast(vs.reason); return; }
    this.stopSpeaking();
    let stream;
    try { stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio: {channelCount: 1, echoCancellation: true, noiseSuppression: true}}); }
    catch (e) { toast("Microphone blocked. Allow it in the browser's address bar, then try again."); return; }
    const ctx = new AudioContext();
    const src = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
    const proc = ctx.createScriptProcessor(4096, 1, 1);
    const chunks = [];
    proc.onaudioprocess = e => {
      const d = e.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0);
      chunks.push(new Float32Array(d));
      let peak = 0; for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 16) peak = Math.max(peak, Math.abs(d[i]));
      $("#recLvl").style.width = Math.min(100, peak * 160) + "%";
    };
    src.connect(proc); proc.connect(ctx.destination);
    const started = Date.now();
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - started) / 1000);
      $("#recTime").textContent = Math.floor(s / 60) + ":" + String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
      if (s >= 300) this.stopRecording();          // 5-minute safety limit
    }, 250);
    this.rec = {stream, ctx, proc, src, chunks, timer};
    $("#micBtn").classList.add("rec"); $("#micBtn").title = "Stop and turn speech into text";
    $("#recText").textContent = "Listening... click the microphone again when you finish";
    $("#recTime").textContent = "0:00"; $("#recbar").classList.remove("hidden");
  },
  async stopRecording() {
    const r = this.rec; if (!r) return;
    this.rec = null;
    clearInterval(r.timer);
    r.proc.disconnect(); r.src.disconnect(); r.stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
    const rate = r.ctx.sampleRate; r.ctx.close();
    $("#micBtn").classList.remove("rec"); $("#micBtn").classList.add("wait"); $("#micBtn").disabled = true;
    $("#recText").textContent = "Turning your speech into text..."; $("#recLvl").style.width = "0";
    try {
      const wav = this.toWav(r.chunks, rate);
      const res = await fetch("/api/voice/transcribe", {method: "POST", headers: {"Content-Type": "audio/wav"}, body: wav});
      const out = await res.json();
      if (!out.ok) toast(out.error || "Voice typing failed.");
      else if (!out.text) toast("I didn't catch anything. Try speaking a little closer to the microphone.");
      else {
        const input = $("#input");
        input.value = (input.value ? input.value.trimEnd() + " " : "") + out.text; grow(); input.focus();
        if (this.on && !S.busy) send();
      }
    } catch (e) { toast("Could not reach the app for voice typing."); }
    $("#micBtn").classList.remove("wait"); $("#micBtn").disabled = false;
    $("#micBtn").title = "Voice typing: click, speak, click again (offline)";
    $("#recbar").classList.add("hidden");
  },
  toWav(chunks, rate) {
    // join, shrink to 16 000 samples per second (what Whisper expects), save as 16-bit WAV
    let n = 0; for (const c of chunks) n += c.length;
    const all = new Float32Array(n); let o = 0; for (const c of chunks) { all.set(c, o); o += c.length; }
    const ratio = rate / 16000, len = Math.floor(all.length / ratio);
    const pcm = new Int16Array(len);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      const a = Math.floor(i * ratio), b = Math.min(all.length, Math.floor((i + 1) * ratio));
      let s = 0; for (let j = a; j < b; j++) s += all[j];
      const v = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, s / Math.max(1, b - a)));
      pcm[i] = v < 0 ? v * 0x8000 : v * 0x7fff;
    }
    const buf = new ArrayBuffer(44 + pcm.length * 2), dv = new DataView(buf);
    const str = (p, s) => { for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) dv.setUint8(p + i, s.charCodeAt(i)); };
    str(0, "RIFF"); dv.setUint32(4, 36 + pcm.length * 2, true); str(8, "WAVE"); str(12, "fmt ");
    dv.setUint32(16, 16, true); dv.setUint16(20, 1, true); dv.setUint16(22, 1, true);
    dv.setUint32(24, 16000, true); dv.setUint32(28, 32000, true); dv.setUint16(32, 2, true); dv.setUint16(34, 16, true);
    str(36, "data"); dv.setUint32(40, pcm.length * 2, true);
    new Int16Array(buf, 44).set(pcm);
    return new Blob([buf], {type: "audio/wav"});
  },
};
if ("speechSynthesis" in window) { Voice.loadVoices(); speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = () => Voice.loadVoices(); }
document.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Escape" && Voice.speaking) { e.preventDefault(); Voice.stopSpeaking(); } });
$("#micBtn").onclick = () => Voice.toggleRecording();
$("#voiceBtn").onclick = () => { Voice.setMode(!Voice.on); toast(Voice.on ? "Voice mode on: speak with the microphone, answers are read aloud" : "Voice mode off"); };
Voice.setMode(Voice.on);

/* ---------- choosing the reading voice */
async function loadVoiceMenu() {
  let r; try { r = await api("/api/voices"); } catch (e) { return; }
  const sel = $("#voiceSel"), c = r.choice;
  const groups = {};
  for (const o of r.options) (groups[o.group] = groups[o.group] || []).push(o);
  sel.innerHTML = `<option value="auto|">Automatic - best voice available</option>` +
    Object.entries(groups).map(([g, list]) => `<optgroup label="${esc(g)}">` +
      list.map(o => `<option value="${o.engine}|${esc(String(o.id))}">${esc(o.name)}</option>`).join("") + "</optgroup>").join("");
  const current = c.engine === "auto" ? "auto|" : c.engine === "kokoro" ? `kokoro|${c.kokoro_sid}`
    : c.engine === "piper" ? `piper|${c.piper_voice}` : "windows|windows";
  sel.value = [...sel.options].some(o => o.value === current) ? current : "auto|";
  sel.title = "Reading voice" + (r.active ? ` - now using ${r.active === "kokoro" ? "Kokoro" : "Piper"}` : " - now using the Windows voice") +
    (r.kokoro && r.kokoro.disabled ? " (Kokoro was too slow, so Automatic switched to Piper)" : "");
}
$("#voiceSel").onchange = async e => {
  const [engine, id] = e.target.value.split("|");
  const r = await api("/api/voices", "POST", {engine, id: id || null});
  if (r.ok === false) { toast(r.error); return; }
  await refreshStatus();
  loadVoiceMenu();
  toast(engine === "kokoro" ? "Kokoro voice chosen. Loading it into memory (a few seconds the first time), then it speaks quickly." : "Voice changed");
};
function previewVoice() {
  if (!(S.status && S.status.voice && S.status.voice.tts)) {   // Windows voice: speak it in the page
    Voice.speak(`Hi ${(S.status && S.status.user_name) || "there"}! This is the Windows voice.`, null); return;
  }
  Voice.stopSpeaking();
  Voice.speakNeural(Voice.PREVIEW, $("#voicePrev"));
}
$("#voicePrev").onclick = () => Voice.speaking ? Voice.stopSpeaking() : previewVoice();
$("#voiceMenuBtn").onclick = e => { e.stopPropagation(); $("#voicePop").classList.toggle("hidden"); };
$("#voicePop").onclick = e => e.stopPropagation();
document.addEventListener("click", () => $("#voicePop").classList.add("hidden"));
loadVoiceMenu();

/* ================================================================ pictures (screenshots of questions) */
const Pics = {
  list: [],
  async add(file) {
    if (!file || !file.type.startsWith("image/")) return;
    if (this.list.length >= 4) { toast("Up to 4 pictures per message"); return; }
    const url = await new Promise((res, rej) => { const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = () => res(r.result); r.onerror = rej; r.readAsDataURL(file); });
    // shrink big photos so they send quickly; 2000 px keeps small print readable
    const img = new Image(); img.src = url; await img.decode();
    const scale = Math.min(1, 2000 / Math.max(img.width, img.height));
    const c = document.createElement("canvas"); c.width = Math.round(img.width * scale); c.height = Math.round(img.height * scale);
    const g = c.getContext("2d"); g.fillStyle = "#fff"; g.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height); g.drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    this.list.push(c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.9)); this.draw();
  },
  clear() { this.list = []; this.draw(); },
  draw() {
    const t = $("#thumbs"); t.classList.toggle("hidden", !this.list.length);
    t.innerHTML = this.list.map((src, i) => `<div class="thumb"><img src="${src}" alt="picture ${i + 1}"><button data-i="${i}" title="Remove">x</button></div>`).join("") +
      (this.list.length ? `<span class="thumbnote">${S.status && S.status.images && S.status.images.vision ? "The model will read and look at the picture" : "The text in the picture will be read and answered"}</span>` : "");
    $$("button", t).forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { this.list.splice(+b.dataset.i, 1); this.draw(); });
  },
};
/* ---------- documents attached to this chat (its context) */
const Docs = {
  list: [], queue: Promise.resolve(), busy: 0,
  set(list) { this.list = (list || []).slice(); this.draw(); },
  add(file) {
    if (file.size > 18 * 1048576) { toast(`${file.name} is too big (up to 18 MB per file)`); return; }
    const temp = {id: "up-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2), name: file.name, uploading: true};
    this.list.push(temp); this.busy++; this.draw();
    // one at a time, so a brand-new chat isn't created twice
    this.queue = this.queue.then(async () => {
      try {
        const data = await new Promise((res, rej) => { const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = () => res(r.result); r.onerror = rej; r.readAsDataURL(file); });
        const r = await fetch("/api/docs", {method: "POST", headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
          body: JSON.stringify({conv_id: S.conv, name: file.name, data})}).then(x => x.json());
        if (r.ok) {
          S.conv = r.conv_id;
          const others = this.list.filter(d => d.uploading && d !== temp);
          this.list = r.docs.concat(others);
          toast(`${file.name} added to this chat (${r.doc.words.toLocaleString()} words)`);
          loadHistory();
        } else { toast(r.error || "Could not read that file"); this.list = this.list.filter(d => d !== temp); }
      } catch (e) { toast("Could not reach the app to read that file"); this.list = this.list.filter(d => d !== temp); }
      this.busy--; this.draw();
    });
  },
  async remove(id) {
    if (!S.conv) return;
    try {
      const r = await fetch(`/api/docs/${encodeURIComponent(S.conv)}/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {method: "DELETE"}).then(x => x.json());
      this.list = r.docs || this.list.filter(d => d.id !== id);
    } catch (e) { toast("Could not reach the app"); }
    this.draw();
  },
  draw() {
    const bar = $("#ctxbar");
    bar.classList.toggle("hidden", !this.list.length);
    const size = d => d.uploading ? "reading" : (d.words >= 1000 ? Math.round(d.words / 100) / 10 + "k words" : d.words + " words");
    bar.innerHTML = this.list.length ? `<span class="lbl" title="Every answer in this chat can use these documents">Context for this chat:</span>` +
      this.list.map(d => `<span class="dchip${d.uploading ? " up" : ""}" title="${esc(d.name)}">${ICON.doc}<span class="nm">${esc(d.name)}</span>
        <span class="sz">${size(d)}</span>${d.uploading ? "" : `<button data-id="${esc(d.id)}" title="Remove from this chat" aria-label="Remove ${esc(d.name)}">&times;</button>`}</span>`).join("") : "";
    $$("button[data-id]", bar).forEach(b => b.onclick = () => this.remove(b.dataset.id));
  },
};
const isPic = f => f.type.startsWith("image/") && !/svg/.test(f.type);
async function takeFiles(files) { for (const f of files) { if (isPic(f)) await Pics.add(f); else Docs.add(f); } }
$("#attachBtn").onclick = () => $("#fileIn").click();
$("#fileIn").onchange = async e => { const fs = [...e.target.files]; e.target.value = ""; await takeFiles(fs); };
$("#input").addEventListener("paste", async e => {
  const files = [...(e.clipboardData?.files || [])];
  if (files.length) { e.preventDefault(); await takeFiles(files); }
});
$("#composer").addEventListener("dragover", e => { e.preventDefault(); });
$("#composer").addEventListener("drop", async e => {
  const files = [...(e.dataTransfer?.files || [])];
  if (files.length) { e.preventDefault(); await takeFiles(files); }
});

/* ================================================================ library page */
let bookTimer = null;
function fmtDur(s) {
  if (s < 90) return Math.max(1, Math.round(s)) + " sec";
  if (s < 5400) return Math.round(s / 60) + " min";
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600), m = Math.round((s % 3600) / 60);
  return h + " h" + (m ? " " + m + " min" : "");
}
// subjects under their group headings (Cybersecurity, Data & AI, ...); "only" limits a search to that subject
function catOptions(groups, have, selected, suffix = "") {
  const seen = new Set();
  let html = "";
  for (const g of groups || []) {
    const items = g.subjects.filter(c => !have || have.has(c));
    items.forEach(c => seen.add(c));
    if (items.length) html += `<optgroup label="${esc(g.group)}">` +
      items.map(c => `<option value="${esc(c)}"${c === selected ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(c)}${suffix}</option>`).join("") + `</optgroup>`;
  }
  const rest = [...(have || [])].filter(c => !seen.has(c));         // a subject not in any group
  if (selected && !seen.has(selected) && !rest.includes(selected)) rest.push(selected);
  if (rest.length) html += `<optgroup label="Your subjects">` +
    rest.map(c => `<option value="${esc(c)}"${c === selected ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(c)}${suffix}</option>`).join("") + `</optgroup>`;
  return html;
}
function fillBookCats(books, groups) {
  const sel = $("#bookCat"), keep = sel.value || store.get("bookCat", "auto");
  const have = new Set(books.filter(b => b.status === "ready").map(b => b.category));
  sel.innerHTML = `<option value="auto">Auto - best subject for each question</option>` + catOptions(groups, have, null, " only");
  sel.value = [...sel.options].some(o => o.value === keep) ? keep : "auto";
}
$("#bookCat").onchange = e => store.set("bookCat", e.target.value);
async function addBooks(files) {
  const r = await api("/api/books/add", "POST", {files});
  if (r.ok === false) { toast(r.error); return; }
  if (r.queued) toast(r.queued === 1 ? "Added to the queue" : r.queued + " books added to the queue");
  setTimeout(loadBooks, 300);
}
async function loadBooks() {
  const r = await api("/api/books");
  const p = r.progress;
  $("#progress").classList.toggle("hidden", !p.running && !p.messages.length);
  $("#progTitle").textContent = p.running
    ? `Adding book ${Math.min(p.done_books + 1, p.total_books)} of ${p.total_books}: ${p.book} - ${p.step}`
    : "Last run: " + (p.step || "done");
  $("#progBar").style.width = (p.running ? p.percent : 100) + "%";
  $("#progLog").textContent = p.messages.join("\n");
  const newOnes = r.books.filter(b => b.status === "new");
  $("#scanBtn").disabled = !newOnes.length;
  const total = newOnes.reduce((a, b) => a + (b.estimate_seconds || 0), 0);
  $("#scanBtn").textContent = newOnes.length ? `Add all new (${newOnes.length}${total ? ", about " + fmtDur(total) : ""})` : "All books added";
  fillBookCats(r.books, r.category_groups);
  const list = $("#bookList");
  list.innerHTML = r.books.length ? "" : '<div class="empty">No books yet. Click "Open books folder", copy your books in, then press Add on each one.</div>';
  let lastCat = null;
  for (const b of r.books) {
    if (b.category !== lastCat) {
      lastCat = b.category;
      const n = r.books.filter(x => x.category === lastCat).length;
      const h = document.createElement("div"); h.className = "cathead";
      h.innerHTML = `${esc(lastCat)} <span>${n} book${n > 1 ? "s" : ""}</span>`;
      list.appendChild(h);
    }
    const d = document.createElement("div"); d.className = "card";
    const state = b.status === "ready" ? `<span class="badge ok">Ready</span>`
      : b.status === "adding" ? `<span class="badge new">Adding... ${p.percent || 0}%</span>`
      : b.status === "queued" ? `<span class="badge">Queued</span>`
      : `<button class="btn primary small" data-add>Add</button>`;
    d.innerHTML = `<div class="grow"><div class="title" title="${esc(b.file)}">${esc(b.title)}</div>
      <div class="meta">${b.status === "ready"
        ? `${b.size_mb != null ? b.size_mb + " MB - " : ""}${b.pages} pages - ${b.passages} passages - added ${esc(b.added)}${b.seconds ? " in " + fmtDur(b.seconds) : ""}${b.empty_pages > 5 ? " - " + b.empty_pages + " pages without text" : ""}`
        : `${b.size_mb != null ? b.size_mb + " MB" : ""}${b.pages ? " - " + b.pages + " pages" : ""}${b.estimate_seconds ? ` - <b class="est">about ${fmtDur(b.estimate_seconds)} to add</b>` : ""}`}</div></div>
      <select class="catsel" title="Subject - helps the assistant choose which books to consult">${catOptions(r.category_groups, null, b.category)}<option value="__new">+ New subject...</option></select>
      ${state}`;
    d.querySelector(".catsel").onchange = async e => {
      let category = e.target.value;
      if (category === "__new") {
        category = (window.prompt("Name the new subject (for example: Novels by Chinua Achebe, Church notes, MSM 112):", "") || "").trim();
        if (!category) { e.target.value = b.category; return; }
      }
      const res = await api("/api/books/category", "POST", {file: b.file, category});
      if (res.ok === false) toast(res.error); else { toast("Subject updated"); loadBooks(); }
    };
    const add = d.querySelector("[data-add]");
    if (add) add.onclick = () => { add.disabled = true; add.textContent = "..."; addBooks([b.file]); };
    list.appendChild(d);
  }
  clearTimeout(bookTimer);
  if (p.running || (r.queue && r.queue.length)) bookTimer = setTimeout(loadBooks, 1000); else refreshStatus();
}
$("#scanBtn").onclick = async () => {
  const r = await api("/api/books");
  addBooks(r.books.filter(b => b.status === "new").map(b => b.file));
};
api("/api/books").then(r => fillBookCats(r.books)).catch(() => {});

async function loadMemory() {
  const r = await api("/api/memory");
  $("#autoLearn").checked = !!(S.status && S.status.auto_learn);
  const list = $("#memList");
  list.innerHTML = r.memories.length ? "" : '<div class="empty">Nothing remembered yet. Chat a little, or add a fact above.</div>';
  for (const m of r.memories) {
    const d = document.createElement("div"); d.className = "card";
    d.innerHTML = `<div class="grow"><div class="memtext">${esc(m.text)}</div>
      <div class="meta">${m.source === "auto" ? "Learned from chat" : "Added by you"} - ${esc(m.created)}</div></div>
      <button class="act" data-a="edit">Edit</button><button class="act" data-a="del">Delete</button>`;
    const t = d.querySelector(".memtext");
    d.querySelector('[data-a="edit"]').onclick = async e => {
      if (t.isContentEditable) {
        t.contentEditable = "false"; e.target.textContent = "Edit";
        await api("/api/memory/" + m.id, "PUT", {text: t.textContent}); toast("Saved");
      } else { t.contentEditable = "true"; t.focus(); e.target.textContent = "Save"; }
    };
    d.querySelector('[data-a="del"]').onclick = async () => { await api("/api/memory/" + m.id, "DELETE"); loadMemory(); refreshStatus(); };
    list.appendChild(d);
  }
}

async function loadModels() {
  const r = await api("/api/models");
  $("#modelsSub").textContent = `Every .gguf file in the models folder. Files over ${r.max_gb} GB are blocked. Switching is manual: press Load.`;
  const list = $("#modelList");
  list.innerHTML = r.models.length ? "" : '<div class="empty">No models found. Download a .gguf file into the models folder.</div>';
  for (const m of r.models) {
    const active = m.name === r.active;
    const d = document.createElement("div"); d.className = "card";
    d.innerHTML = `<div class="grow"><div class="title">${esc(m.name)}</div>
      <div class="meta">${m.size_gb} GB${m.reason ? " - " + esc(m.reason) : ""}</div></div>
      <span class="badge ${!m.allowed ? "bad" : active ? "ok" : ""}">${!m.allowed ? "Blocked" : active ? (r.state === "loading" ? "Loading" : "In use") : "Available"}</span>
      <button class="btn" ${!m.allowed || active ? "disabled" : ""}>Load</button>`;
    d.querySelector("button").onclick = async () => {
      await api("/api/models/load", "POST", {name: m.name}); toast("Loading " + m.name + "...");
      setTimeout(() => { loadModels(); fillModelSelect(); refreshStatus(); }, 500);
    };
    list.appendChild(d);
  }
}

function applyWall(s) {
  if (!s) return;
  document.body.classList.toggle("has-wall", !!s.wallpaper);
  const url = s.wallpaper ? `url("/api/wallpaper?v=${s.wallpaper}")` : "";
  $("#wall").style.backgroundImage = url;
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--wall-dim", (s.wall_dim ?? 35) / 100);
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--wall-blur", (s.wall_blur ?? 0) + "px");
  const prev = $("#wallPrev");
  if (prev) { prev.style.backgroundImage = url; prev.textContent = s.wallpaper ? "" : "No wallpaper"; }
}
function loadPersonal() {
  const s = S.status || {};
  $("#nickIn").value = s.nickname || ""; $("#cfgName").textContent = s.config_name || "your name";
  $("#wallDim").value = s.wall_dim ?? 35; $("#wallDimV").textContent = ($("#wallDim").value) + "%";
  $("#wallBlur").value = s.wall_blur ?? 0; $("#wallBlurV").textContent = $("#wallBlur").value + " px";
  applyTheme(store.get("theme", "light")); applyWall(s);
}
