/* ================================================================ QuantaKai blob (the living logo)
   A watery body drawn on a small canvas: it wobbles, breathes, has a fine pixel
   texture, eyes that follow the cursor, and parts around the cursor when you sweep
   through it, then springs back. It stays where it is (not draggable).
   Cost: one animation loop for all blobs, paused when hidden or off screen. */
var Blobs = (() => {
  const all = [];
  const P = {x: -9999, y: -9999, vx: 0, vy: 0, t: 0};
  const reduce = window.matchMedia && matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
  let colors = null, running = false, last = 0;
  addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
    const now = performance.now(), dt = Math.max(8, now - P.t);
    P.vx = (e.clientX - P.x) / dt; P.vy = (e.clientY - P.y) / dt;
    P.x = e.clientX; P.y = e.clientY; P.t = now;
    if (reduce) all.forEach(b => b.draw(now / 1000, 0));
  }, {passive: true});
  addEventListener("pointerleave", () => { P.x = P.y = -9999; });
  const readColors = () => {
    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const get = (n, d) => (cs.getPropertyValue(n) || d).trim() || d;
    colors = [get("--accent", "#2ee6d0"), get("--accent2", "#8b7bff"), get("--accent3", "#ff6ad5")];
  };
  new MutationObserver(readColors).observe(document.documentElement, {attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["data-theme", "class"]});

  // ---- colours: Chat keeps the full mix, Offline turns teal, Online turns pink ----
  const rgbOf = (() => {
    const cx = document.createElement("canvas").getContext("2d"), cache = {};
    return c => cache[c] || (cache[c] = (() => {
      cx.fillStyle = "#000"; cx.fillStyle = c; const v = cx.fillStyle;
      return v[0] === "#" ? [1, 3, 5].map(i => parseInt(v.slice(i, i + 2), 16)) : v.match(/[\d.]+/g).slice(0, 3).map(Number);
    })());
  })();
  const mixRGB = (a, b, k) => a.map((v, i) => v + (b[i] - v) * k);
  const css = (c, a = 1) => `rgba(${c.map(Math.round).join(",")},${a})`;
  function palette(M) {
    const base = colors.map(rgbOf);
    const solid = c => [mixRGB(c, [255, 255, 255], 0.22), c, mixRGB(c, [0, 0, 0], 0.18)];   // one colour, light to dark
    let p = base;
    if (M.teal > 0.001) { const s1 = solid(base[0]); p = p.map((c, i) => mixRGB(c, s1[i], M.teal)); }
    if (M.pink > 0.001) { const s1 = solid(base[2]); p = p.map((c, i) => mixRGB(c, s1[i], M.pink)); }
    if (M.gold > 0.001) {   // Create mode: a warm sunset gold
      const s1 = [[255, 214, 102], [245, 158, 11], [234, 88, 12]];
      p = p.map((c, i) => mixRGB(c, s1[i], M.gold));
    }
    return p;
  }

  class Blob {
    constructor(canvas) {
      this.c = canvas; this.g = canvas.getContext("2d");
      this.size = +canvas.dataset.size || 40;
      this.N = this.size > 60 ? 48 : 28;
      this.off = new Float32Array(this.N); this.vel = new Float32Array(this.N);
      this.seed = Math.random() * 100;
      this.blinkAt = 2 + Math.random() * 3; this.blink = 0; this.squint = 0;
      this.visible = true; this.pokes = []; this.wasInside = false;
      this.mood = {smile: 0, mask: 0, glasses: 0, tie: 0, cube: 0, vib: 0, teal: 0, pink: 0, gold: 0, masq: 0, bolt: 0, mono: 0};
      const dpr = this.dpr = Math.min(2, devicePixelRatio || 1);
      canvas.width = canvas.height = Math.round(this.size * dpr);
      canvas.style.width = canvas.style.height = this.size + "px";
      this.g.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      if ("IntersectionObserver" in window)
        new IntersectionObserver(es => { this.visible = es[0].isIntersecting; wake(); }).observe(canvas);
    }
    targets() {
      // what the blob wears depends on the mode and answer style you picked
      const has = typeof S !== "undefined", mode = (has && S.mode) || "chat", style = (has && S.style) || "explain";
      const st = (mode === "chat" || mode === "create") ? "" : style;   // styles belong to Offline and Online only
      const v = (typeof Voice !== "undefined" && Voice.on) ? ((Voice.speaking || Voice.rec) ? 1 : 0.4) : 0;
      return {smile: mode === "chat" ? 1 : 0, mask: st === "code" ? 1 : 0, glasses: st === "teach" ? 1 : 0,
              tie: st === "teach" ? 1 : 0, cube: st === "quiz" ? 1 : 0, vib: v,
              teal: mode === "study" ? 1 : 0, pink: mode === "online" ? 1 : 0,   // colour follows the mode buttons
              gold: mode === "create" ? 1 : 0, masq: mode === "create" ? 1 : 0,
              bolt: st === "quick" ? 1 : 0, mono: st === "explain" ? 1 : 0};
    }
    step(t, dt) {
      const {N, off, vel, size} = this, R = size * 0.34;
      const goal = this.targets(), M = this.mood;
      for (const k in M) M[k] += (goal[k] - M[k]) * Math.min(1, 0.1 * dt);
      this.bph = (this.bph || 0) + dt * 0.01667 * (1.4 + 1.0 * M.vib);             // rhythm speeds up smoothly while talking
      const beat = Math.pow(0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(this.bph * Math.PI * 2), 2);
      const rect = this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cx = rect.left + rect.width / 2, cy = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
      const lx = P.x - cx, ly = P.y - cy, ld = Math.hypot(lx, ly), la = Math.atan2(ly, lx);
      const speed = Math.min(3, Math.hypot(P.vx, P.vy));
      let inside = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        const a = (i / N) * Math.PI * 2;
        // idle wobble + breathing (the watery motion)
        const rest = R * (0.045 * Math.sin(t * 1.7 + a * 3 + this.seed) + 0.03 * Math.sin(t * 1.1 - a * 2)
                       + 0.025 * Math.sin(t * 0.9)) * (1 - 0.65 * M.cube)
                   + R * M.vib * (0.05 * beat + 0.022 * Math.sin(a * 7 + t * 17) * (0.4 + 0.6 * beat));
        let force = (rest - off[i]) * 0.09;
        // the cursor parts the body: points near the cursor are pushed in to the cursor's distance
        const edge = R + off[i];
        if (ld < edge + R * 0.25) {
          let da = Math.abs(a - la); da = Math.min(da, Math.PI * 2 - da);
          const spread = Math.min(1.3, (R * 0.55) / Math.max(ld, R * 0.25));
          if (da < spread) {
            const fall = 1 - da / spread;
            const targetEdge = Math.max(R * 0.18, ld - R * 0.06);
            if (targetEdge < edge) force += (targetEdge - edge) * 0.35 * fall * fall * (0.6 + 0.4 * Math.min(1, speed * 2));
            if (ld < edge) inside = true;
          }
        }
        // neighbours pull together, so the surface stays smooth like water
        const nb = off[(i + 1) % N] + off[(i - 1 + N) % N] - 2 * off[i];
        vel[i] = (vel[i] + force * dt + nb * 0.08 * dt) * Math.pow(0.82, dt);
        off[i] = Math.max(-R * 0.82, Math.min(R * 0.22, off[i] + vel[i] * dt));   // limit stretching
      }
      if (inside && !this.wasInside) { this.pokes.push(t); if (this.pokes.length > 4) this.pokes.shift(); }
      this.wasInside = inside;
      this.squint += ((inside ? 1 : 0) - this.squint) * 0.25 * dt;
      if (t > this.blinkAt) { this.blink = 1; this.blinkAt = t + 2.5 + Math.random() * 4; }
      this.blink = Math.max(0, this.blink - 0.12 * dt);
      this.look = {lx, ly, ld};
    }
    path(R, cx, cy) {
      const {N, off, g} = this, pts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        const a = (i / N) * Math.PI * 2, c = this.mood ? this.mood.cube : 0;
        const sq = c > 0.001 ? 0.9 / Math.pow(Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.cos(a)), 4) + Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.sin(a)), 4), 0.25) : 1;
        const r = R * (1 + (sq - 1) * c) + off[i];
        pts.push([cx + Math.cos(a) * r, cy + Math.sin(a) * r]);
      }
      g.beginPath();
      const mid = (p, q) => [(p[0] + q[0]) / 2, (p[1] + q[1]) / 2];
      let m = mid(pts.at(-1), pts[0]); g.moveTo(m[0], m[1]);
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { const p = pts[i], q = pts[(i + 1) % N], n = mid(p, q); g.quadraticCurveTo(p[0], p[1], n[0], n[1]); }
      g.closePath();
    }
    draw(t, dt) {
      if (!colors) readColors();
      const {g, size} = this, R = size * 0.34, cx = size / 2, cy = size / 2;
      g.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);
      const pal = this.pal = palette(this.mood);
      // soft glow
      const glow = g.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, R * 0.6, cx, cy, size * 0.5);
      glow.addColorStop(0, css(pal[1], 0.33)); glow.addColorStop(1, css(pal[1], 0));
      g.fillStyle = glow; g.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
      // body: slowly turning teal -> violet -> pink
      this.path(R, cx, cy);
      const ang = t * 0.4, gx = Math.cos(ang) * R, gy = Math.sin(ang) * R;
      const body = g.createLinearGradient(cx - gx, cy - gy, cx + gx, cy + gy);
      body.addColorStop(0, css(pal[0])); body.addColorStop(0.55, css(pal[1])); body.addColorStop(1, css(pal[2]));
      g.fillStyle = body; g.fill();
      // tiny cubic pixel pattern, shimmering inside the body
      g.save(); g.clip();
      const cell = Math.max(2, Math.round(size / 24));
      for (let y = 0; y < size; y += cell) for (let x = 0; x < size; x += cell) {
        const n = Math.sin(x * 0.37 + y * 0.53 + t * 2.1) * Math.sin(x * 0.11 - y * 0.29 - t * 1.3);
        if (n > 0.25) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${(n - 0.25) * 0.32})`; g.fillRect(x, y, cell - 1, cell - 1); }
        else if (n < -0.55) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(10,14,30,${(-n - 0.55) * 0.35})`; g.fillRect(x, y, cell - 1, cell - 1); }
      }
      // glossy highlight
      const hi = g.createRadialGradient(cx - R * 0.38, cy - R * 0.45, 0, cx - R * 0.38, cy - R * 0.45, R * 0.75);
      hi.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,.55)"); hi.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,255,255,0)");
      g.fillStyle = hi; g.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
      g.restore();
      g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 60); g.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,.28)"; this.path(R, cx, cy); g.stroke();
      // eyes that follow the cursor
      const L = this.look || {lx: 0, ly: 0, ld: 1};
      const k = Math.min(1, L.ld / (size * 3)), ux = L.ld ? L.lx / L.ld : 0, uy = L.ld ? L.ly / L.ld : 0;
      const ex = R * 0.34, ey = -R * 0.06 + (this.off[Math.round(this.N * 0.75)] || 0) * 0.4;
      const rx = R * 0.17, ry = R * 0.21 * (1 - this.blink * 0.9);
      const M = this.mood, ink = "#0b1020", lw = Math.max(1, size / 50), eyeY = cy + ey;
      // CREATE mode: an original "night masquerade" half-mask - obsidian, gold trim,
      // swept-up wing tips and twinkling stars - drawn under the eyes so they look through it
      if (M.masq > 0.02) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.masq;
        const w = R * 0.92, top = eyeY - R * 0.2, bot = eyeY + R * 0.2, lift = R * (0.42 + 0.03 * Math.sin(t * 2));
        g.beginPath();
        g.moveTo(cx, top + R * 0.05);
        g.bezierCurveTo(cx + w * 0.35, top - R * 0.08, cx + w * 0.8, top - R * 0.02, cx + w * 1.05, eyeY - lift);   // right wing tip
        g.bezierCurveTo(cx + w * 1.0, eyeY - R * 0.05, cx + w * 0.9, bot, cx + w * 0.55, bot);
        g.bezierCurveTo(cx + w * 0.3, bot, cx + w * 0.12, bot - R * 0.02, cx, bot - R * 0.12);                       // nose dip
        g.bezierCurveTo(cx - w * 0.12, bot - R * 0.02, cx - w * 0.3, bot, cx - w * 0.55, bot);
        g.bezierCurveTo(cx - w * 0.9, bot, cx - w * 1.0, eyeY - R * 0.05, cx - w * 1.05, eyeY - lift);               // left wing tip
        g.bezierCurveTo(cx - w * 0.8, top - R * 0.02, cx - w * 0.35, top - R * 0.08, cx, top + R * 0.05);
        g.closePath();
        const mg = g.createLinearGradient(cx, top - R * 0.3, cx, bot);
        mg.addColorStop(0, "#2a1b3d"); mg.addColorStop(1, "#120b1c");
        g.fillStyle = mg; g.fill();
        g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 45); g.strokeStyle = "#f5c451"; g.stroke();
        // a fine gold filigree swirl on each wing
        g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.7, size / 90); g.strokeStyle = "rgba(245,196,81,.75)";
        for (const sd of [-1, 1]) {
          g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx + sd * w * 0.62, bot - R * 0.04);
          g.quadraticCurveTo(cx + sd * w * 0.95, eyeY - R * 0.02, cx + sd * w * 0.9, eyeY - lift * 0.7); g.stroke();
        }
        // twinkling four-point stars
        const star = (x, y, r, a) => {
          g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,236,170,${a})`; g.beginPath();
          for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const ang = i * Math.PI / 4, rr = i % 2 ? r * 0.28 : r; g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(ang) * rr, y + Math.sin(ang) * rr); }
          g.fill();
        };
        star(cx + w * 0.78, eyeY - lift * 0.55, R * 0.07, 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 3.1));
        star(cx - w * 0.7, eyeY - lift * 0.4, R * 0.055, 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 2.3 + 1));
        star(cx, top - R * 0.02, R * 0.05, 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 2.7 + 2));
        if (size > 60) {                                   // a curling quill feather on the right wing
          g.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,214,102,.9)"; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 70); g.lineCap = "round";
          const fx = cx + w * 1.02, fy = eyeY - lift, sway = Math.sin(t * 1.6) * R * 0.05;
          g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(fx, fy); g.quadraticCurveTo(fx + R * 0.18, fy - R * 0.25, fx + R * 0.08 + sway, fy - R * 0.5); g.stroke();
          g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, size / 140);
          for (let i = 1; i < 7; i++) {
            const k2 = i / 7, bx = fx + R * 0.13 * k2 + sway * k2 * k2, by = fy - R * 0.48 * k2;
            g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(bx, by); g.lineTo(bx + R * 0.09 * (1 - k2 * 0.5), by + R * 0.03); g.stroke();
            g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(bx, by); g.lineTo(bx - R * 0.07 * (1 - k2 * 0.5), by + R * 0.05); g.stroke();
          }
        }
        g.restore();
      }
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
        const x = cx + s * ex + ux * R * 0.05 * k, y = cy + ey + uy * R * 0.04 * k;
        if (this.squint > 0.5) {               // happy squint while you poke through it
          g.strokeStyle = "#0b1020"; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1.2, size / 34); g.lineCap = "round";
          g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y + ry * 0.35, rx * 0.9, Math.PI * 1.15, Math.PI * 1.85); g.stroke();
          continue;
        }
        g.fillStyle = "#ffffff"; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x, y, rx, Math.max(0.6, ry), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
        if (ry > rx * 0.3) {
          const px = x + ux * rx * 0.45 * k, py = y + uy * ry * 0.45 * k, pr = rx * 0.55;
          g.fillStyle = "#0b1020"; g.beginPath(); g.arc(px, py, pr, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
          g.fillStyle = "#ffffff"; g.beginPath(); g.arc(px - pr * 0.35, py - pr * 0.35, pr * 0.32, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
        }
      }
      if (M.masq > 0.02) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.masq; g.strokeStyle = "#f5c451"; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 60);
        for (const sd of [-1, 1]) { g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(cx + sd * ex, eyeY, rx * 1.35, rx * 1.15, sd * -0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); }
        g.restore();
      }
      // CODE style: an original white hacker mask - a smooth porcelain faceplate with glowing
      // slit eyes, faint circuit lines, a scan line and the odd digital glitch
      if (M.mask > 0.02) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.mask;
        const fw = R * 0.8, ftop = cy - R * 0.62, fbot = cy + R * 0.7;
        const plate = () => {
          g.beginPath();
          g.moveTo(cx, ftop);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx + fw * 0.95, ftop, cx + fw * 1.02, cy - R * 0.1, cx + fw * 0.9, cy + R * 0.18);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx + fw * 0.75, cy + R * 0.5, cx + fw * 0.3, fbot, cx, fbot);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx - fw * 0.3, fbot, cx - fw * 0.75, cy + R * 0.5, cx - fw * 0.9, cy + R * 0.18);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx - fw * 1.02, cy - R * 0.1, cx - fw * 0.95, ftop, cx, ftop);
          g.closePath();
        };
        plate();
        const pg = g.createRadialGradient(cx - R * 0.2, cy - R * 0.3, R * 0.1, cx, cy, R * 0.95);
        pg.addColorStop(0, "#ffffff"); pg.addColorStop(0.7, "#e8ecf3"); pg.addColorStop(1, "#b9c2d3");
        g.fillStyle = pg; g.fill();
        g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 70); g.strokeStyle = "rgba(20,30,50,.35)"; g.stroke();
        g.save(); plate(); g.clip();
        // faint circuit traces on both cheeks
        g.strokeStyle = "rgba(14,165,233,.45)"; g.fillStyle = "rgba(14,165,233,.6)"; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.7, size / 110);
        for (const sd of [-1, 1]) {
          const x0 = cx + sd * fw * 0.25, y0 = cy + R * 0.48;
          g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x0, y0); g.lineTo(x0 + sd * R * 0.12, y0 - R * 0.12); g.lineTo(x0 + sd * R * 0.42, y0 - R * 0.12);
          g.moveTo(x0 + sd * R * 0.2, y0 - R * 0.12); g.lineTo(x0 + sd * R * 0.3, y0 - R * 0.24); g.lineTo(x0 + sd * R * 0.5, y0 - R * 0.24); g.stroke();
          for (const [px, py] of [[x0 + sd * R * 0.42, y0 - R * 0.12], [x0 + sd * R * 0.5, y0 - R * 0.24], [x0, y0]]) {
            g.beginPath(); g.arc(px, py, Math.max(0.8, R * 0.025), 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
          }
        }
        // a thin seam down the middle
        g.strokeStyle = "rgba(20,30,50,.18)"; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx, ftop + R * 0.08); g.lineTo(cx, fbot - R * 0.05); g.stroke();
        // scan line sweeping down the faceplate
        const sy = ftop + ((t * 0.45) % 1) * (fbot - ftop);
        g.fillStyle = "rgba(57,255,140,.55)"; g.fillRect(cx - fw, sy, fw * 2, Math.max(1, size / 110));
        g.restore();
        // glowing slit eyes that still follow the cursor
        const open = 1 - this.blink * 0.85;
        for (const sd of [-1, 1]) {
          const x = cx + sd * ex + ux * R * 0.04 * k, y = eyeY + uy * R * 0.03 * k;
          const ew = rx * 1.45, eh = rx * 0.42 * open;
          g.beginPath();
          g.moveTo(x - sd * ew, y + eh * 0.2);
          g.lineTo(x + sd * ew * 0.9, y - eh * 1.1);
          g.lineTo(x + sd * ew, y + eh * 0.15);
          g.lineTo(x - sd * ew * 0.6, y + eh * 1.0);
          g.closePath();
          g.fillStyle = "#0b1020"; g.fill();
          g.shadowColor = "rgba(34,211,238,.95)"; g.shadowBlur = size / 10;
          g.fillStyle = `rgba(34,211,238,${0.75 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 4)})`;
          g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x + ux * rx * 0.25 * k, y + uy * rx * 0.1 * k, ew * 0.45, Math.max(0.5, eh * 0.38), sd * -0.32, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
          g.shadowBlur = 0;
        }
        g.restore();
        // now and then a quick digital glitch: two slices of the blob jump sideways
        const ph = (t + this.seed) % 3.2;
        if (ph < 0.12 && size > 30) {
          const d = this.dpr || 1;
          for (const [f, dx] of [[0.38, R * 0.08], [0.6, -R * 0.06]]) {
            const y0 = size * f, h = size * 0.06;
            g.drawImage(this.c, 0, Math.round(y0 * d), Math.round(size * d), Math.round(h * d), dx, y0, size, h);
          }
        }
      }
      // QUICK style: a crackling lightning bolt floating by the top of the head
      if (M.bolt > 0.02) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.bolt;
        const flick = 0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(t * 23) * Math.sin(t * 7), bob = Math.sin(t * 2.4) * R * 0.04;
        const bx = cx + R * 0.62, by = cy - R * 0.98 + bob, s2 = R * 0.62 * (0.85 + 0.15 * M.bolt);
        g.translate(bx, by); g.rotate(0.28);
        g.beginPath();
        g.moveTo(s2 * 0.12, -s2 * 0.5); g.lineTo(-s2 * 0.24, s2 * 0.06); g.lineTo(-s2 * 0.02, s2 * 0.06);
        g.lineTo(-s2 * 0.14, s2 * 0.5); g.lineTo(s2 * 0.26, -s2 * 0.08); g.lineTo(s2 * 0.03, -s2 * 0.08); g.closePath();
        g.shadowColor = `rgba(253,224,71,${0.9 * flick})`; g.shadowBlur = size / 8;
        const bg = g.createLinearGradient(0, -s2 * 0.5, 0, s2 * 0.5);
        bg.addColorStop(0, "#fff7b0"); bg.addColorStop(0.5, "#fde047"); bg.addColorStop(1, "#f59e0b");
        g.fillStyle = bg; g.fill();
        g.shadowBlur = 0; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 80); g.strokeStyle = "rgba(146,64,14,.7)"; g.stroke();
        if (size > 60 && flick > 0.93) {                     // tiny sparks now and then
          g.strokeStyle = "rgba(253,224,71,.9)"; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 110); g.lineCap = "round";
          for (const [a2, l] of [[-0.6, 0.22], [2.3, 0.18], [0.9, 0.16]]) {
            g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(Math.cos(a2) * s2 * 0.38, Math.sin(a2) * s2 * 0.38);
            g.lineTo(Math.cos(a2) * s2 * (0.38 + l), Math.sin(a2) * s2 * (0.38 + l)); g.stroke();
          }
        }
        g.restore();
      }
      // DETAILED style: a gentleman's monocle on the right eye and a curly mustache
      if (M.mono > 0.02 && this.squint < 0.5) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.mono;
        const mx = cx + ex + ux * R * 0.05 * k, my = eyeY + uy * R * 0.04 * k, mr = rx * 1.55;
        g.fillStyle = "rgba(220,240,255,.18)"; g.beginPath(); g.arc(mx, my, mr, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
        g.lineWidth = Math.max(1.2, size / 38); g.strokeStyle = "#c9a227"; g.stroke();
        g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, size / 120); g.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,.7)";        // glint
        g.beginPath(); g.arc(mx, my, mr * 0.72, Math.PI * 1.1, Math.PI * 1.45); g.stroke();
        if (size > 30) {                                                                   // the chain, swinging a little
          g.strokeStyle = "#c9a227"; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.8, size / 90);
          const sw = Math.sin(t * 1.8) * R * 0.05;
          g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(mx + mr * 0.7, my + mr * 0.7);
          g.quadraticCurveTo(mx + mr * 1.5 + sw, my + R * 0.55, mx + mr * 0.9 + sw, cy + R * 0.82); g.stroke();
        }
        // handlebar mustache
        const yy = eyeY + R * 0.3, mw = R * 0.42, th = R * 0.11, wig = Math.sin(t * 2.2) * R * 0.015;
        g.fillStyle = "#2b1a12";
        for (const sd of [-1, 1]) {
          g.beginPath();
          g.moveTo(cx, yy - th * 0.35);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx + sd * mw * 0.35, yy - th * 1.1, cx + sd * mw * 0.8, yy - th * 0.2, cx + sd * mw * 0.95, yy - th * 1.1 + wig);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx + sd * mw * 1.08, yy - th * 1.6 + wig, cx + sd * mw * 1.2, yy - th * 0.9 + wig, cx + sd * mw * 1.06, yy - th * 0.5 + wig);
          g.bezierCurveTo(cx + sd * mw * 0.95, yy + th * 0.55, cx + sd * mw * 0.4, yy + th * 0.75, cx, yy + th * 0.25);
          g.closePath(); g.fill();
        }
        g.restore();
      }
      // round glasses (teach style)
      if (M.glasses > 0.02) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.glasses; g.strokeStyle = ink; g.lineWidth = lw * 1.2;
        const gr = rx * 1.6, gy = eyeY;
        for (const sd of [-1, 1]) {
          g.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,.12)";
          g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx + sd * ex, gy, gr, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.stroke();
          g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx + sd * (ex + gr), gy - gr * 0.2); g.lineTo(cx + sd * R * 0.98, gy - gr * 0.45); g.stroke();
        }
        g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - ex + gr, gy - gr * 0.15); g.quadraticCurveTo(cx, gy - gr * 0.6, cx + ex - gr, gy - gr * 0.15); g.stroke();
        g.restore();
      }
      // little tie (teach style)
      if (M.tie > 0.02 && size > 30) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.tie;
        const top = cy + R * 0.56, len = R * 0.4 * M.tie, wdt = R * 0.11;
        g.fillStyle = "#1c2445";
        g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - wdt, top); g.lineTo(cx + wdt, top); g.lineTo(cx + wdt * 0.55, top + wdt * 1.1); g.lineTo(cx - wdt * 0.55, top + wdt * 1.1); g.fill();
        g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - wdt * 0.6, top + wdt * 1.1); g.lineTo(cx + wdt * 0.6, top + wdt * 1.1);
        g.lineTo(cx + wdt * 1.15, top + len); g.lineTo(cx, top + len + wdt * 0.9); g.lineTo(cx - wdt * 1.15, top + len); g.fill();
        g.strokeStyle = colors[0]; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, size / 70);
        g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - wdt * 0.8, top + len * 0.55); g.lineTo(cx + wdt * 0.8, top + len * 0.35); g.stroke();
        g.restore();
      }
      // friendly smile (chat mode)
      if (M.smile > 0.02 && this.squint < 0.5) {
        g.save(); g.globalAlpha = M.smile; g.strokeStyle = ink; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1.2, size / 40); g.lineCap = "round";
        const mw = R * 0.2 * (0.6 + 0.4 * M.smile), my2 = eyeY + R * 0.28;
        g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx + (L.ld ? L.lx / L.ld : 0) * R * 0.03 * k, my2 - mw * 0.55, mw, Math.PI * 0.18, Math.PI * 0.82); g.stroke();
        g.restore();
      }
      // little cheeks on the big one
      if (size > 60 && M.mask < 0.5) {
        g.fillStyle = css(mixRGB(rgbOf(colors[2]), [255, 255, 255], this.mood.pink * 0.5), 0.33 * (1 - M.mask));
        for (const s of [-1, 1]) { g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(cx + s * R * 0.55, cy + R * 0.22, R * 0.11, R * 0.06, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
      }
    }
  }
  // ---- The background: rings of text characters pulsing out from the big blob ----
  const RAMP = " .:-=+xX#8@";
  const fields = [];
  class Field {
    constructor(canvas) {
      this.c = canvas; this.g = canvas.getContext("2d"); this.visible = true; this.drawnAt = 0;
      this.cw = 9; this.ch = 15;
      this.resize();
      if ("ResizeObserver" in window) new ResizeObserver(() => this.resize()).observe(canvas);
      if ("IntersectionObserver" in window)
        new IntersectionObserver(es => { this.visible = es[0].isIntersecting; wake(); }).observe(canvas);
    }
    resize() {
      const r = this.c.getBoundingClientRect(), dpr = this.dpr = Math.min(1.5, devicePixelRatio || 1);
      this.w = Math.max(1, r.width); this.h = Math.max(1, r.height);
      this.c.width = Math.round(this.w * dpr); this.c.height = Math.round(this.h * dpr);
      this.g.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      this.cols = Math.ceil(this.w / this.cw); this.rows = Math.ceil(this.h / this.ch);
      this.atlas = null;
    }
    buildAtlas(pal) {
      // every character in every colour, drawn once; each frame just copies them (much faster than text)
      const {cw, ch, dpr} = this, a = document.createElement("canvas");
      a.width = Math.ceil(RAMP.length * cw * dpr); a.height = Math.ceil(colors.length * ch * dpr);
      const g = a.getContext("2d"); g.scale(dpr, dpr);
      g.font = `600 12px ui-monospace, "Cascadia Mono", Consolas, monospace`; g.textAlign = "center"; g.textBaseline = "middle";
      pal.forEach((col, ci) => { g.fillStyle = col; for (let i = 1; i < RAMP.length; i++) g.fillText(RAMP[i], i * cw + cw / 2, ci * ch + ch / 2 + 1); });
      this.atlas = a; this.atlasKey = pal.join();
    }
    draw(t) {
      if (!colors) readColors();
      const host0 = this.c.parentElement && this.c.parentElement.querySelector("canvas.qblob.big");
      const pal = host0 && host0._blob && host0._blob.pal ? host0._blob.pal.map(c => css(c)) : colors;   // same colours as the blob
      if (!this.atlas || this.atlasKey !== pal.join()) this.buildAtlas(pal);
      const {g, w, h, cw, ch, cols, rows, dpr, atlas} = this;
      g.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      const fr = this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
      const host = this.c.parentElement && this.c.parentElement.querySelector("canvas.qblob.big");
      const blob = host && host._blob;
      let cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2;
      if (host) { const br = host.getBoundingClientRect(); cx = br.left + br.width / 2 - fr.left; cy = br.top + br.height / 2 - fr.top; }
      const mx = P.x - fr.left, my = P.y - fr.top;
      const pokes = blob ? blob.pokes : [];
      const step = Math.min(0.2, Math.max(0, t - (this.lt || t))); this.lt = t;
      const ph = this.ph = (this.ph || 0) + step * (1.5 + (blob ? blob.mood.vib * 1.6 : 0));   // rings quicken in voice mode
      const th = document.documentElement.dataset.theme, light = th === "light" || (th !== "dark" && matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").matches);
      const base = light ? 0.75 : 0.55;
      const sw = cw * dpr, sh = ch * dpr, n = RAMP.length - 1, TAU = Math.PI * 2;
      for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
        const y = row * ch + ch / 2, dy = (y - cy) * 2.1;          // squashed: wide ovals like the video
        for (let col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
          const x = col * cw + cw / 2, dx = x - cx;
          const edge = 1 - Math.hypot(dx / (w * 0.5), (y - cy) / (h * 0.5));   // fade out towards the edges
          if (edge <= 0.02) continue;
          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          // rings travelling outwards, with a slow shimmer so they never look mechanical
          let ring = Math.max(0, Math.cos(d * 0.034 - ph)); ring *= ring; ring *= ring * ring;
          const shimmer = 0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(x * 0.012 + t * 0.5) * Math.sin(y * 0.03 - t * 0.35);
          let v = ring * shimmer * Math.exp(-d / 520);
          for (const p of pokes) {                                   // a splash when you cut through the blob
            const age = t - p; if (age > 1.8 || age < 0) continue;
            const r = age * 300, q = (d - r) / 26;
            v += Math.exp(-q * q) * (1 - age / 1.8);
          }
          const md = (x - mx) * (x - mx) + (y - my) * (y - my);
          if (md < 22500) v += 0.3 * (1 - md / 22500);              // glows a little around the cursor
          v *= Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (d - 70) / 70)) * Math.min(1, edge * 2.5);   // keep the blob itself clear
          if (y > cy + 95) v *= Math.max(0.35, 1 - (y - cy - 95) / 90);   // softer behind the heading text
          let level = Math.min(n, Math.floor(v * n * 1.15));
          if (level < 1) {                                           // faint dot grid everywhere else
            if (col % 5 || row % 2) continue;
            level = 1; g.globalAlpha = 0.18 * Math.min(1, edge * 2) * base;
          } else g.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, 0.3 + v) * base;
          let a = (Math.atan2(dy, dx) + t * 0.25) / TAU; a -= Math.floor(a);
          const ci = Math.floor(a * colors.length) % colors.length;
          g.drawImage(atlas, level * sw, ci * sh, sw, sh, col * cw, row * ch, cw, ch);
        }
      }
      g.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
  }

  function frame(now) {
    const t = now / 1000, dt = Math.min(3, (now - (last || now)) / 16.67) || 1;
    last = now;
    for (let i = all.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (!all[i].c.isConnected) all.splice(i, 1);
    for (let i = fields.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (!fields[i].c.isConnected) fields.splice(i, 1);
    const live = all.filter(b => b.visible);
    live.forEach(b => { b.step(t, dt); b.draw(t, dt); });
    fields.forEach(f => { if (f.visible && now - f.drawnAt > 33) { f.drawnAt = now; f.draw(t); } });   // 30 frames a second is plenty
    if ((live.length || fields.some(f => f.visible)) && !document.hidden && !reduce) requestAnimationFrame(frame); else { running = false; last = 0; }
  }
  function wake() { if (!running && !reduce && !document.hidden) { running = true; requestAnimationFrame(frame); } }
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", wake);
  return {
    refresh() {          // mode, style or voice changed: wake the animation (or redraw once if motion is reduced)
      if (reduce) all.forEach(b => { Object.assign(b.mood, b.targets(), {vib: 0}); b.draw(performance.now() / 1000, 0); });
      else wake();
    },
    mount(root = document) {
      $$("canvas.qblob", root).forEach(c => {
        if (c._blob) return;
        const b = new Blob(c); c._blob = b; all.push(b);
        b.step(performance.now() / 1000, 1); b.draw(performance.now() / 1000, 1);
      });
      $$("canvas.qfield", root).forEach(c => {
        if (c._field) return;
        const f = new Field(c); c._field = f; fields.push(f);
        requestAnimationFrame(() => { f.resize(); f.draw(performance.now() / 1000); });   // a still picture even with reduced motion
      });
      wake();
    },
  };
})();
Blobs.mount();
</script>
</body>
</html>

25. scripts\setup.ps1

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\scripts\setup.ps1283 lines14,088 bytesSHA-256 6b9eff55fe0289ce…

Automatic setup: portable Python and its packages, the llama.cpp CPU engine, both models, a speed test. Safe to run again.

  • Written in plain ASCII so Windows PowerShell 5.1 reads it correctly.
  • Downloads resume (curl.exe -C -); finished parts are skipped; a summary goes to logs\setup_report.txt.
scripts\setup.ps1283 lines
# setup.ps1 - one-time setup for QuantaKai. Run it through SETUP.bat.
# Safe to run again: anything already done is skipped, broken downloads resume.
#   1. Portable Python in runtime\python + numpy and pymupdf
#   2. AI engine: llama.cpp for Windows (CPU build) from GitHub
#   3. Models: the chat model and the search model from Hugging Face
#   4. Speed test: loads the chat model and times one answer
# Written in plain ASCII so Windows PowerShell 5.1 reads it correctly.

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$ProgressPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
$Root = Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path)
Set-Location $Root
[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12

$Report = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
function Say([string]$m) { Write-Host ("  " + $m) }
function Note([string]$m) { $Report.Add($m); Write-Host ("  " + $m) }

foreach ($d in 'books', 'models', 'engine', 'data', 'logs') {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Join-Path $Root $d) | Out-Null
}
$ReportPath = Join-Path $Root 'logs\setup_report.txt'
$Config = Get-Content (Join-Path $Root 'config.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$Report.Add("QuantaKai setup report - " + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm'))
$Report.Add("Folder: $Root")

# ===================================================================== 1. Python
# QuantaKai carries its own portable Python ("embeddable package") in runtime\python,
# so it never depends on the Python installed on the computer. That is what lets
# you move the folder anywhere.
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[1/4] Portable Python"
$RuntimeDir = Join-Path $Root 'runtime\python'
$Py = Join-Path $RuntimeDir 'python.exe'
$WheelDir = Join-Path $Root 'runtime\wheels'
$SitePackages = Join-Path $RuntimeDir 'Lib\site-packages'
$PyVersions = @('3.14.5', '3.14.4', '3.14.3', '3.14.2', '3.14.1', '3.14.0')

function Test-Runtime {
    if (-not (Test-Path $Py)) { return $false }
    & $Py -c "import numpy, pymupdf" 2>$null
    return ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0)
}

function Find-Python {
    foreach ($cmd in @('py', 'python')) {
        if (-not (Get-Command $cmd -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { continue }
        try {
            if ($cmd -eq 'py') { $out = & py -3 -c "import sys; print(sys.executable)" 2>$null }
            else { $out = & python -c "import sys; print(sys.executable)" 2>$null }
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and $out) {
                $p = ($out | Select-Object -Last 1).Trim()
                if (Test-Path $p) { return $p }
            }
        } catch { }
    }
    return $null
}

if (Test-Runtime) {
    Say "Portable Python already set up."
} else {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $RuntimeDir | Out-Null
    if (-not (Test-Path $Py)) {
        foreach ($v in $PyVersions) {
            $zip = Join-Path $Root "runtime\python-$v-embed-amd64.zip"
            Say "Downloading portable Python $v from python.org..."
            & curl.exe -L --fail --retry 3 -s -o $zip "https://www.python.org/ftp/python/$v/python-$v-embed-amd64.zip"
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
                Expand-Archive -Path $zip -DestinationPath $RuntimeDir -Force
                Remove-Item $zip -Force
                break
            }
            if (Test-Path $zip) { Remove-Item $zip -Force }
        }
    }
    if (Test-Path $Py) {
        # The ._pth file tells portable Python where to look for code:
        #   its own zip, its own folder, Lib\site-packages, and ..\.. (the QuantaKai folder).
        # All paths are relative, so they stay correct wherever the folder is moved.
        $pth = Get-ChildItem -Path $RuntimeDir -Filter 'python3*._pth' | Select-Object -First 1
        $stdlib = Get-ChildItem -Path $RuntimeDir -Filter 'python3*.zip' | Select-Object -First 1
        if ($pth -and $stdlib) {
            Set-Content -Path $pth.FullName -Value @($stdlib.Name, '.', 'Lib\site-packages', '..\..', 'import site') -Encoding ASCII
        }
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $SitePackages | Out-Null
        # Install numpy and pymupdf by unpacking the .whl files shipped in runtime\wheels.
        foreach ($w in (Get-ChildItem -Path $WheelDir -Filter '*.whl' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
            Say ("Unpacking " + $w.Name + " ...")
            & $Py -c "import sys, zipfile; zipfile.ZipFile(sys.argv[1]).extractall(sys.argv[2])" $w.FullName $SitePackages
        }
        if (-not (Test-Runtime)) {
            # Fallback: ask any installed Python's pip to fetch packages that match the portable Python.
            $sysPy = Find-Python
            if ($sysPy) {
                $ver = (& $Py -c "import sys; print('%d.%d' % sys.version_info[:2])").Trim()
                Say "Fetching packages for Python $ver with pip..."
                & $sysPy -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check -q --upgrade --target $SitePackages `
                    --platform win_amd64 --python-version $ver --only-binary=:all: -r (Join-Path $Root 'requirements.txt')
            }
        }
    }
}
if (Test-Runtime) {
    $pv = (& $Py -c "import sys, numpy, pymupdf; print(sys.version.split()[0], '| numpy', numpy.__version__, '| pymupdf', pymupdf.__version__)").Trim()
    Note "PYTHON: OK (portable Python $pv)"
} elseif (-not (Test-Path $Py)) {
    Note "PYTHON: FAILED - could not download portable Python from python.org. Check the internet and run SETUP.bat again."
} else {
    Note "PYTHON: FAILED - numpy or pymupdf would not load. Read the messages above."
}

# ===================================================================== 2. Engine
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[2/4] AI engine (llama.cpp)"
$EngineDir = Join-Path $Root 'engine'
function Find-Server {
    $f = Get-ChildItem -Path $EngineDir -Recurse -Filter 'llama-server.exe' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1
    if ($f) { return $f.FullName }
    return $null
}
$Server = Find-Server
if ($Server) {
    Say "Engine already present."
} else {
    Say "Asking GitHub for the newest Windows CPU build..."
    try {
        $rels = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri 'https://api.github.com/repos/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases?per_page=20' `
                                  -Headers @{ 'User-Agent' = 'quantakai-setup' }
        $asset = $null
        foreach ($r in $rels) {
            $asset = $r.assets | Where-Object { $_.name -like 'llama-*-bin-win-cpu-x64.zip' } | Select-Object -First 1
            if ($asset) { break }
        }
        if ($asset) {
            $zip = Join-Path $EngineDir $asset.name
            Say ("Downloading " + $asset.name + " ...")
            & curl.exe -L --fail --retry 3 -o $zip $asset.browser_download_url
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
                Expand-Archive -Path $zip -DestinationPath $EngineDir -Force
                Remove-Item $zip -Force
                $Server = Find-Server
                if ($Server) { $Report.Add("ENGINE SOURCE: " + $asset.name) }
            }
        } else { Say "No Windows CPU build found in the latest releases." }
    } catch {
        Say ("GitHub download failed: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    }
    if (-not $Server) {
        $old = Join-Path $HOME 'Desktop\Schooly\python\QuantaKai\tools\llama.cpp'
        if (Test-Path (Join-Path $old 'llama-server.exe')) {
            Say "Using the older engine copy from the QuantaKai group project instead..."
            Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $old '*') -Destination $EngineDir -Recurse -Force
            $Server = Find-Server
            if ($Server) { $Report.Add("ENGINE SOURCE: copied from the group project (Schooly\python\QuantaKai\tools\llama.cpp)") }
        }
    }
}
if ($Server) { Note "ENGINE: OK ($Server)" }
else { Note "ENGINE: FAILED - download llama.cpp by hand (BUILD_GUIDE.html, Download the engine) and unzip it into the engine folder." }

# ===================================================================== 3. Models
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[3/4] Models"
function Test-Gguf([string]$Path) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { return $false }
    try {
        $s = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($Path)
        try {
            $b = New-Object byte[] 4
            if ($s.Read($b, 0, 4) -ne 4) { return $false }
            return ([System.Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetString($b) -eq 'GGUF')
        } finally { $s.Dispose() }
    } catch { return $false }
}
function Get-Model([string]$Name, [string[]]$Urls, [double]$MinMB, [string]$Label) {
    $dest = Join-Path $Root ("models\" + $Name)
    if ((Test-Gguf $dest) -and ((Get-Item $dest).Length / 1MB) -ge $MinMB) {
        Note ("{0}: OK (already downloaded, {1:N0} MB)" -f $Label, ((Get-Item $dest).Length / 1MB))
        return $true
    }
    $part = $dest + '.part'
    foreach ($u in $Urls) {
        Say "Downloading $Name (it resumes if the connection drops)..."
        & curl.exe -L --fail --retry 5 --retry-delay 5 -C - -o $part $u
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and (Test-Gguf $part) -and ((Get-Item $part).Length / 1MB) -ge $MinMB) {
            Move-Item -Force $part $dest
            Note ("{0}: OK ({1:N0} MB)" -f $Label, ((Get-Item $dest).Length / 1MB))
            return $true
        }
        if ((Test-Path $part) -and -not (Test-Gguf $part)) { Remove-Item $part -Force }
    }
    Note "${Label}: FAILED - download did not finish. Run SETUP.bat again to resume."
    return $false
}

$chatOk = Get-Model $Config.default_model @(
    'https://huggingface.co/bartowski/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-GGUF/resolve/main/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf'
) 1500 'CHAT MODEL (Qwen2.5 3B, about 1.9 GB)'

$embedOk = Get-Model $Config.embedding_model @(
    'https://huggingface.co/ggml-org/bge-small-en-v1.5-Q8_0-GGUF/resolve/main/bge-small-en-v1.5-q8_0.gguf',
    'https://huggingface.co/CompendiumLabs/bge-small-en-v1.5-gguf/resolve/main/bge-small-en-v1.5-q8_0.gguf'
) 20 'SEARCH MODEL (bge-small, about 37 MB)'

# ===================================================================== 4. Speed test
Write-Host ""
Write-Host "[4/4] Speed test"
if ($Server -and $chatOk) {
    $port = [int]$Config.chat_port
    $modelPath = Join-Path $Root ("models\" + $Config.default_model)
    $outLog = Join-Path $Root 'logs\speedtest.out.log'
    $errLog = Join-Path $Root 'logs\speedtest.err.log'
    $engineArgs = @('-m', ('"{0}"' -f $modelPath), '-c', "$($Config.context_window)", '-t', "$($Config.threads)",
                    '-ngl', "$($Config.gpu_layers)", '--parallel', '1', '--host', '127.0.0.1', '--port', "$port")
    $proc = $null
    try {
        $watch = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        $proc = Start-Process -FilePath $Server -ArgumentList $engineArgs -WorkingDirectory $Root `
            -RedirectStandardOutput $outLog -RedirectStandardError $errLog -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
        $ready = $false
        $deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds(240)
        while ((Get-Date) -lt $deadline) {
            try {
                $h = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "http://127.0.0.1:$port/health" -TimeoutSec 2 -UseBasicParsing -ErrorAction Stop
                if ($h.StatusCode -eq 200) { $ready = $true; break }
            } catch { }
            if ($proc.HasExited) { break }
            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
        }
        $watch.Stop()
        if (-not $ready) {
            Note "SPEED TEST: FAILED - the engine did not start. Read logs\speedtest.err.log"
        } else {
            Note ("MODEL LOAD TIME: {0:N1} s" -f $watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds)
            Say "Asking a test question (can take up to a minute)..."
            $body = @{
                messages = @(
                    @{ role = 'system'; content = 'You are a helpful tutor. Explain simply.' },
                    @{ role = 'user'; content = 'In about 80 words, explain what overfitting means in machine learning to a beginner.' }
                )
                max_tokens = 140
                temperature = 0.2
            } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5
            $sw = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
            $resp = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "http://127.0.0.1:$port/v1/chat/completions" -Method Post `
                -ContentType 'application/json' -Body $body -TimeoutSec 300
            $sw.Stop()
            $tokens = [int]$resp.usage.completion_tokens
            $tps = 0.0
            if ($resp.timings -and $resp.timings.predicted_per_second) { $tps = [double]$resp.timings.predicted_per_second }
            elseif ($sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -gt 0) { $tps = $tokens / $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds }
            Note ("SPEED TEST: OK - {0} tokens in {1:N1} s = {2:N1} tokens per second (processor only, {3} threads)" -f $tokens, $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, $tps, $Config.threads)
            if ($resp.timings -and $resp.timings.prompt_per_second) {
                Note ("READING SPEED: {0:N1} tokens per second" -f [double]$resp.timings.prompt_per_second)
            }
            $Report.Add("TEST ANSWER: " + ($resp.choices[0].message.content -replace "\s+", ' '))
            Write-Host ""
            Write-Host "  The model answered:"
            Write-Host ("  " + $resp.choices[0].message.content)
        }
    } catch {
        Note ("SPEED TEST: FAILED - " + $_.Exception.Message)
    } finally {
        if ($proc -and -not $proc.HasExited) { Stop-Process -Id $proc.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
} else {
    Note "SPEED TEST: SKIPPED (engine or chat model missing)"
}

try {
    $os = Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem
    $Report.Add(("FREE RAM AT END: {0:N1} GB" -f ($os.FreePhysicalMemory / 1MB)))
    $drive = Get-PSDrive -Name ($Root.Substring(0, 1))
    $Report.Add(("FREE DISK: {0:N1} GB" -f ($drive.Free / 1GB)))
} catch { }

$Report | Set-Content -Path $ReportPath -Encoding UTF8

26. scripts\setup_voice.ps1

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\scripts\setup_voice.ps1121 lines7,244 bytesSHA-256 ee563bd6b87b68a1…

Downloads whisper.cpp (whisper-cli), the Whisper base.en model, Piper and the en_US-hfc_female-medium voice into the voice folder.

  • Kokoro is optional and not downloaded by this script; the guide's Voice section gives its two downloads.
scripts\setup_voice.ps1121 lines
# setup_voice.ps1 - downloads OFFLINE voice typing for QuantaKai (run by VOICE_SETUP.bat)
#   1. whisper.cpp for Windows (whisper-cli.exe), about 4 MB
#   2. the Whisper "base.en" speech model, about 148 MB (for listening)
#   3. Piper + a bright US female voice, about 80 MB (for speaking)
# Both go in QuantaKai\voice. After this, voice typing never needs the internet.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$ProgressPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'   # makes downloads much faster in Windows PowerShell
[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12

$root  = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$voice = Join-Path $root 'voice'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $voice | Out-Null
$report = Join-Path $root 'logs\voice_setup.txt'
function Say($text) { Write-Host $text; Add-Content -Path $report -Value $text }
Set-Content -Path $report -Value "QuantaKai voice setup - $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')"

try {
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1. engine
    $cli = Get-ChildItem $voice -Recurse -Filter 'whisper-cli.exe' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1
    if ($cli) {
        Say "ENGINE: already installed ($($cli.FullName))"
    } else {
        Say 'ENGINE: finding the latest whisper.cpp release...'
        $rel = Invoke-RestMethod 'https://api.github.com/repos/ggml-org/whisper.cpp/releases/latest' -Headers @{ 'User-Agent' = 'QuantaKai' }
        $asset = $rel.assets | Where-Object { $_.name -eq 'whisper-bin-x64.zip' } | Select-Object -First 1
        if ($asset) {
            $url = $asset.browser_download_url; $tag = $rel.tag_name
        } else {
            # some releases (e.g. v1.9.5) ship source code only; v1.8.7 is the last with a Windows build we know of
            $url = 'https://github.com/ggml-org/whisper.cpp/releases/download/v1.8.7/whisper-bin-x64.zip'; $tag = 'v1.8.7'
        }
        Say "ENGINE: downloading whisper.cpp $tag..."
        $zip = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'quantakai-whisper.zip'
        $tmp = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'quantakai-whisper'
        Invoke-WebRequest $url -OutFile $zip -UseBasicParsing
        if (Test-Path $tmp) { Remove-Item $tmp -Recurse -Force }
        Expand-Archive $zip $tmp -Force
        $found = Get-ChildItem $tmp -Recurse -Filter 'whisper-cli.exe' | Select-Object -First 1
        if (-not $found) { throw 'whisper-cli.exe was not inside the download.' }
        Copy-Item (Join-Path $found.DirectoryName '*') $voice -Recurse -Force
        Remove-Item $zip -Force; Remove-Item $tmp -Recurse -Force
        Say 'ENGINE: OK'
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2. speech model
    $model = Join-Path $voice 'ggml-base.en.bin'
    if ((Test-Path $model) -and ((Get-Item $model).Length -gt 100MB)) {
        Say 'SPEECH MODEL: already downloaded'
    } else {
        Say 'SPEECH MODEL: downloading Whisper base.en (about 148 MB, a few minutes)...'
        $urls = @('https://huggingface.co/ggerganov/whisper.cpp/resolve/main/ggml-base.en.bin',
                  'https://huggingface.co/ggml-org/whisper.cpp/resolve/main/ggml-base.en.bin')
        $ok = $false
        foreach ($u in $urls) {
            try {
                Invoke-WebRequest $u -OutFile "$model.part" -UseBasicParsing
                if ((Get-Item "$model.part").Length -gt 100MB) { Move-Item "$model.part" $model -Force; $ok = $true; break }
            } catch { Say "  (could not use $u, trying the next source)" }
        }
        if (-not $ok) { throw 'The speech model could not be downloaded. Check your internet and run VOICE_SETUP.bat again.' }
        Say 'SPEECH MODEL: OK'
    }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. speaking voice (Piper)
    $piperDir = Join-Path $voice 'piper'
    if (Get-ChildItem $piperDir -Recurse -Filter 'piper.exe' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1) {
        Say 'VOICE ENGINE: already installed'
    } else {
        Say 'VOICE ENGINE: downloading Piper (about 22 MB)...'
        $zip = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'quantakai-piper.zip'
        Invoke-WebRequest 'https://github.com/rhasspy/piper/releases/download/2023.11.14-2/piper_windows_amd64.zip' -OutFile $zip -UseBasicParsing
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $piperDir | Out-Null
        Expand-Archive $zip $piperDir -Force
        Remove-Item $zip -Force
        if (-not (Get-ChildItem $piperDir -Recurse -Filter 'piper.exe' | Select-Object -First 1)) { throw 'piper.exe was not inside the download.' }
        Say 'VOICE ENGINE: OK'
    }
    # bright young US female voice first; the others are backups if a download fails
    $voices = @('en_US/hfc_female/medium/en_US-hfc_female-medium',
                'en_US/amy/medium/en_US-amy-medium',
                'en_US/lessac/medium/en_US-lessac-medium')
    $haveVoice = Get-ChildItem $voice -Filter '*.onnx' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { Test-Path ($_.FullName + '.json') }
    if ($haveVoice) {
        Say "VOICE: already downloaded ($($haveVoice[0].Name))"
    } else {
        $got = $false
        foreach ($v in $voices) {
            $name = Split-Path $v -Leaf
            $base = "https://huggingface.co/rhasspy/piper-voices/resolve/main/en/$v"
            try {
                Say "VOICE: downloading $name (about 60 MB)..."
                Invoke-WebRequest "$base.onnx.json" -OutFile (Join-Path $voice "$name.onnx.json") -UseBasicParsing
                Invoke-WebRequest "$base.onnx" -OutFile (Join-Path $voice "$name.onnx.part") -UseBasicParsing
                Move-Item (Join-Path $voice "$name.onnx.part") (Join-Path $voice "$name.onnx") -Force
                $got = $true
                if ($name -ne 'en_US-hfc_female-medium') { Say "  Note: set ""tts_voice"": ""$name"" in config.json" }
                break
            } catch { Say "  (could not download $name, trying the next voice)" }
        }
        if (-not $got) { throw 'No speaking voice could be downloaded. Check your internet and run VOICE_SETUP.bat again.' }
        Say 'VOICE: OK'
    }
    # say hello, to prove it works
    $piperExe = (Get-ChildItem $piperDir -Recurse -Filter 'piper.exe' | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
    $onnx = (Get-ChildItem $voice -Filter '*.onnx' | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
    $hello = Join-Path $voice 'hello.wav'
    "Hi! I'm your offline voice. Everything I say is made right here on your laptop." | & $piperExe --model $onnx --output_file $hello --length_scale 0.88 2>$null
    if (Test-Path $hello) { (New-Object Media.SoundPlayer $hello).PlaySync(); Remove-Item $hello -Force; Say 'VOICE TEST: OK (you should have heard it)' }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. quick test
    $cli = Get-ChildItem $voice -Recurse -Filter 'whisper-cli.exe' | Select-Object -First 1
    & $cli.FullName --help *> $null
    Say 'TEST: the speech engine starts correctly'
    Say ''
    Say 'Voice typing is ready. Restart QuantaKai (STOP.bat, then START.bat) and click the microphone.'
} catch {
    Say ''
    Say "PROBLEM: $($_.Exception.Message)"
    Say 'Nothing else was changed. Fix the problem (usually the internet connection) and run VOICE_SETUP.bat again.'
    exit 1
}

27. README.txt

Full path: C:\Users\Mulenga Mwamba\Desktop\QuantaKai\README.txt28 lines1,558 bytesSHA-256 79470198b07512d4…

The short reminder kept in the QuantaKai folder: start, stop, voice, and what each folder holds.

README.txt28 lines
QUANTAKAI  (personal offline chatbot)
=====================================

First time:   double-click SETUP.bat   (needs internet once, about 2.1 GB)
Every time:   double-click START.bat   (the chat page opens in your browser)
To quit:      close the black START window, or double-click STOP.bat
Voice typing: double-click VOICE_SETUP.bat once (needs internet, about 230 MB)

The folder is self-contained: it carries its own Python, engine and models.
You can move or copy the whole QuantaKai folder anywhere (another drive,
a USB disk, another Windows PC) and START.bat still works. Close it first.

Folders
  app\        the Python program (server, engines, library, memory, chat)
  ui\         index.html - the chat page you see in the browser
  books\      put PDF, EPUB, Word (.docx), PowerPoint (.pptx), .txt or .md files
              here, then press "Add new books" in Library
  models\     .gguf model files. Any file of 5 GB or less appears in the picker
  engine\     llama.cpp (llama-server.exe) - runs the models
  runtime\    portable Python (python\) and its two packages (wheels\)
  data\       your book index, memories and saved chats (never leaves the PC)
  logs\       setup report and engine logs, for troubleshooting
  scripts\    setup.ps1, used by SETUP.bat
  tests\      smoke_test.py checks the app without real models
  voice\      whisper.cpp + speech model for offline voice typing (VOICE_SETUP.bat)

The full step-by-step build guide is BUILD_GUIDE.html.
Back up the data\ folder to keep your memories, chats and book index.

28. tests\fake_llama_server.py

A stand-in for llama-server used only by tests: answers /health, chat (streamed and not) and embeddings with predictable fake data.

  • Started by the app instead of llama-server when QUANTAKAI_FAKE_ENGINE=1.
  • With QUANTAKAI_FAKE_DUMP set to a file path, it saves the last chat request there, so tests can inspect the exact prompt.

tests\fake_llama_server.py91 lines
"""fake_llama_server.py - a stand-in for llama-server, used only by the automatic tests.

It answers the same web addresses as the real engine (/health,
/v1/chat/completions, /v1/embeddings) with simple made-up results, so the
whole app can be tested without downloading any models. You never run this
yourself; tests\\smoke_test.py starts it for you.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import re
import sys
import time
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

args = sys.argv[1:]
PORT = int(args[args.index("--port") + 1])
EMBEDDINGS = "--embeddings" in args
DIM = 384


def vector(text: str) -> list[float]:
    text = text.replace("Represent this sentence for searching relevant passages: ", "")
    v = [0.0] * DIM
    for word in re.findall(r"[a-z]{3,}", text.lower()):
        h = int(hashlib.md5(word.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)
        v[h % DIM] += 1.0
    n = sum(x * x for x in v) ** 0.5 or 1.0
    return [x / n for x in v]


def answer_for(messages: list[dict]) -> str:
    last = messages[-1]["content"]
    if "Facts:" in last:
        return "- The user is studying data science at university." if "I am" in last or "I'm" in last else "- NONE"
    if "Book passages:" in last:
        return "Overfitting means a model memorises the training data instead of learning the pattern [1]. It then does badly on new data [1]."
    return "Hello! I am a test reply from the fake engine. Everything is connected."


class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _send(self, obj, code=200):
        b = json.dumps(obj).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)

    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path == "/health":
            self._send({"status": "ok"})
        else:
            self._send({"error": "nope"}, 404)

    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))) or b"{}")
        if self.path == "/v1/embeddings" and EMBEDDINGS:
            inputs = body["input"] if isinstance(body["input"], list) else [body["input"]]
            self._send({"data": [{"index": i, "embedding": vector(t)} for i, t in enumerate(inputs)]})
            return
        if self.path == "/v1/chat/completions" and not EMBEDDINGS:
            import os
            dump = os.environ.get("QUANTAKAI_FAKE_DUMP")
            if dump:                      # lets tests check what the app sent to the model
                with open(dump, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    json.dump(body, f)
            text = answer_for(body["messages"])
            if not body.get("stream"):
                self._send({"choices": [{"message": {"content": text}}]})
                return
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
            self.end_headers()
            for word in re.findall(r"\S+\s*", text):
                chunk = {"choices": [{"delta": {"content": word}}]}
                self.wfile.write(f"data: {json.dumps(chunk)}\n\n".encode())
                self.wfile.flush()
                time.sleep(0.02)
            final = {"choices": [], "timings": {"predicted_per_second": 9.5, "prompt_per_second": 60.0}}
            self.wfile.write(f"data: {json.dumps(final)}\n\n".encode())
            self.wfile.write(b"data: [DONE]\n\n")
            return
        self._send({"error": "wrong endpoint"}, 404)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    time.sleep(0.5)  # pretend to load a model
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", PORT), H).serve_forever()

29. tests\smoke_test.py

Checks the whole app in a temporary copy with the fake engine: start-up, the size limit, adding a book, study answers with sources, chat, memory, saved chats, security.

"""smoke_test.py - checks the whole app works, without real models.

Run it from the project folder:
    runtime\\python\\python.exe tests\\smoke_test.py

It copies the app into a temporary folder, uses the fake engine instead of
llama.cpp, makes a small test PDF, then tests: start-up, the 5 GB size limit,
adding a book, a study answer with sources, a chat answer, "remember that...",
auto-learn, the memory page and saved chats. Your real folders are not touched.
"""
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

SRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
PORT = 8765
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
failures = []


def check(name, condition, detail=""):
    print(("  PASS  " if condition else "  FAIL  ") + name + (f"  ({detail})" if detail and not condition else ""))
    if not condition:
        failures.append(name)


def call(method, path, data=None, timeout=30):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, method=method,
                                 data=json.dumps(data).encode() if data is not None else None,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
        return json.loads(r.read().decode())


def chat(message, mode="chat", style="quick", conv_id=None, simpler=False):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/chat", method="POST",
                                 data=json.dumps({"message": message, "mode": mode, "style": style,
                                                  "conv_id": conv_id, "simpler": simpler}).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    events = []
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as r:
        for raw in r:
            line = raw.decode().strip()
            if line.startswith("data: "):
                events.append(json.loads(line[6:]))
    return events


def wait_for(fn, seconds=30):
    end = time.time() + seconds
    while time.time() < end:
        try:
            if fn():
                return True
        except Exception:
            pass
        time.sleep(0.3)
    return False


def main():
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="quantakai-test-"))
    print(f"Testing in {tmp}")
    for item in ("app", "ui", "tests", "config.json"):
        src = SRC / item
        (shutil.copytree if src.is_dir() else shutil.copy)(src, tmp / item)
    cfg = json.loads((tmp / "config.json").read_text())
    cfg.update(ui_port=PORT, chat_port=8766, embed_port=8767,
               study_min_score=0.05)  # the fake engine's vectors score lower than real ones
    (tmp / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    models = tmp / "models"
    models.mkdir()
    (models / cfg["default_model"]).write_bytes(b"GGUF" + b"\0" * 1000)
    (models / cfg["embedding_model"]).write_bytes(b"GGUF" + b"\0" * 1000)
    (models / "broken-download.gguf").write_bytes(b"\0" * 1000)
    with open(models / "too-big-model.gguf", "wb") as f:   # 6 GB "sparse" file, uses no real disk space
        f.write(b"GGUF")
        f.truncate(6 * 1024 ** 3)

    import pymupdf
    books = tmp / "books"
    books.mkdir()
    doc = pymupdf.open()
    lines = ("Overfitting happens when a machine learning model learns the training data too closely, "
             "including its noise, so it performs badly on new unseen data. ") * 12
    for i in range(3):
        page = doc.new_page()
        page.insert_textbox(pymupdf.Rect(50, 50, 550, 800), f"Chapter {i + 1}. " + lines, fontsize=10)
    doc.save(books / "Intro_to_ML.pdf")

    env = dict(os.environ, QUANTAKAI_FAKE_ENGINE="1", PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")
    # Start the COPY's app (the portable Python always looks in the real folder first, so point it at the copy).
    boot = (f"import sys, runpy; sys.path.insert(0, r'{tmp}'); sys.argv = ['app.server', '--no-browser']; "
            "runpy.run_module('app.server', run_name='__main__')")
    server = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", boot], cwd=tmp, env=env)
    try:
        check("web page loads", wait_for(lambda: urllib.request.urlopen(BASE + "/", timeout=2).status == 200))
        check("chat model and search engine start",
              wait_for(lambda: call("GET", "/api/status")["chat"]["state"] == "ready"
                       and call("GET", "/api/status")["search"]["state"] == "ready"))
        listed = {m["name"]: m for m in call("GET", "/api/models")["models"]}
        check("5 GB limit blocks the 6 GB model", listed["too-big-model.gguf"]["allowed"] is False)
        check("broken download is blocked", listed["broken-download.gguf"]["allowed"] is False)
        check("search model hidden from chat picker", cfg["embedding_model"] not in listed)
        call("POST", "/api/models/load", {"name": "too-big-model.gguf"})
        check("loading the 6 GB model is refused",
              wait_for(lambda: "limit" in call("GET", "/api/status")["chat"]["error"], 10))
        call("POST", "/api/models/load", {"name": cfg["default_model"]})
        check("switching back to the 3B model works",
              wait_for(lambda: call("GET", "/api/status")["chat"]["state"] == "ready"))

        call("POST", "/api/books/scan")
        check("book gets added", wait_for(lambda: call("GET", "/api/status")["books"] == 1))
        wait_for(lambda: not call("GET", "/api/books")["progress"]["running"])
        book = call("GET", "/api/books")["books"][0]
        check("book has pages and passages", book.get("pages") == 3 and book.get("passages", 0) > 0, str(book))

        ev = chat("What is overfitting in machine learning?", mode="study", style="explain")
        types = [e["type"] for e in ev]
        sources = next((e["sources"] for e in ev if e["type"] == "sources"), [])
        text = "".join(e["text"] for e in ev if e["type"] == "token")
        check("study answer streams", types[0] == "meta" and "token" in types and types[-1] == "done", str(types))
        check("study answer has book sources with page numbers", sources and sources[0]["page"] >= 1, str(sources)[:200])
        check("study answer cites [1]", "[1]" in text, text)
        conv = next(e["conv_id"] for e in ev if e["type"] == "meta")
        ev2 = chat("", mode="study", style="quick", conv_id=conv, simpler=True)
        check("'simpler please' reuses the same sources",
              next((e["sources"] for e in ev2 if e["type"] == "sources"), None) == sources)

        ev = chat("Remember that my statistics exam is on 3 December")
        check("'remember that' saves a memory", any(e["type"] == "memory" and e["saved"] for e in ev))
        ev = chat("Hi! I am a second-year data science student.")
        check("chat answer streams", "".join(e.get("text", "") for e in ev).startswith("Hello"))
        check("auto-learn saves a fact",
              wait_for(lambda: any(m["source"] == "auto" for m in call("GET", "/api/memory")["memories"]), 15))
        mems = call("GET", "/api/memory")["memories"]
        target = mems[0]["id"]
        call("PUT", f"/api/memory/{target}", {"text": "The user likes short answers."})
        check("memory can be edited",
              any(m["text"] == "The user likes short answers." for m in call("GET", "/api/memory")["memories"]))
        call("DELETE", f"/api/memory/{target}")
        check("memory can be deleted", all(m["id"] != target for m in call("GET", "/api/memory")["memories"]))

        chats = call("GET", "/api/chats")["chats"]
        check("conversations are saved", len(chats) == 3, str(chats))
        saved = call("GET", f"/api/chats/{conv}")
        check("saved chat keeps sources", saved["messages"][1]["sources"] == sources)

        bad = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/status", headers={"Host": "evil.example:8765"})
        try:
            urllib.request.urlopen(bad, timeout=5)
            check("requests from other web sites are blocked", False)
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            check("requests from other web sites are blocked", e.code == 403)
    finally:
        server.terminate()
        server.wait(timeout=10)
        time.sleep(0.5)
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c",
                        "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, r'%s'); from app import engine; engine.kill_leftover_engines()" % tmp],
                       cwd=tmp, env=env)
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    print()
    print("ALL TESTS PASSED" if not failures else f"{len(failures)} TEST(S) FAILED: {', '.join(failures)}")
    sys.exit(1 if failures else 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

13Check every file

A typo anywhere can stop the app. This checker compares every file you created with the original using its SHA-256 fingerprint. Save it as tools\verify_files.py, then run it.

def fingerprint(path: Path) -> str:
    text = path.read_bytes().decode("utf-8-sig").replace("\r\n", "\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


bad = 0
for rel, want in EXPECTED.items():
    p = ROOT / rel
    if not p.exists():
        print(f"MISSING    {rel}")
        bad += 1
    elif fingerprint(p) != want:
        print(f"DIFFERENT  {rel}  (copy it again from the guide)")
        bad += 1
    else:
        print(f"OK         {rel}")
print()
print("ALL FILES MATCH" if not bad else f"{bad} file(s) need fixing")
sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

Every line must start with OK and the last line must say ALL FILES MATCH. For a DIFFERENT file, use its Save file button instead of copy and paste, then run the checker again.

14Test without models

This runs the whole app in a temporary folder with the pretend engine (tests\fake_llama_server.py), makes a small test PDF and checks each feature. It needs no real models and never touches your folders.

PowerShell - in the QuantaKai folder1 line
runtime\python\python.exe tests\smoke_test.py

Expected: 22 lines that say PASS, then ALL TESTS PASSED. A FAIL names the part that broke, usually a typo in the file of the same name.

15First real run

  1. Double-click START.bat. A black window says QuantaKai is running. Keep it open.
  2. The browser opens http://127.0.0.1:8000. The model picker shows Loading for a few seconds, then the model name.
  3. Chat: type "Hi, I'm Mwamba, a second-year data science student". The reply streams in; a few seconds later the Memory count goes up (auto-learn).
  4. Library: click Open books folder, copy in your books, then Add all new (or Add on one book). A progress bar and a time estimate show for each book.
  5. Offline: back in Chat, click Offline, keep Detailed, and ask a question from one of your books. The sources bar lists the exact pages; any [1] in the answer jumps to its source.
  6. Click Simpler please under an answer for a beginner version from the same pages.
  7. Online: ask about something recent; the answer cites numbered web pages.
  8. Create: choose Story or Character and give a prompt.
  9. Models: lists every .gguf; blocked files say why. Press Load, or use the picker at the top.
  10. Personalise: set a nickname (press Hear it to check how it sounds), theme and wallpaper.
  11. To quit: close the black window (the engines close with it) or double-click STOP.bat.
Shortcut: SETUP.bat

Once all files exist, SETUP.bat does the engine, model and portable Python steps for you: portable Python (+ the wheels in runtime\wheels), the newest llama.cpp CPU build, both models, and a speed test. Results go to logs\setup_report.txt. It is safe to run again.

Moving QuantaKai

Quit first, then move or copy the whole folder anywhere (another drive, a USB disk, another Windows PC). START.bat works from the new place with your books, memories and chats.

16Voice: talking and listening

Voice typing and the fast reading voice

Double-click VOICE_SETUP.bat once (internet, about 230 MB). It downloads whisper.cpp, the Whisper ggml-base.en.bin model, Piper and the en_US-hfc_female-medium voice into voice, plays a short test sentence, and writes logs\voice_setup.txt. Then restart QuantaKai.

By hand instead: unzip whisper-bin-x64.zip into voice; put ggml-base.en.bin in voice; unzip piper_windows_amd64.zip into voice\piper; put the voice's .onnx and .onnx.json in voice. (Or drop the zips in voice or new stuff: QuantaKai unpacks them at start-up.)

Kokoro: the most natural voice (optional)

  1. Download sherpa-onnx-v1.13.5-win-x64-shared-MT-Release.tar.bz2 from github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/tag/v1.13.5. Use v1.13.5 exactly: kokoro_worker.py copies that version's C structure layouts.
  2. Download kokoro-int8-multi-lang-v1_0.tar.bz2 from github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/tag/tts-models.
  3. Put both files in voice (or new stuff) and start QuantaKai: it unpacks them (about a minute). Or unpack yourself: tar -xf voice\kokoro-int8-multi-lang-v1_0.tar.bz2 -C voice and the same for sherpa-onnx.
  4. In the voice menu (the speaker button at the top) choose a Kokoro voice, for example Heart, and press Preview.
File QuantaKai looks for (anywhere inside voice)Used for
whisper-cli.exe + ggml-base.en.binVoice typing
piper\…\piper.exe + <voice>.onnx + <voice>.onnx.jsonFast reading voice
sherpa-onnx-offline-tts.exeKokoro, slow per-sentence fallback
sherpa-onnx-c-api.dllKokoro kept in memory by kokoro_worker.py (fast)
voices.bin, tokens.txt, model.int8.onnx, espeak-ng-data\The Kokoro model folder

Using voice

  • Voice typing: click the microphone, speak, click again. The words appear in the box (and are sent in Voice mode).
  • Voice mode (the speaker button): answers are read aloud sentence by sentence while they are written, and kept short (2 to 4 sentences).
  • Read aloud under any answer, or highlight any text on the page and choose Read aloud.
  • Stop: the Stop talking pill, or Esc.
  • Without Piper or Kokoro the page falls back to the Windows built-in voices.

17How each feature works

Chat mode and memory

  • memory.relevant() embeds your message and picks up to 4 stored facts scoring 0.55 or more; they are added to CHAT_SYSTEM under "Things you know about…".
  • "remember that …" is caught by a regular expression and saved at once; the reply event says "Saved to memory."
  • After the reply, a background job asks the model with MEMORY_EXTRACT for new facts (temperature 0, 120 tokens). Facts 0.92 similar to existing ones are skipped.
  • History: up to the last 12 exchanges, newest first, as many as fit in the token budget.

Offline mode (your books)

  • The question is spell-checked against the words in your books (search only), turned into a vector with the bge query prefix, and compared with every passage (one matrix multiplication).
  • The same question is scored with BM25 keywords. The two rankings are fused (RRF). Overlapping passages from the same pages are dropped.
  • Subject: the one chosen in the subject picker, or Auto, which keeps the best-matching subject plus any within 0.03 of it.
  • The top 4 passages scoring at least study_min_score (0.40), or with a strong keyword match, are numbered [1]-[4] with title and pages.
  • The style's rules (Detailed, Quick, Code, Teach, Quiz) tell the model to use only those passages and cite them, or say "I couldn't find this in your books."
  • Follow-ups under 6 words are searched together with the previous question. Simpler please reuses the same passages.

Adding a book

  • Text is pulled out page by page (PyMuPDF for PDF/EPUB; built-in XML readers for Word and PowerPoint).
  • All words are cut into 180-word passages that overlap by 40, each remembering its first and last page.
  • Passages are embedded 16 at a time; a passage the engine rejects is shortened and retried, so one odd page cannot fail the book.
  • Files saved: .jsonl, .npy, an entry in books.json, and an automatic subject. Time per book: about 4 to 12 minutes for a 300 to 900 page book on this laptop.

Online mode

  • web.gather(): DuckDuckGo HTML results, the top 3 pages fetched in parallel (8 s timeout each), the best paragraphs up to 170 words a page, numbered [1]-[3] with links.

Create mode

  • No books, no web. STORY_RULES (600-900 words, a bold title, continue on "continue") or CHARACTER_RULES (a fixed character sheet). Temperature 0.9, up to 1500 / 1100 tokens.

Pictures and documents

  • Pictures (paste, drag or the picture button, up to 4 per message): Windows OCR reads their text, which is added to the question; a vision model also sees the image.
  • Documents (the paperclip): read once by docs.py and saved with that chat. Every question in that chat gets the whole text if it fits, otherwise the most relevant 1000-character chunks.

Code, Teach and Quiz

  • Code: complete runnable code in a fenced block. Python and Java blocks get a Run button (POST /api/run); output appears under the code; Fix this error sends the error back.
  • Teach: a full lesson (goals, definition, formulas, worked examples, mistakes, 3 practice questions) or a project walkthrough.
  • Quiz: 6 multiple-choice questions and 8 flashcards in a fixed format; the page turns them into an interactive quiz and can save a printable study guide.

Retry, Edit and Stop

  • Each question and its answer share a turn tag. Retry and Edit send rewind= so the old answer is removed before the new one is written. Stop closes the connection; the engine stops writing and the text so far is kept.

18HTTP API reference

All addresses are on http://127.0.0.1:8000. Requests must carry Host: 127.0.0.1:8000 (or localhost:8000); POST, PUT and DELETE are refused (403) if an Origin header from another site is present. Bodies are JSON unless stated.

MethodPathBodyAnswer
GET/-The page (ui/index.html)
GET/api/status-app_name, user_name, nickname, config_name, wallpaper, wall_dim, wall_blur, chat{state,model,error}, search{state,error}, books, passages, memories, auto_learn, max_model_gb, voice{…}, run{python,java}, images{ocr,vision}
GET/api/models-models[{name,size_gb,allowed,reason}], active, state, max_gb
POST/api/models/load{name}Starts loading in the background: {ok}
GET/api/books-books[…], progress{running,book,done_books,total_books,step,percent,messages}, categories, category_groups, queue
POST/api/books/scan-Queue every new or changed book (409 if the search engine is not ready)
POST/api/books/add{files:[…]} or {file}{ok, queued}
POST/api/books/category{file, category}Set a book's subject (a new name creates your own subject)
POST/api/chat{conv_id, mode, style, message, simpler, book_category, images[], turn, rewind, voice}Server-Sent Events, one data: line each: meta, status, notice, ocr, memory, sources, context, token, done, error
GET/api/chats-chats[{id,title,updated}], newest first
GET/api/chats/<id>-The whole conversation file
DELETE/api/chats/<id>-Deletes the chat and its documents
POST/api/docs{conv_id, name, data (base64 or data URL)}{ok, conv_id, doc, docs} or {ok:false, error}
DELETE/api/docs/<conv>/<doc>-{ok, docs}
GET/api/memory-memories, newest first (without vectors)
POST/api/memory{text}{ok, item}
PUT/api/memory/<id>{text}{ok}
DELETE/api/memory/<id>-{ok}
POST/api/settings{auto_learn?, nickname?, wall_dim?, wall_blur?}Saves to state.json, returns status
GET/api/wallpaper-The picture (404 if none)
POST/api/wallpaper{data: data URL}PNG/JPG/GIF/WebP up to 15 MB
DELETE/api/wallpaper-{ok}
POST/api/run{code, language: python|java}{ok, output, error, exit_code, seconds}
GET/api/voices-options[{engine,id,name,group}], choice, active, kokoro
POST/api/voices{engine: auto|kokoro|piper|windows, id}Saves the choice and pre-makes the preview
POST/api/voice/speak{text} or {preview: true}audio/wav (503 + {error} if no voice)
POST/api/voice/transcriberaw 16 kHz mono WAV body (≤ 30 MB){ok, text}
POST/api/open-folder{which: books|models}Opens the folder in File Explorer

Server-Sent Events from /api/chat

typeFieldsMeaning
metaconv_id, titleAlways first: which chat this is
statustextProgress note (reading a picture, searching the web)
noticetextA warning to show as a toast
ocrtextText read from the pictures
memorysaved[], note"remember that" result
sourcessources[], categories[], corrections{}Book passages or web pages used (Offline/Online)
