errormessageSomething went wrong; nothing more follows

19Data files and formats

FileFormat
data\state.json{last_model, auto_learn, nickname, wall_dim, wall_blur, voice:{engine, kokoro_sid, piper_voice}}
data\memory.json[{id, text, source: manual|auto, created, vec: [384 floats]}]
data\chats\<id>.json{id, title, created, updated, messages:[{role, content, mode, style, turn, display?, sources?, stats?}], docs?:[{id, name, chars, words, added}]}. Ids look like 20261113-101500-a1b2.
data\chat_docs\<chat id>\<doc id>.txtThe extracted text of an attached document
data\library\books.json{book_id: {file, title, pages, empty_pages, passages, size, mtime, added, seconds, category_auto}}
data\library\<book_id>.jsonlOne passage per line: {id, book_id, page, page_end, text}
data\library\<book_id>.npynumpy float32 array, shape (passages, 384); row i belongs to line i
data\library\categories.json{"": "", "_custom": [your own subjects]}
data\engine_pids.json{chat: pid, embed: pid, ui: pid}
data\wallpaper.<png|jpg|gif|webp>Your wallpaper
logs\chat-engine.log, embed-engine.logThe exact engine command and everything it printed

book_id = the file name in lower case with non-letters turned into dashes (40 characters at most), a dash, and the first 6 characters of the SHA-1 of the file name. The browser also keeps a few view settings in localStorage: mode, style2, cstyle, bookCat, theme, themeDefault, voiceMode.

Backups

Copy the data folder to keep memories, chats, attached documents and the book index. Deleting data\library only means books must be added again.

20The interface (ui\index.html)

One file of about 2,300 lines: CSS at the top, the HTML layout, then JavaScript. No libraries, no build step.

PartWhat it does
storeSafe wrapper around localStorage (never breaks if storage is blocked)
SThe page's state: mode, style, cstyle (story/character), current chat, busy flag, status
api(), toast()fetch helper with JSON, and the small pop-up messages
markdown(), inline()Turns answers into HTML after escaping them: headings, lists, tables, code blocks, [n] citations
send()Posts /api/chat and reads the SSE stream; updates the answer as tokens arrive; handles Stop
renderSources(), addRunButtons(), parseQuiz(), renderQuiz(), saveStudyGuide()Sources bar, Run buttons for Python/Java, the interactive quiz and the printable study guide
retry(), editUser(), showResend()Retry, Edit and Resend using the turn tags
VoiceRecording (WAV), speak(), live feed() while streaming, the Stop talking pill, Esc
SelPopHighlight any text: Read aloud / Explain in detail
Pics, DocsPictures and documents attached to the message or chat
loadBooks(), catOptions()Library page: grouped subject menus, Add buttons, time estimates, progress
loadMemory(), loadModels(), loadPersonal(), applyWall()Memory, Models and Personalise pages, wallpaper
BlobsThe animated mascot drawn on canvas elements with class qblob

The Blob mascot

A soft, wobbly ball drawn 60 times a second. Its look moves smoothly toward targets that depend on the mode and style (each value eases from 0 to 1). Styles only dress the Blob in Offline and Online modes.

Mode or styleLook
ChatDefault colours and a smile
OfflineTeal
OnlinePink
CreateGold, with a masquerade mask
Detailed (explain)Gold monocle on the right eye and a curly mustache
QuickA flickering lightning bolt beside the head
CodeA white hacker faceplate with slit eyes and circuit lines
TeachRound glasses and a tie
QuizA floating cube
Voice modeVibrates gently while listening or speaking

Keyboard

  • Enter sends, Shift+Enter makes a new line.
  • Esc stops reading aloud and closes the highlight menu.

21Settings you can change (config.json)

Close QuantaKai, edit config.json, start it again.

Extra keys the code understands if you add them: voice_language (Whisper language, default en), tts_kokoro_fast (false turns the kept-in-memory Kokoro off), tts_kokoro_length_scale, tts_kokoro_max_slowness (default 2.5), tts_kokoro_chinese.

22Security and safety

RiskHow QuantaKai handles it
Other devices on the Wi-FiThe server binds to 127.0.0.1 only
DNS rebindingRequests whose Host header is not 127.0.0.1:8000 or localhost:8000 get 403
Other websites sending requests (CSRF)POST, PUT and DELETE with a foreign Origin header get 403; this protects /api/run, which executes code
Model output containing HTML or scriptsThe page escapes all model text before turning Markdown into HTML
Running codeOnly when you press Run; fresh temporary folder, isolated Python (-I), no input, 20-second limit, output capped
Uploaded filesSize limits (documents 18 MB, wallpaper 15 MB, any request body 32 MB, recordings 30 MB), wallpaper type checked by its first bytes, names cleaned before use
Path tricks in idsChat and document ids are stripped to letters, digits, _ and - before becoming file names
Killing the wrong processA saved process number is only killed if its name is still llama-server (or python for the app)
Leftover engines eating RAMA Windows Job Object kills the engines when the app's window closes
Broken or oversized modelsFiles must start with GGUF and be at most 5 GB to load

An abliterated model will not refuse requests; the person using QuantaKai is responsible for what they ask it to do.

23Speed and tuning

Each answer shows its speed (for example "18 s - 11.8 tokens/s"). Measured on the Ryzen 5 3500U with Qwen2.5-3B Q4_K_M: about 11.8 tokens/s writing, about 22 tokens/s reading the prompt. A full Offline prompt (4 passages, about 900 tokens) therefore takes about 40 s before the first word; Chat starts in a few seconds thanks to cache_prompt.

ChangeEffect
threads: try 4, 6, 8More is not always faster; physical cores (4) is usually best
study_top_k 4 → 3About 25% less reading before an Offline answer starts
chunk_words 180 → 140Shorter passages, faster reading (re-add books)
A 2B model (Qwen3.5-2B)Roughly 1.5× faster, a little less careful
Vulkan build + gpu_layers 99May help prompt reading on the Vega 8; test it: same question before and after
Voice modeAnswers capped at 220 tokens, spoken from the first sentence
Piper instead of KokoroPiper speaks almost instantly; Kokoro sounds better but uses more processor

Trying the graphics chip (Vulkan)

  1. Download the -bin-win-vulkan-x64.zip from the same llama.cpp release.
  2. Rename engine to engine-cpu, make a new empty engine and unzip the Vulkan build into it.
  3. Set "gpu_layers": 99, restart, ask the same question; check logs\chat-engine.log mentions Vulkan.
  4. Keep whichever is faster. To go back: delete the new engine folder, rename engine-cpu to engine, gpu_layers 0.

24Adding and switching models

  • Any GGUF chat model of 5 GB or less works: put the .gguf in models (or new stuff) and it appears on the Models page and in the picker.
  • Switching is manual: press Load or pick it at the top. Only one chat model is in memory at a time; the last one used loads at the next start.
  • Good sizes for 16 GB RAM: 2B to 4B at Q4_K_M (1.3 to 2.5 GB). 7B/8B at Q4_K_M (4.4 to 4.9 GB) fits under the limit but is about half the speed.
  • Abliterated versions (search Hugging Face for the model name + abliterated GGUF) are loaded the same way.
  • Picture-capable models need their mmproj file in models, named so that it contains both mmproj and the model family, e.g. Qwen3.5-2B-mmproj-F16.gguf for Qwen3.5-2B-Q4_K_M.gguf. A mismatched mmproj crashes the engine.
  • The search model (bge-small) and mmproj files never appear in the chat picker.

25Troubleshooting

ProblemCause and fix
START.bat says "not set up yet"runtime\python\python.exe is missing: do Set up the portable Python, or run SETUP.bat
"Port 8000 is busy"QuantaKai is already running, or another program uses 8000: run STOP.bat, or change ui_port
ModuleNotFoundError: apppython314._pth lacks the ..\.. line, or START.bat is not in the QuantaKai folder
ModuleNotFoundError: numpy / pymupdfThe wheels were not unpacked into runtime\python\Lib\site-packages (see Set up the portable Python)
Model stuck on Loading / errorRead logs\chat-engine.log: missing DLL (re-extract the engine), not enough RAM (close programs or use a smaller model), bad file (re-download)
"Search model … is missing"bge-small-en-v1.5-q8_0.gguf is not in models or has another name
Book fails: no text foundIt is a scanned book (pictures of pages); OCR it first or attach pages as pictures
Offline answers say "couldn't find" too oftenLower study_min_score to 0.35, pick the subject by hand, or ask with the book's own words
Online mode: could not reach the internetCheck the connection; DuckDuckGo may also briefly block repeated searches
Voice typing: not set upRun VOICE_SETUP.bat; check voice contains whisper-cli.exe and ggml-base.en.bin
Kokoro missing from the voice menuvoice must contain sherpa-onnx-offline-tts.exe and a folder with voices.bin + tokens.txt + model(.int8).onnx
Kokoro very slowsherpa-onnx-c-api.dll is missing (not the shared build) or not v1.13.5; Piper is faster on 4 cores anyway
Run: Java isn't installedInstall a JDK 17+ and make sure java is on PATH
Page looks old after an updatePress Ctrl+F5 to reload without the cache
Engines still running after closingDouble-click STOP.bat
verify_files.py says DIFFERENTCopy that file again with Save file (copy-paste can change quotes or spaces)

26Privacy, backups and moving

  • Everything you type, every book, memory and chat stays in the QuantaKai folder. Only Online mode sends your question (to DuckDuckGo) and fetches web pages.
  • Memories can be read, edited and deleted on the Memory page; auto-learn can be switched off there.
  • Back up the data folder. To move QuantaKai, close it and move the whole folder.
  • Do not put books, data, models or voice files on GitHub (copyright, privacy and size). The repository's .gitignore keeps them out.
